import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

interface BackendUnavailablePageProps {
  title: string
  feature: string
  backTo?: '/events' | '/organizer/dashboard'
  children?: ReactNode
}

export function BackendUnavailablePage({
  title,
  feature,
  backTo = '/events',
  children,
}: BackendUnavailablePageProps) {
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-3xl px-4 py-20 sm:px-6">
      <Link to={backTo} className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">
        ← {backTo === '/events' ? 'Events' : 'Organizer dashboard'}
      </Link>
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">{title}</h1>
      <p role="status" className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 p-4 text-sm text-amber-900">
        {feature} is unavailable because the connected backend does not currently provide the required API.
        No local or demo data is being shown or saved.
      </p>
      {children && <div className="mt-4">{children}</div>}
    </main>
  )
}

interface QrCodeProps {
  value: string
  size?: number
  dataUrl?: string
}

export function QrCode({ value, size = 170, dataUrl }: QrCodeProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className="qr flex items-center justify-center"
      role={dataUrl ? 'img' : undefined}
      aria-label={dataUrl ? `QR code for ${value}` : undefined}
      style={{ width: size + 18 }}
    >
      {dataUrl ? (
        <img
          src={dataUrl}
          width={size}
          height={size}
          alt=""
          style={{ width: size, height: size, objectFit: 'contain' }}
        />
      ) : (
        <p className="px-2 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">The backend did not provide a QR code.</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

import { useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useMutation, useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { Search, UserPlus, UserRoundX } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'
import {
  createManagedUser,
  fetchManagedUsers,
  setManagedUserActive,
  updateManagedUserRole,
  type BackendManagedUser,
  type ManagedRole,
} from '../../../lib/backendUsers'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../../../components/ConfirmDialog'

const PAGE_SIZE = 8
const ROLES: ManagedRole[] = ['customer', 'organizer', 'staff', 'admin']
const filterClass = 'rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'
const emptyForm = { name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'customer' as ManagedRole }

function formatDate(value?: string): string {
  if (!value) return '—'
  const parsed = new Date(value)
  return Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())
    ? '—'
    : parsed.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
}

export function UserManagement() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [role, setRole] = useState<ManagedRole | 'all'>('all')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [statusChange, setStatusChange] = useState<{ user: BackendManagedUser; isActive: boolean } | null>(null)

  const usersQuery = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['admin-users'],
    queryFn: fetchManagedUsers,
  })

  const roleMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ userId, role }: { userId: string; role: ManagedRole }) =>
      updateManagedUserRole(userId, role),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin-users'] })
      showToast('User role updated', 'success')
    },
    onError: (error) => showToast(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not update user role', 'error'),
  })

  const createMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: createManagedUser,
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin-users'] })
      setForm(emptyForm)
      setCreating(false)
      showToast('User created', 'success')
    },
    onError: (error) => setFormError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not create user'),
  })

  const statusMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: ({ userId, isActive }: { userId: string; isActive: boolean }) =>
      setManagedUserActive(userId, isActive),
    onSuccess: async (_result, variables) => {
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin-users'] })
      setStatusChange(null)
      showToast(variables.isActive ? 'Account activated' : 'Account deactivated', 'success')
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      setStatusChange(null)
      showToast(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not change account status', 'error')
    },
  })

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const term = search.trim().toLowerCase()
    return (usersQuery.data ?? [])
      .filter((item) => role === 'all' || item.role === role)
      .filter((item) => !term || item.name.toLowerCase().includes(term) || item.email.toLowerCase().includes(term))
      .sort((a, b) => (b.createdAt ?? '').localeCompare(a.createdAt ?? ''))
  }, [usersQuery.data, role, search])

  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE))
  const current = Math.min(page, pages)
  const visible = filtered.slice((current - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, current * PAGE_SIZE)

  function submitCreate(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    setFormError(null)
    if (form.name.trim().length < 2) {
      setFormError('Name must contain at least 2 characters.')
      return
    }
    if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(form.email.trim())) {
      setFormError('Enter a valid email address.')
      return
    }
    if (form.password.length < 10 || form.password.length > 72) {
      setFormError('Password must be between 10 and 72 characters.')
      return
    }
    createMutation.mutate(form)
  }

  return (
    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">
          Backend users{usersQuery.data ? ` (${usersQuery.data.length})` : ''}
        </h2>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={() => { setFormError(null); setCreating((value) => !value) }}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-navy px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark"
        >
          <UserPlus size={16} aria-hidden="true" /> {creating ? 'Close form' : 'Create user'}
        </button>
      </div>

      {creating && (
        <form onSubmit={submitCreate} className="mb-5 rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-slate-50 p-4">
          <h3 className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">Create backend user</h3>
          <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Name
              <input className={`${filterClass} mt-1 w-full`} required minLength={2} maxLength={150} value={form.name} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, name: event.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Email
              <input className={`${filterClass} mt-1 w-full`} type="email" required maxLength={255} value={form.email} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, email: event.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Temporary password
              <input className={`${filterClass} mt-1 w-full`} type="password" required minLength={10} maxLength={72} value={form.password} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, password: event.target.value })} />
            </label>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
              Role
              <select className={`${filterClass} mt-1 w-full`} value={form.role} onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, role: event.target.value as ManagedRole })}>
                {ROLES.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>
          {formError && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm text-red-600">{formError}</p>}
          <button type="submit" disabled={createMutation.isPending} className="mt-3 rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white disabled:opacity-50">
            {createMutation.isPending ? 'Creating…' : 'Create user'}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <label className="relative min-w-56 flex-1">
          <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
          <input
            aria-label="Search users"
            value={search}
            onChange={(event) => {
              setSearch(event.target.value)
              setPage(1)
            }}
            placeholder="Search name or email"
            className={`${filterClass} w-full pl-9`}
          />
        </label>
        <select
          aria-label="Filter by role"
          value={role}
          onChange={(event) => {
            setRole(event.target.value as ManagedRole | 'all')
            setPage(1)
          }}
          className={filterClass}
        >
          <option value="all">All roles</option>
          {ROLES.map((item) => <option key={item} value={item}>{item}</option>)}
        </select>
      </div>

      {usersQuery.isLoading && <p role="status" className="py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading users from the backend…</p>}
      {usersQuery.isError && (
        <div role="alert" className="py-8 text-center text-red-600">
          <p>{usersQuery.error instanceof Error ? usersQuery.error.message : 'Could not load users.'}</p>
          <button type="button" className="mt-2 underline" onClick={() => void usersQuery.refetch()}>Try again</button>
        </div>
      )}

      {!usersQuery.isLoading && !usersQuery.isError && (
        <>
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-slate-600">
              <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-xs uppercase text-slate-500">
                <tr>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3">Name</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3">Role</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3">Registered</th>
                  <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Actions</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {visible.length === 0 && (
                  <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">No backend users match your search.</td></tr>
                )}
                {visible.map((item) => (
                  <tr key={item.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0">
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <div className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{item.name}</div>
                      <div className="text-xs">{item.email}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3">
                      <select
                        aria-label={`Role for ${item.name}`}
                        value={item.role}
                        disabled={!user || user.id === item.id || roleMutation.isPending}
                        onChange={(event) => roleMutation.mutate({
                          userId: item.id,
                          role: event.target.value as ManagedRole,
                        })}
                        className={filterClass}
                      >
                        {ROLES.map((option) => <option key={option} value={option}>{option}</option>)}
                      </select>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs capitalize">
                      <span>{item.status ?? 'unknown'}</span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">{formatDate(item.createdAt)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                      {item.status && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          disabled={!user || user.id === item.id || statusMutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => setStatusChange({ user: item, isActive: item.status !== 'active' })}
                          className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold disabled:opacity-40 ${
                            item.status === 'active'
                              ? 'border-red-200 text-red-700 hover:bg-red-50'
                              : 'border-emerald-200 text-emerald-700 hover:bg-emerald-50'
                          }`}
                        >
                          <UserRoundX size={14} aria-hidden="true" />
                          {item.status === 'active' ? 'Deactivate' : 'Activate'}
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>

          <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-600">
            <span>Page {current} of {pages}</span>
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button type="button" disabled={current <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(current - 1)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">Previous</button>
              <button type="button" disabled={current >= pages} onClick={() => setPage(current + 1)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">Next</button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!statusChange}
        onClose={() => setStatusChange(null)}
        onConfirm={() => {
          if (statusChange) statusMutation.mutate({
            userId: statusChange.user.id,
            isActive: statusChange.isActive,
          })
        }}
        title={`${statusChange?.isActive ? 'Activate' : 'Deactivate'} this account?`}
        description={statusChange ? `This changes the backend account status for ${statusChange.user.name} (${statusChange.user.email}).` : ''}
        confirmLabel={statusMutation.isPending ? 'Saving…' : statusChange?.isActive ? 'Activate account' : 'Deactivate account'}
        variant={statusChange?.isActive ? 'default' : 'danger'}
      />
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/authentication/authStore.ts
================================================================================
import { api, setToken } from '../../lib/backend'

export type Role = 'customer' | 'organizer' | 'staff' | 'admin'

export interface User {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: Role
}

interface BackendRoleResponse {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: 'ATTENDEE' | 'ORGANIZER' | 'CHECK_IN_STAFF' | 'ADMIN'
}

const SESSION_KEY = 'tm_user'

const ROLE_FROM_BACKEND: Record<BackendRoleResponse['role'], Role> = {
  ATTENDEE: 'customer',
  ORGANIZER: 'organizer',
  CHECK_IN_STAFF: 'staff',
  ADMIN: 'admin',
}

function mapUser(user: BackendRoleResponse): User {
  const role = ROLE_FROM_BACKEND[user.role]
  if (!role) throw new Error(`The backend returned an unsupported account role: ${user.role}`)
  return { id: String(user.id), name: user.name, email: user.email, role }
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
}

function isBackendRole(value: string): value is BackendRoleResponse['role'] {
  return Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ROLE_FROM_BACKEND, value)
}

function userFromAccessToken(accessToken: string): BackendRoleResponse | null {
  const payload = accessToken.split('.')[1]
  if (!payload) return null

  try {
    const base64 = payload.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/')
    const decoded: unknown = JSON.parse(atob(base64.padEnd(Math.ceil(base64.length / 4) * 4, '=')))
    if (!isRecord(decoded)) return null

    const id = decoded.sub
    const email = decoded.email
    const role = decoded.role
    if (
      (typeof id !== 'string' && typeof id !== 'number') ||
      typeof email !== 'string' ||
      typeof role !== 'string' ||
      !isBackendRole(role)
    ) {
      return null
    }

    return {
      id: String(id),
      name: typeof decoded.name === 'string' && decoded.name.trim() ? decoded.name : email,
      email,
      role,
    }
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

function parseAuthResponse(value: unknown): { accessToken: string; user: User } {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid authentication response.')
  }

  const accessToken =
    typeof value.accessToken === 'string'
      ? value.accessToken
      : typeof value.access_token === 'string'
        ? value.access_token
        : typeof value.token === 'string'
          ? value.token
          : null

  if (!accessToken?.trim()) {
    throw new Error('The backend did not return an access token. Please log in to continue.')
  }

  const tokenUser = userFromAccessToken(accessToken)
  const rawUser = isRecord(value.user) ? value.user : value
  const id = rawUser.id ?? tokenUser?.id
  const name = rawUser.name ?? tokenUser?.name ?? rawUser.email ?? tokenUser?.email
  const email = rawUser.email ?? tokenUser?.email
  const role = rawUser.role ?? tokenUser?.role

  if (
    (typeof id !== 'string' && typeof id !== 'number') ||
    typeof name !== 'string' ||
    typeof email !== 'string' ||
    typeof role !== 'string'
  ) {
    throw new Error('The backend did not return the account details required to start a session.')
  }
  if (!isBackendRole(role)) {
    throw new Error(`The backend returned an unsupported account role: ${role}`)
  }

  return {
    accessToken,
    user: mapUser({
      id: String(id),
      name,
      email,
      role,
    }),
  }
}

function readSession(): User | null {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY)
    if (!raw) return null
    const value = JSON.parse(raw) as User
    if (
      typeof value.id !== 'string' ||
      typeof value.name !== 'string' ||
      typeof value.email !== 'string' ||
      !['customer', 'organizer', 'staff', 'admin'].includes(value.role)
    ) {
      localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY)
      return null
    }
    return value
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

function saveSession(user: User): User {
  localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(user))
  return user
}

async function postAuth(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<unknown> {
  try {
    return await api<unknown>(path, { method: 'POST', body })
  } catch (error) {
    const status = (error as { status?: number }).status
    if (status === 401) throw new Error('Wrong email or password')
    if (status === 409) throw new Error('This account already exists')
    throw error
  }
}

function startSession(result: unknown): User {
  const session = parseAuthResponse(result)
  const user = saveSession(session.user)
  setToken(session.accessToken)
  return user
}

async function authenticate(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<User> {
  return startSession(await postAuth(path, body))
}

export function getUser(): User | null {
  return readSession()
}

export async function loginRequest(email: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
  return authenticate('/auth/login', { email: email.trim(), password })
}

export async function registerRequest(
  name: string,
  email: string,
  password: string,
  role: 'customer' | 'organizer' = 'customer',
): Promise<User> {
  return authenticate('/auth/register', {
    name: name.trim(),
    email: email.trim(),
    password,
    role: role === 'organizer' ? 'ORGANIZER' : 'ATTENDEE',
  })
}

export function logoutRequest(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY)
  setToken(null)
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/checkout/completePayment.ts
================================================================================
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { StoredEvent } from '../../lib/events'
import type { TicketType } from '../../types/event'
import { backendTicketToRecord, purchaseBackendTicket } from '../../lib/backendTickets'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../lib/backendEvents'
import { fetchTicketRecords } from '../tickets/ticketData'
import {
  addPendingTickets,
  getPendingPayment,
  markPaymentCompleted,
  verifyPayment,
  type PendingPayment,
} from '../../lib/paychangu'

export type CompletionResult =
  | { kind: 'success'; tickets: TicketRecord[]; payment: PendingPayment }
  | {
      kind: 'partial'
      tickets: TicketRecord[]
      message: string
      eventId: string
      payment: PendingPayment
    }
  | { kind: 'pending' }
  | { kind: 'failed'; message: string; eventId?: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown' }

const inFlight = new Map<string, Promise<CompletionResult>>()

async function ticketsFor(payment: PendingPayment): Promise<TicketRecord[]> {
  const ids = new Set(payment.ticketIds ?? [])
  if (ids.size === 0) return []

  const tickets = await fetchTicketRecords(payment.userId, payment.holder)
  return tickets.filter((ticket) => ids.has(ticket.id))
}

async function claimBackendTickets(
  pending: PendingPayment,
  event: StoredEvent,
  ticketType: TicketType,
): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const tickets = await ticketsFor(pending)
  let failure: string | null = null

  for (let index = tickets.length; index < pending.quantity; index += 1) {
    try {
      const purchased = await purchaseBackendTicket(event.id, ticketType.id, pending.txRef)
      const ticket = backendTicketToRecord(purchased, {
        userId: pending.userId,
        event,
        ticketType,
        holder: pending.holder,
      })
      tickets.push(ticket)
      addPendingTickets(pending.txRef, [ticket.id])
    } catch (caught) {
      failure = caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'The ticket service could not issue the ticket.'
      break
    }
  }

  const updated = getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? pending

  if (tickets.length >= pending.quantity) {
    markPaymentCompleted(pending.txRef, tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id))
    return {
      kind: 'success',
      tickets,
      payment: getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? {
        ...updated,
        status: 'completed',
        ticketIds: tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id),
      },
    }
  }

  const issued = tickets.length
  const message =
    issued > 0
      ? `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through. ${issued} of ${pending.quantity} tickets were issued${failure ? `: ${failure}` : '.'} Contact support with this reference for the remaining ticket(s).`
      : `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but no tickets could be issued${failure ? `: ${failure}` : '.'} Contact support for a refund.`

  if (issued > 0) {
    return { kind: 'partial', tickets, message, eventId: pending.eventId, payment: updated }
  }
  return { kind: 'failed', eventId: pending.eventId, message }
}

async function run(txRef: string): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const pending = getPendingPayment(txRef)
  if (!pending) return { kind: 'unknown' }

  if (pending.status === 'completed') {
    const tickets = await ticketsFor(pending)
    if (tickets.length < pending.quantity) {
      throw new Error('The backend has not returned all tickets recorded for this payment.')
    }
    return { kind: 'success', tickets, payment: pending }
  }

  const verified = await verifyPayment(txRef)

  if (verified.status === 'pending') return { kind: 'pending' }
  if (verified.status !== 'success') {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: 'The payment was not completed. No ticket was issued and you were not charged.',
    }
  }

  if (verified.tx_ref !== pending.txRef) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: 'The backend confirmed a different payment reference. No ticket was issued. Please contact support.',
    }
  }

  let event: StoredEvent
  try {
    event = await fetchBackendEvent(pending.eventId)
  } catch (caught) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) was confirmed, but the event details could not be loaded. Please contact support. ${caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : ''}`.trim(),
    }
  }

  const ticketType = event.ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === pending.ticketTypeId)
  if (!ticketType) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but this ticket is no longer available. Please contact support for a refund.`,
    }
  }

  return claimBackendTickets(pending, event, ticketType)
}

export function completePayment(txRef: string): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const existing = inFlight.get(txRef)
  if (existing) return existing
  const promise = run(txRef).finally(() => inFlight.delete(txRef))
  inFlight.set(txRef, promise)
  return promise
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

interface QuantityStepperProps {
  value: number
  min: number
  max: number
  onChange: (value: number) => void
}

export function QuantityStepper({ value, min, max, onChange }: QuantityStepperProps): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="stepper" role="group" aria-label="Ticket quantity">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="stepper-btn"
        onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(min, value - 1))}
        disabled={value <= min}
        aria-label="Decrease quantity"
      >
        −
      </button>
      <span className="stepper-value" aria-live="polite">
        {value}
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="stepper-btn"
        onClick={() => onChange(Math.min(max, value + 1))}
        disabled={value >= max}
        aria-label="Increase quantity"
      >
        +
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { formatKwacha } from '../format'
import type { CheckoutEvent, TicketType } from '../types'
import { QuantityStepper } from './QuantityStepper'

interface TicketSummaryCardProps {
  event: CheckoutEvent
  ticketType: TicketType
  quantity: number
  onChangeQuantity: (quantity: number) => void
}

export function TicketSummaryCard({
  event,
  ticketType,
  quantity,
  onChangeQuantity,
}: TicketSummaryCardProps): ReactNode {
  const remaining = ticketType.capacity - ticketType.sold
  const soldOut = remaining <= 0

  return (
    <div className="card row">
      <div>
        <b>{event.name}</b>
        <div className="m">
          {event.date} · {ticketType.name} · {event.venue}, {event.city}
        </div>
        <div className="m">
          {formatKwacha(ticketType.price)} each · {soldOut ? 'Sold out' : `${remaining} left`}
        </div>
      </div>
      <QuantityStepper
        value={quantity}
        min={1}
        max={Math.max(remaining, 1)}
        onChange={onChangeQuantity}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/checkout/format.ts
================================================================================
import { SERVICE_FEE } from './config'

export interface OrderTotals {
  subtotal: number
  serviceFee: number
  total: number
}

export function formatKwacha(amount: number): string {
  return `MK${Math.round(amount).toLocaleString('en-US')}`
}

export function calculateTotals(
  price: number,
  quantity: number,
  serviceFee: number = SERVICE_FEE,
): OrderTotals {
  const subtotal = price * quantity
  return { subtotal, serviceFee, total: subtotal + serviceFee }
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/checkout/hooks/useCheckout.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../../types/event'
import {
  createOrder,
  initiatePayment,
  savePendingPayment,
} from '../../../lib/paychangu'
import type { CheckoutInput } from '../types'

export class CheckoutError extends Error {}

// The backend owns the order, reserves stock, starts PayChangu and verifies payment.
async function startPayment(input: CheckoutInput): Promise<never> {
  const stored = await fetchBackendEvent(input.event.id)
  if (!stored || stored.status !== 'Published') {
    throw new CheckoutError('This event is not available for booking.')
  }
  const ticketType = stored.ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === input.ticketType.id)
  if (!ticketType) throw new CheckoutError('That ticket type no longer exists.')

  if (input.quantity < 1) throw new CheckoutError('Select at least one ticket.')
  const remaining = remainingSeats(ticketType)
  if (remaining <= 0) throw new CheckoutError(`${ticketType.name} tickets are sold out for this event.`)
  if (input.quantity > remaining) {
    throw new CheckoutError(`Only ${remaining} ${ticketType.name} ticket${remaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left.`)
  }

  try {
    const order = await createOrder({
      eventId: stored.id,
      ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
      quantity: input.quantity,
    })
    const { checkout_url: checkoutUrl, tx_ref: txRef } = await initiatePayment(order.orderNumber)
    savePendingPayment({
      txRef,
      orderNumber: order.orderNumber,
      eventId: stored.id,
      ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
      quantity: input.quantity,
      holder: input.holder,
      userId: input.userId,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'pending',
    })
    window.location.assign(checkoutUrl)
  } catch (error) {
    throw new CheckoutError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not start the payment.')
  }

  // keep the button on "Redirecting…" until the browser leaves the page
  return new Promise<never>(() => {})
}

export function useCheckout() {
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: startPayment })
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/checkout/pages/CheckoutPage.tsx

import { useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useParams, useSearch, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { calculateTotals, formatKwacha } from '../format'
import { useCheckout } from '../hooks/useCheckout'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import type { CheckoutEvent, TicketType } from '../types'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../../types/event'

const toCheckoutEvent = (ev: import('../../../types/event').Event): CheckoutEvent => ({
  id: ev.id,
  name: ev.title,
  date: ev.date,
  time: ev.time,
  venue: ev.venue,
  city: ev.city,
  organizer: ev.organizer,
})

const toCheckoutTicketTypes = (ev: import('../../../types/event').Event): TicketType[] =>
  ev.ticketTypes.map((t) => ({ ...t }))


export function CheckoutPage(): ReactNode {
  const { eventId } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { eventId?: string }
  const { type, qty } = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { type?: string; qty?: string }
  const parsedQty = parseInt(qty ?? '', 10)

  const [selectedTypeId, setSelectedTypeId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(() =>
    Number.isNaN(parsedQty) || parsedQty < 1 ? 1 : parsedQty,
  )
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const holder = user?.name ?? ''
  const [clientError, setClientError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const { data: eventData, isLoading, isError } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['checkout-event', eventId],
    queryFn: () => fetchBackendEvent(eventId ?? ''),
    enabled: !!eventId,
  })

  const ticketTypes: TicketType[] = eventData ? toCheckoutTicketTypes(eventData) : []
  const activeTypeId = selectedTypeId ?? type ?? ticketTypes[0]?.id
  const ticketType =
    ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === activeTypeId) ?? ticketTypes[0]
  const checkoutEvent: CheckoutEvent = eventData
    ? toCheckoutEvent(eventData)
    : {
        id: eventId ?? 'unknown',
        name: 'Event',
        date: '',
        time: '',
        venue: '',
        city: '',
        organizer: '',
      }

  const checkout = useCheckout()

  const totals = ticketType
    ? calculateTotals(ticketType.price, quantity)
    : { subtotal: 0, serviceFee: 0, total: 0 }
  const remaining = ticketType ? remainingSeats(ticketType) : 0
  const soldOut = remaining <= 0
  const exceedsAvailable = quantity > remaining
  function selectTicketType(id: string) {
    setSelectedTypeId(id)
    setQuantity(1)
    setClientError(null)
  }

  function handlePay() {
    if (!ticketType || !user) return
    setClientError(null)
    // goes to PayChangu; tickets are issued on the return page once the payment is confirmed
    checkout.mutate({
      userId: user.id,
      event: checkoutEvent,
      ticketType,
      quantity,
      holder,
    })
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 sm:px-6">
        <div className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-3xl bg-slate-100" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isError || !eventData || eventData.status !== 'Published') {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 text-center sm:px-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Event not found</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">We couldn’t find this event to check out.</p>
        <Link to="/events" className="mt-6 inline-block rounded-full bg-navy px-6 py-2 text-white">Browse events</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const mutationError = checkout.error instanceof Error ? checkout.error.message : null
  const errorMessage = clientError ?? mutationError
  const blocked = soldOut || exceedsAvailable

  const MAX_PER_ORDER = 10
  const maxQty = Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_PER_ORDER, remaining))
  const canPay = !blocked && !checkout.isPending && !!ticketType

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <Link to="/events" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-navy">
          ← Back to events
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold text-navy sm:text-4xl">Confirm your purchase</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-slate-500">Check your order, then pay securely.</p>

        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_380px]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <div className="bg-navy px-6 py-5 text-white">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300">Your event</p>
                <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-white">{checkoutEvent.name}</h2>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-200">
                  {checkoutEvent.date}
                  {checkoutEvent.time ? ` · ${checkoutEvent.time}` : ''}
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-200">
                  {checkoutEvent.venue}
                  {checkoutEvent.city ? `, ${checkoutEvent.city}` : ''}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-6 p-6">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ticket type</h3>
                  <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    {ticketTypes.map((option) => {
                      const left = option.capacity - option.sold
                      const disabled = left <= 0
                      const selected = option.id === ticketType?.id
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={option.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => selectTicketType(option.id)}
                          disabled={disabled}
                          aria-pressed={selected}
                          className={`rounded-xl border-2 p-4 text-left transition ${
                            selected
                              ? 'border-navy bg-navy/5'
                              : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300'
                          } ${disabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-50' : ''}`}
                        >
                          <span className="block font-semibold text-slate-900">{option.name}</span>
                          <span className="mt-1 block text-lg font-bold text-navy">{formatKwacha(option.price)}</span>
                          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                            {disabled ? 'Sold out' : `${left} left`}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Quantity</h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Up to {MAX_PER_ORDER} per order</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
                      disabled={quantity <= 1}
                      className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-slate-300 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      −
                    </button>
                    <span className="w-8 text-center text-lg font-bold" aria-live="polite">{quantity}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Increase quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.min(maxQty, q + 1))}
                      disabled={quantity >= maxQty}
                      className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-slate-300 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      +
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ticket holder</h3>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
                    <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-navy text-sm font-bold text-white">
                      {(holder || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate font-medium text-slate-900">{holder}</p>
                      <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{user?.email}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {soldOut && ticketType && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                <b>Sold out.</b> There are no {ticketType.name} tickets left.
              </div>
            )}
            {!soldOut && exceedsAvailable && ticketType && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                Only {remaining} ticket{remaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left. Reduce the quantity to continue.
              </div>
            )}
            {errorMessage && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:self-start">
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-navy">Order summary</h2>
              <dl className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-600">
                  <dt>
                    {quantity} × {ticketType?.name ?? 'Ticket'}
                  </dt>
                  <dd className="font-medium text-slate-900">{formatKwacha(totals.subtotal)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-600">
                  <dt>Service fee</dt>
                  <dd className="font-medium text-slate-900">{formatKwacha(totals.serviceFee)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                  <dt className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Total</dt>
                  <dd className="text-2xl font-bold text-navy">{formatKwacha(totals.total)}</dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handlePay}
                disabled={!canPay}
                className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-3.5 text-base font-semibold text-white shadow transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {checkout.isPending ? 'Redirecting…' : `Pay ${formatKwacha(totals.total)}`}
              </button>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/landing/components/HeroSlider.tsx

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, Pause, Play } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEvents } from '../../events'
import SearchBar from './SearchBar'
import type { Filters } from '../filters'
import { useAutoplay } from '../hooks/useAutoplay'

interface HeroSliderProps {
  filters: Filters
  setFilters: (f: Filters, options?: { replace?: boolean }) => void
  onShowAll?: (filters: Filters) => void
}

export default function HeroSlider({ filters, setFilters, onShowAll }: HeroSliderProps) {
  const [current, setCurrent] = useState(0)
  const { data: events = [] } = useEvents()
  const total = Math.max(events.length, 1)
  const event = events[current % total]

  useEffect(() => {
    if (current >= total) setCurrent(0)
  }, [current, total])

  const goTo = useCallback(
    (index: number) => setCurrent((index + total) % total),
    [total],
  )

  // Auto-advance stops on hover, keyboard focus, reduced motion, or when the
  // visitor presses pause (WCAG 2.2.2).
  const next = useCallback(() => goTo(current + 1), [goTo, current])
  const { paused, reducedMotion, togglePause, handlers } = useAutoplay(6000, next)

  const arrowStyle =
    'absolute top-1/2 z-10 hidden h-12 w-12 -translate-y-1/2 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/20 text-white backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-white/40 sm:flex'

  return (
    <section className="relative min-h-screen bg-navy-dark" {...handlers}>
      <div className="absolute inset-0 overflow-hidden">
        {events.map((item, i) => (
          <img
            key={item.id}
            src={item.image}
            alt=""
            aria-hidden={i !== current}
            className={`absolute inset-0 h-full w-full object-cover transition-all duration-1500 motion-reduce:transition-none ${
              i === current ? 'scale-105 opacity-100' : 'scale-100 opacity-0'
            }`}
          />
        ))}
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-linear-to-b from-navy-dark/70 via-navy-dark/50 to-navy-dark/80" />
      </div>

      <div className="relative z-20 mx-auto flex min-h-screen max-w-5xl flex-col items-center justify-center px-4 pb-20 pt-28 text-center">
        <div key={current} className="fade-up">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-extrabold text-white sm:text-5xl lg:text-7xl">
            {event?.title ?? 'Discover events near you'}
          </h1>
          <p className="mx-auto mt-4 max-w-2xl text-base text-white/85 sm:text-xl">
            {event?.description || 'Browse upcoming events and find your next experience.'}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-10 w-full">
          <SearchBar filters={filters} setFilters={setFilters} onShowAll={onShowAll} />
        </div>
      </div>

      {events.length > 1 && (
        <>
          <button aria-label="Previous slide" onClick={() => goTo(current - 1)} className={`${arrowStyle} left-4`}>
            <ChevronLeft />
          </button>
          <button aria-label="Next slide" onClick={() => goTo(current + 1)} className={`${arrowStyle} right-4`}>
            <ChevronRight />
          </button>
        </>
      )}

      {events.length > 1 && (
        <div className="absolute bottom-6 left-0 right-0 z-10 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
          {!reducedMotion && (
            <button
              aria-label={paused ? 'Resume hero slideshow' : 'Pause hero slideshow'}
              onClick={togglePause}
              className="mr-2 flex h-8 w-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-white/20 text-white backdrop-blur-sm hover:bg-white/40"
            >
              {paused ? <Play size={14} /> : <Pause size={14} />}
            </button>
          )}
          {events.map((item, i) => (
            <button
              key={item.id}
              aria-label={`Event ${i + 1}`}
              onClick={() => setCurrent(i)}
              className={`h-2 rounded-full transition-all ${i === current ? 'w-8 bg-white' : 'w-2 bg-white/50'}`}
            />
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/landing/pages/HomePage.tsx

import { useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { filtersFromSearch, searchFromFilters, type Filters } from '../filters'
import HeroSlider from '../components/HeroSlider'
import About from '../components/About'
import EventsSection from '../components/EventsSection'
import Organizers from '../components/Organizers'
import Contact from '../components/Contact'

export default function HomePage() {
  const search = useSearch({ from: '/' })
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const filters = filtersFromSearch(search)

  // Typing replaces the history entry so the typeahead does not spam Back.
  function setFilters(next: Filters, options?: { replace?: boolean }) {
    navigate({ to: '/', search: searchFromFilters(next), replace: options?.replace })
  }

  // Committing the search carries the same filters to the full catalogue.
  function showAll(next: Filters) {
    navigate({ to: '/events', search: searchFromFilters(next) })
  }

  return (
    <>
      <HeroSlider filters={filters} setFilters={setFilters} onShowAll={showAll} />
      <About />
      <EventsSection filters={filters} />
      <Organizers />
      <Contact />
    </>
  )
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/organizer/components/EventForm.tsx
================================================================================
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { totalCapacity, type NewEventInput } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer';

interface EventFormProps {
  onSubmit: (event: NewEventInput) => Promise<void> | void;
  onCancel: () => void;
}

interface TicketRow {
  name: string;
  price: string;
  quantity: string;
}

const emptyRow = (name = ''): TicketRow => ({ name, price: '', quantity: '' });

export const EventForm: React.FC<EventFormProps> = ({ onSubmit, onCancel }) => {
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [description, setDescription] = useState('');
  const [venue, setVenue] = useState('');
  const [location, setLocation] = useState('');
  const [startDate, setStartDate] = useState('');
  const [startTime, setStartTime] = useState('');
  const [endDate, setEndDate] = useState('');
  const [endTime, setEndTime] = useState('');
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<TicketRow[]>([emptyRow('General')]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<'Published' | 'Draft'>('Draft');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  const updateRow = (index: number, patch: Partial<TicketRow>) =>
    setRows(rows.map((row, i) => (i === index ? { ...row, ...patch } : row)));

  const ticketTypes = rows.map((row) => ({
    name: row.name.trim(),
    price: Number(row.price),
    quantity: Number(row.quantity),
  }));
  const capacity = totalCapacity(ticketTypes.filter((type) => Number.isFinite(type.quantity)));
  const startAt = startDate && startTime ? `${startDate}T${startTime}` : '';
  const endAt = endDate && endTime ? `${endDate}T${endTime}` : '';

  const handleSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!name.trim() || !description.trim() || !venue.trim() || !location.trim() || !startAt || !endAt) {
      setError('Fill in the title, description, venue, city, start date and time, and end date and time.');
      return;
    }
    const startDateTime = new Date(startAt);
    const endDateTime = new Date(endAt);
    if (Number.isNaN(startDateTime.getTime()) || Number.isNaN(endDateTime.getTime())) {
      setError('Enter a valid start and end date and time.');
      return;
    }
    if (endDateTime <= startDateTime) {
      setError('The event must end after it starts.');
      return;
    }
    const badType = ticketTypes.find(
      (type) => !type.name || !(type.price >= 0) || !Number.isInteger(type.quantity) || type.quantity < 1,
    );
    if (badType || rows.some((row) => row.price === '' || row.quantity === '')) {
      setError('Every ticket type needs a name, a price (0 or more) and a whole number of tickets.');
      return;
    }
    setError('');
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await onSubmit({
        name,
        description,
        venue,
        location,
        startAt: startDateTime.toISOString(),
        endAt: endDateTime.toISOString(),
        ticketTypes,
        status,
      });
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not save the event. Try again.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <form className="event-creation-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
      {error && <div className="form-error-banner">{error}</div>}

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Event Title</label>
        <input type="text" value={name} onChange={(e) => setName(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Afrobeat Showcase 2026" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Description</label>
        <textarea rows={3} value={description} onChange={(e) => setDescription(e.target.value)} placeholder="What is this event about?" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Venue</label>
        <input type="text" value={venue} onChange={(e) => setVenue(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Bingu International Conference Centre" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>City and country</label>
        <input type="text" value={location} onChange={(e) => setLocation(e.target.value)} placeholder="e.g. Lilongwe, Malawi" />
      </div>

      <div className="form-row">
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Start date</label>
          <input type="date" value={startDate} onChange={(e) => setStartDate(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>Start time</label>
          <input type="time" value={startTime} onChange={(e) => setStartTime(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>End date</label>
          <input type="date" value={endDate} onChange={(e) => setEndDate(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
        <div className="form-group">
          <label>End time</label>
          <input type="time" value={endTime} onChange={(e) => setEndTime(e.target.value)} />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Ticket types (prices in MWK)</label>
        {rows.map((row, index) => (
          <div className="ticket-row" key={index}>
            <input type="text" aria-label="Ticket type name" value={row.name} onChange={(e) => updateRow(index, { name: e.target.value })} placeholder="Name, e.g. VIP" />
            <input type="number" min="0" aria-label="Price" value={row.price} onChange={(e) => updateRow(index, { price: e.target.value })} placeholder="Price" />
            <input type="number" min="1" aria-label="Number of tickets" value={row.quantity} onChange={(e) => updateRow(index, { quantity: e.target.value })} placeholder="How many" />
            {rows.length > 1 && (
              <button type="button" className="btn-action" onClick={() => setRows(rows.filter((_, i) => i !== index))}>Remove</button>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
        <div className="ticket-row-footer">
          <button type="button" className="btn-action" onClick={() => setRows([...rows, emptyRow()])}>+ Add ticket type</button>
          <span className="form-hint">Total capacity: {capacity.toLocaleString()} tickets</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="form-group">
        <label>Visibility Status</label>
        <select value={status} onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value as 'Published' | 'Draft')}>
          <option value="Draft">Save as Draft</option>
          <option value="Published">Publish Directly</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="form-actions">
        <button type="button" className="btn-secondary" onClick={onCancel} disabled={saving}>Cancel</button>
        <button type="submit" className="btn-primary" disabled={saving}>{saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Create Event'}</button>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
};

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/organizer/hooks/useAttendee.ts
================================================================================
import { useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication';
import { api } from '../../../lib/backend';
import { listMyEvents } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer';
import type { AttendeeFilters, AttendeeItem } from '../Data/Attendee';

interface BackendEventTicket {
  ticketNumber: string;
  ticketTypeId: string;
  customerId: string | null;
  customerName?: string | null;
  customerEmail?: string | null;
  attendee?: { userId?: string; fullName?: string; email?: string } | null;
  status: 'ISSUED' | 'USED' | 'CANCELLED' | 'REFUNDED';
  issuedAt: string;
  purchasedAt?: string | null;
  purchaseDate?: string | null;
  checkedInAt: string | null;
}

interface BackendTicketType {
  ticketTypeId: string;
  name: string;
  price: number | string;
}

const EMPTY_ATTENDEES: AttendeeItem[] = [];

function attendeeErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
  if (!(error instanceof Error)) return '';
  if ('status' in error && error.status === 401) {
    return 'Your session has expired or is no longer authorized. Log out and sign in again. If this continues, confirm your organizer account has access.';
  }
  return error.message;
}

function formatPurchaseDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'Unknown';
  return date.toLocaleString('en-GB', { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' });
}

export async function fetchBackendAttendees(): Promise<AttendeeItem[]> {
  const events = await listMyEvents();
  const attendeesByEvent = await Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => {
      const [tickets, ticketTypes] = await Promise.all([
        api<BackendEventTicket[]>(`/tickets/events/${encodeURIComponent(event.id)}`),
        api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(event.id)}/ticket-types`),
      ]);
      const ticketTypeById = new Map(ticketTypes.map((ticketType) => [ticketType.ticketTypeId, ticketType]));

      return tickets.flatMap((ticket) => {
        if (!ticket.customerId || ticket.status === 'CANCELLED' || ticket.status === 'REFUNDED') return [];
        const ticketType = ticketTypeById.get(ticket.ticketTypeId);
        const purchasedAt = ticket.purchasedAt ?? ticket.purchaseDate ?? ticket.issuedAt;

        return [{
          id: ticket.ticketNumber,
          name: ticket.customerName ?? ticket.attendee?.fullName ?? `Customer #${ticket.customerId}`,
          email: ticket.customerEmail ?? ticket.attendee?.email ?? '',
          event: event.name,
          eventId: event.id,
          ticketType: ticketType?.name ?? 'Unknown',
          quantity: 1,
          amount: Number(ticketType?.price ?? 0),
          purchaseDate: formatPurchaseDate(purchasedAt),
          purchasedAt,
          status: ticket.status === 'USED' || ticket.checkedInAt ? 'Checked In' : 'Registered',
        } satisfies AttendeeItem];
      });
    }),
  );

  return attendeesByEvent.flat().sort(
    (a, b) => new Date(b.purchasedAt).getTime() - new Date(a.purchasedAt).getTime(),
  );
}

export function filterAttendees(
  attendees: AttendeeItem[],
  filters: AttendeeFilters,
): AttendeeItem[] {
  const search = filters.search?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? '';

  return attendees.filter((attendee) => {
    if (filters.eventId && filters.eventId !== 'all' && attendee.eventId !== filters.eventId) {
      return false;
    }
    if (
      filters.ticketType &&
      filters.ticketType !== 'all' &&
      !attendee.ticketType.split(', ').includes(filters.ticketType)
    ) {
      return false;
    }
    if (!search) return true;
    return [attendee.name, attendee.email, attendee.event, attendee.ticketType, attendee.id].some(
      (value) => value.toLowerCase().includes(search),
    );
  });
}

export const useAttendees = () => {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [eventId, setEventId] = useState('all');
  const [ticketType, setTicketType] = useState('all');

  const backendAttendees = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['organizer-attendees', user?.id],
    queryFn: fetchBackendAttendees,
    enabled: !!user,
    refetchInterval: user ? 30_000 : false,
  })
  const allAttendees = backendAttendees.data ?? EMPTY_ATTENDEES
  const attendees = useMemo(
    () => filterAttendees(allAttendees, { search, eventId, ticketType }),
    [allAttendees, search, eventId, ticketType],
  );

  const eventOptions = useMemo(() => {
    const byId = new Map(allAttendees.map((attendee) => [attendee.eventId, attendee.event]));
    return [...byId.entries()]
      .map(([id, title]) => ({ id, title }))
      .sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
  }, [allAttendees]);

  const ticketTypeOptions = useMemo(
    () =>
      [...new Set(allAttendees.flatMap((attendee) => attendee.ticketType.split(', ')))].sort((a, b) =>
        a.localeCompare(b),
      ),
    [allAttendees],
  );

  return {
    attendees,
    totalAttendees: allAttendees.length,
    checkedInCount: allAttendees.filter((attendee) => attendee.status === 'Checked In').length,
    loading: backendAttendees.isLoading,
    error: attendeeErrorMessage(backendAttendees.error),
    search,
    setSearch,
    eventId,
    setEventId,
    ticketType,
    setTicketType,
    eventOptions,
    ticketTypeOptions,
  };
};

import React, { useState } from 'react';

function initials(name: string | undefined): string {
  return name?.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2).map((part) => part[0].toUpperCase()).join('') || 'OR';
}

export const OrganizerDashboardPage: React.FC = () => {
  const [currentTab, setCurrentTab] = useState<'events' | 'staff' | 'attendees' | 'create'>('events');

  const { user } = useAuth();
  const { events, totalEvents, publishedCount, draftCount, totalTickets, search: evSearch, setSearch: setEvSearch, filterStatus, setFilterStatus, addEvent, publishEvent, cancelEvent, restoreEvent, loading: evLoading, loadError: evError } = useEvents();
  const {
    attendees, totalAttendees, checkedInCount,
    search: atSearch, setSearch: setAtSearch,
    eventId: atEventId, setEventId: setAtEventId,
    ticketType: atTicketType, setTicketType: setAtTicketType,
    eventOptions, ticketTypeOptions, loading: attendeeLoading, error: attendeeError,
  } = useAttendees();

  // Throws when the backend refuses, so the form can show the message and stay open.

    await addEvent(newEvent);
    setCurrentTab('events');
  };

    <div className="dashboard-root">
      {/* Sidebar Architecture Layout */}
      <aside className="dashboard-sidebar">
        <div className="sidebar-brand">
          <div className="brand-logo">Tic-EM</div>
          <div className="brand-info">
            <span className="panel-title">Organizer</span>
            <span className="panel-subtitle">Dashboard</span>
          </div>

          <div className="avatar-placeholder">{initials(user?.name)}</div>
          <div className="user-details">
            <span className="user-name">{user?.name ?? 'Organizer'}</span>
            <span className="user-role">Organizer ▼</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <nav className="sidebar-nav">
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'events' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('events')}>
            <CalendarDays size={18} aria-hidden="true" /> My Events
          </button>
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'create' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('create')}>
            <Plus size={18} aria-hidden="true" /> Create Event
          </button>
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'staff' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('staff')}>
            <Users size={18} aria-hidden="true" /> Staff Roster
          </button>
          <button className={`nav-link-btn ${currentTab === 'attendees' ? 'active' : ''}`} onClick={() => setCurrentTab('attendees')}>
            <Ticket size={18} aria-hidden="true" /> Attendees
          </button>
        </nav>
      </aside>

      {/* Main Core View Area */}
      <main className="dashboard-viewport">
        {currentTab === 'create' ? (
          <CreateEventPage onEventCreated={handleEventFormSubmission} onCancel={() => setCurrentTab('events')} />
        ) : (
          <div className="page-content-wrapper">
            
            {/* Context Dependent Metric Row Blocks */}
            {currentTab === 'events' && (
              <>
                <div className="content-header">
                  <div>
                    <h2 className="view-title">My Events</h2>
                    <p className="view-subtitle">Manage and track your active performance profiles.</p>
                  </div>
                  <button className="btn-primary-header" onClick={() => setCurrentTab('create')}>+ Create Event</button>
                </div>
                <div className="metrics-grid">
                  <StatCard title="Total Events" value={totalEvents} icon="📊" color="#3b82f6" />
                  <StatCard title="Published" value={publishedCount} icon="✅" color="#10b981" />
                  <StatCard title="Draft" value={draftCount} icon="🕒" color="#64748b" />
                  <StatCard title="Total Tickets Sold" value={totalTickets.toLocaleString()} icon="📈" color="#f59e0b" />

                  <select className="toolbar-dropdown" value={filterStatus} onChange={(e) => setFilterStatus(e.target.value)}>
                    <option value="All">All Statuses</option>
                    <option value="Published">Published Only</option>
                    <option value="Draft">Drafts Only</option>
                  </select>
                </div>
                {evError && <div className="form-error-banner">{evError}</div>}
                {evLoading ? <p className="view-subtitle">Loading your events...</p> : <EventTable events={events} onPublish={publishEvent} onCancel={cancelEvent} onRestore={restoreEvent} />}
              </>

                  </div>
                  <div className="split-analytics-side">
                    <SalesChart events={events} />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </>
            )}

          </div>
        )}
      </main>
    </div>
  );
};

import type { Booking } from '../bookingTypes';
import styles from './BookingCard.module.css';

function formatDate(iso: string) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

export function BookingCard({ booking }: { booking: Booking }) {
  const statusClass = styles[booking.status]

  return (
    <article className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.info}>
        <h3 className={styles.title}>{booking.eventTitle}</h3>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          {booking.venue} · {formatDate(booking.date)}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          {booking.id} · {booking.quantity} ticket{booking.quantity > 1 ? 's' : ''}
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.side}>
        <strong className={styles.total}>
          {booking.total === undefined ? 'Total unavailable' : `MK ${booking.total.toLocaleString()}`}
        </strong>
        <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{booking.status}</span>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/tickets/components/TicketDetail.tsx

import type { Ticket } from '../ticketTypes';
import { formatDate } from '../ticketFormat';
import { downloadTicketImage, ticketFromView } from '../ticketImage';
import styles from './TicketDetail.module.css';

// The ticket itself: status, event, QR code and the download button.
// The full details (venue, price, purchase time...) are in TicketInfoTable.
export function TicketDetail({ ticket }: { ticket: Ticket }) {
  const statusClass = ticket.status === 'valid' ? styles.valid : styles.used;

  return (
    <article className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.info}>
        <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{ticket.status}</span>
        <h2 className={styles.title}>{ticket.eventTitle}</h2>
        <p className={styles.venue}>
          {ticket.venue}, {ticket.city}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.venue}>
          {formatDate(ticket.date)} · {ticket.time}
        </p>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.download}
          disabled={!ticket.qrCodeDataUrl}
          onClick={() => void downloadTicketImage(ticketFromView(ticket), null, ticket.qrCodeDataUrl)}
        >
          Download ticket
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.qrBox}>
        {ticket.qrCodeDataUrl ? (
          <img
            src={ticket.qrCodeDataUrl}
            width={168}
            height={168}
            alt=""
            style={{ width: 168, height: 168, objectFit: 'contain' }}
          />
        ) : (
          <p className={styles.qrNote}>A QR code is unavailable for this ticket.</p>
        )}
        <p className={styles.qrId}>{ticket.id}</p>
        <p className={styles.qrNote}>
          {ticket.status === 'valid'
            ? 'Show this code at the entrance'
            : ticket.status === 'used'
              ? 'This ticket has already been used'
              : `This ticket is ${ticket.status}`}
        </p>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

import { BookingCard } from '../components/BookingCard'
import { useBookings } from '../hooks/useBookings'
import styles from './BookingHistoryPage.module.css'

export function BookingHistoryPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, error, refetch } = useBookings()

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <h1 className={styles.heading}>Booking history</h1>
        <p className={styles.sub}>Orders and payment status from your account</p>
      </header>
      {isLoading && <p className={styles.state}>Loading your bookings...</p>}
      {isError && (
        <p className={styles.state} role="alert">
          {error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not load your bookings.'}{' '}
          <button type="button" className={styles.retry} onClick={() => refetch()}>Try again</button>
        </p>
      )}
      {!isLoading && !isError && data?.length === 0 && (
        <p className={styles.state}>You have no bookings yet.</p>
      )}
      {!isLoading && !isError && data && data.length > 0 && (
        <section className={styles.list} aria-label="Your bookings">
          {data.map((booking) => <BookingCard key={booking.id} booking={booking} />)}
        </section>
      )}
    </main>
  )
}

import { api } from '../../lib/backend'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../lib/backendEvents'
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { Ticket, TicketStatus } from './ticketTypes'

interface BackendHistoryTicket {
  ticketNumber: string
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  status: 'ISSUED' | 'USED' | 'CANCELLED' | 'REFUNDED'
  issuedAt: string
  checkedInAt: string | null
  qrCodeDataUrl?: string
}

const TICKET_STATUS: Record<BackendHistoryTicket['status'], TicketStatus> = {
  ISSUED: 'valid',
  USED: 'used',
  CANCELLED: 'cancelled',
  REFUNDED: 'refunded',
}

export function toTicketView(record: TicketRecord): Ticket {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    eventTitle: record.eventName,
    venue: record.venue,
    city: record.city,
    date: record.date,
    time: record.time,
    ticketType: record.ticketType,
    seat: `${record.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: record.price,
    holder: record.holder,
    status: record.status,
    purchasedAt: record.createdAt,
    qrCodeDataUrl: record.qrCodeDataUrl,
  }
}

export async function fetchTicketRecords(userId?: string, userName = ''): Promise<TicketRecord[]> {
  if (!userId) return []
  const records = await api<BackendHistoryTicket[]>('/tickets/history')
  const eventIds = [...new Set(records.map((ticket) => ticket.eventId))]
  const events = await Promise.all(eventIds.map((id) => fetchBackendEvent(id)))
  const eventById = new Map(events.map((event) => [event.id, event]))

  return records.flatMap((record) => {
    const event = eventById.get(record.eventId)
    const ticketType = event?.ticketTypes.find((type) => type.id === record.ticketTypeId)
    if (!event || !ticketType) return []

    return [{
      id: record.ticketNumber,
      userId,
      eventId: event.id,
      eventName: event.title,
      date: event.date,
      time: event.time,
      venue: event.venue,
      city: event.city,
      ticketType: ticketType.name,
      price: ticketType.price,
      holder: userName,
      status: TICKET_STATUS[record.status],
      createdAt: record.issuedAt,
      qrCodeDataUrl: record.qrCodeDataUrl,
    } satisfies TicketRecord]
  })
}

export async function fetchTickets(userId?: string, userName = ''): Promise<Ticket[]> {
  const records = await fetchTicketRecords(userId, userName)
  return records.map(toTicketView)
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/features/validation/pages/ScannerPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { FormEvent, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import type { TicketValidationResult } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { useEvents } from '../../events/hooks/useEvents'
import { ScanResult } from '../components/ScanResult'
import { useQrScanner } from '../hooks/useQrScanner'
import { validateTicket } from '../hooks/useTicketValidator'

export function ScannerPage(): ReactNode {
  const [result, setResult] = useState<TicketValidationResult | null>(null)
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<TicketValidationResult[]>([])
  const [manualCode, setManualCode] = useState('')
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false)
  const checkingRef = useRef(false)
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { data: backendEvents = [], isLoading: loadingEvents } = useEvents()
  const events = backendEvents
  const [selectedEventId, setSelectedEventId] = useState(events[0]?.id ?? '')
  const selectedEvent = events.find((e) => e.id === selectedEventId) ?? events[0]
  const activeEventId = selectedEvent?.id ?? ''

  const handleCode = useCallback(
    async (rawCode: string) => {
      const outcome = await (async (): Promise<TicketValidationResult> => {
        if (!activeEventId) {
          return {
            status: 'invalid',
            title: 'No event selected',
            message: 'Choose the event you are checking tickets for.',
          }
        }
        try {
          return await validateTicket(rawCode, activeEventId)
        } catch (caught) {
          return {
            status: 'invalid',
            title: 'Could not check ticket',
            message: caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'The ticket could not be checked. Try again.',
          }
        }
      })()

      setResult(outcome)
      setHistory((previous) => [outcome, ...previous].slice(0, 6))
      setManualCode('')
      if (outcome.status === 'valid') {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['event-stats'] })
      }
    },
    [queryClient, activeEventId],
  )

  const checkCode = useCallback(
    (rawCode: string) => {
      if (checkingRef.current) return
      checkingRef.current = true
      setChecking(true)
      void handleCode(rawCode).finally(() => {
        checkingRef.current = false
        setChecking(false)
      })
    },
    [handleCode],
  )

  const { videoRef, status, error, start, stop } = useQrScanner(checkCode)

  function handleManualSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    checkCode(manualCode)
  }

  const checkedIn = history.filter((item) => item.status === 'valid').length

  const scanning = status === 'scanning'

  if (!selectedEvent && loadingEvents) {
    return (
      <div>
        <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
        <div className="alert info" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>Loading events…</div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!selectedEvent) {
    return (
      <div>
        <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
        <div className="alert info" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
          No events are assigned to you yet. Ask your organizer to assign you to an event.
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="row">
        <div>
          <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
          <p className="m">{selectedEvent.venue}</p>
          <label className="m" style={{ display: 'block', marginTop: 8 }}>
            Event gate
            <select
              className="in"
              style={{ marginTop: 4 }}
              value={selectedEvent.id}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setSelectedEventId(event.target.value)
                setResult(null)
                setHistory([])
              }}
            >
              {events.map((event) => (
                <option key={event.id} value={event.id}>
                  {event.title}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>
        <div className="card stat" style={{ minWidth: 160 }}>
          <span className="m">Checked in this session</span>
          <b>{checkedIn}</b>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="split" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
        <div className="g">
          <div className="cam">
            <video ref={videoRef} playsInline muted className="cam-video" />
            {!scanning && (
              <div className="cam-hint">
                <div className="cam-frame" />
                <p>Point the camera at a ticket QR code to validate it.</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="row" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {scanning ? (
              <button type="button" className="btn o" onClick={stop}>
                Stop camera
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn"
                onClick={start}
                disabled={status === 'starting' || checking}
              >
                {status === 'starting' ? 'Starting camera…' : 'Start camera'}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {error && <div className="alert error">{error}</div>}

          <form className="row" style={{ gap: 8 }} onSubmit={handleManualSubmit}>
            <input
              className="in"
              style={{ margin: 0 }}
              placeholder="Paste or scan the ticket QR token"
              value={manualCode}
              onChange={(event) => setManualCode(event.target.value)}
            />
            <button type="submit" className="btn" disabled={!manualCode.trim() || checking}>
              {checking ? 'Checking…' : 'Validate'}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="alert info">
            <>
              <b>Server-checked tickets</b>
              <p className="m">The backend records each scan, so a ticket is accepted only once.</p>
            </>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          {result ? (
            <ScanResult result={result} />
          ) : (
            <div className="alert info">Scan or enter a ticket to see the validation result.</div>
          )}

          <h4>Other results</h4>
          {history.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="m">No scans yet.</p>
          ) : (
            history.map((item, index) => (
              <ScanResult key={`${item.status}-${index}`} result={item} compact />
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
