FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/lib/backend.ts
================================================================================
// Application and payment requests are served by the Docker backend.
export const USE_BACKEND = true

// The dev server forwards /be/* to the backend (see vite.config.ts), so the browser
// never calls port 3000 directly and there is no CORS problem.
export const API = '/be'

const TOKEN_KEY = 'tm_token'
export const NO_SERVER = 'Cannot reach the server. Is the backend running?'

export function getToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function setToken(token: string | null): void {
  try {
    if (token) localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token)
    else localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY)
  } catch {
    // storage unavailable: the user simply has to log in again
  }
}

function messageFrom(status: number, data: unknown): string {
  const message = data && typeof data === 'object' && 'message' in data ? (data as { message: unknown }).message : null
  if (status >= 500) return NO_SERVER
  if (Array.isArray(message)) return message.join('. ')
  if (typeof message === 'string') return message
  return `Request failed (${status})`
}

// JSON request to the backend. Adds the login token when there is one.
export async function api<T>(path: string, options: { method?: string; body?: unknown } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {}
  const token = getToken()
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  if (options.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'

  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(API + path, {
      method: options.method ?? 'GET',
      headers,
      body: options.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(options.body),
    })
  } catch {
    throw new Error(NO_SERVER)
  }

  let data: unknown = null
  try {
    data = await response.json()
  } catch {
    data = null
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(messageFrom(response.status, data)) as Error & { status: number }
    error.status = response.status
    throw error
  }
  return data as T
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/lib/backendEvents.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from './backend'
import type { StoredEvent } from './events'
import type { TicketType } from '../types/event'

// Published events from the backend, converted to the shape the pages already use.
const KNOWN_CITIES = ['Lilongwe', 'Blantyre', 'Mzuzu', 'Zomba', 'Mangochi', 'Salima']
const IMAGES = ['/images/events/event-1.webp', '/images/events/event-2.jpg', '/images/events/event-3.webp']

interface BackendEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string
  venue: string
  location: string
  startAt: string
  status?: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'
}

interface BackendTicketType {
  ticketTypeId: string
  name: string
  price: number | string
  quantity: number
  remaining: number
}

interface Page {
  data: BackendEvent[]
  meta: { totalPages: number }
}

function toTicketType(type: BackendTicketType): TicketType {
  return {
    id: String(type.ticketTypeId),
    name: type.name,
    price: Number(type.price),
    capacity: type.quantity,
    sold: Math.max(0, type.quantity - type.remaining),
  }
}

// The backend has no category, image or organizer name yet.
function toStoredEvent(event: BackendEvent, ticketTypes: BackendTicketType[]): StoredEvent {
  const start = new Date(event.startAt)
  const location = event.location ?? ''
  return {
    id: String(event.id),
    title: event.title,
    description: event.description,
    category: '',
    city: KNOWN_CITIES.find((city) => location.toLowerCase().includes(city.toLowerCase())) ?? location,
    venue: event.venue,
    date: start.toLocaleDateString('en-CA'),
    time: start.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    organizer: '',
    image: IMAGES[(Number(event.id) || 0) % IMAGES.length],
    status: event.status === 'DRAFT' ? 'Draft' : event.status === 'CANCELLED' ? 'Cancelled' : 'Published',
    ticketTypes: ticketTypes.map(toTicketType),
  }
}

// All published events with their ticket types.
export async function fetchPublishedEvents(): Promise<StoredEvent[]> {
  const events: BackendEvent[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages = 1
  do {
    const result = await api<Page>(`/events?page=${page}&limit=50`)
    events.push(...result.data)
    totalPages = result.meta.totalPages
    page += 1
  } while (page <= totalPages)

  return Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => toStoredEvent(event, await api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${event.id}/ticket-types`))),
  )
}

export async function fetchBackendEvent(eventId: string): Promise<StoredEvent> {
  const event = await api<BackendEvent>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`)
  const ticketTypes = await api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/ticket-types`)
  return toStoredEvent(event, ticketTypes)
}

export { DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES, resolveEventImage } from './eventImage'

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/lib/backendOrganizer.ts

import { API, api, getToken } from './backend'
import { resolveEventImage } from './backendEvents'
import type { EventItem } from '../features/organizer/Data/Events'

// Organizer side of the backend: create, publish, cancel and list the organizer's own events.

export interface NewTicketType {
  name: string
  price: number
  quantity: number
  perOrderLimit?: number
}

export interface NewEventInput {
  name: string
  description: string
  venue: string
  location: string
  startAt: string // ISO date-time
  endAt: string // ISO date-time
  ticketTypes: NewTicketType[]
  status: 'Published' | 'Draft'
  imageUrl?: string // an uploaded image (/uploads/..), a bundled default, or nothing
}

interface BackendMyEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  venue: string
  startAt: string
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'
  publishedAt?: string | null
  imageUrl?: string | null
}

interface MyEventsPage {
  data: BackendMyEvent[]
  meta: { totalPages: number }
}

interface SalesSummary {
  totals: { sold: number }
}

const STATUS: Record<BackendMyEvent['status'], EventItem['status']> = {
  DRAFT: 'Draft',
  PUBLISHED: 'Published',
  CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
}

// "10 May 2026 • 19:00", the same style the dashboard table already shows
export function formatEventDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso)
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return iso
  const day = date.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
  const time = date.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
  return `${day} • ${time}`
}

// The backend wants the total capacity as well. It is simply the sum of the ticket types.
export function totalCapacity(ticketTypes: NewTicketType[]): number {
  return ticketTypes.reduce((sum, type) => sum + type.quantity, 0)
}

async function soldFor(eventId: string): Promise<number> {
  const summary = await api<SalesSummary>(`/events/${eventId}/sales-summary`)
  return summary.totals.sold
}

// Every event of the logged-in organizer, in all statuses, with tickets sold.
export async function listMyEvents(): Promise<EventItem[]> {
  const events: BackendMyEvent[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages = 1
  do {
    const result = await api<MyEventsPage>(`/events/mine?page=${page}&limit=50`)
    events.push(...result.data)
    totalPages = result.meta.totalPages
    page += 1
  } while (page <= totalPages)

  return Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => ({
      id: String(event.id),
      name: event.title,
      date: formatEventDate(event.startAt),
      venue: event.venue,
      ticketsSold: event.status === 'PUBLISHED' ? await soldFor(event.id) : 0,
      status: STATUS[event.status] ?? 'Draft',
      ...(event.status === 'CANCELLED'
        ? { restoreStatus: event.publishedAt ? 'Published' as const : 'Draft' as const }
        : {}),
      image: resolveEventImage(event.imageUrl, String(event.id)),
    })),
  )
}

export async function setOrganizerEventStatus(
  eventId: string,
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED',
): Promise<void> {
  await api(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    body: { status },
  })
}

// Publishing makes the event public. Tickets only exist after "generate", so both run together.
// Generating again is safe: it only creates tickets that are still missing.
export async function publishEvent(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/publish`, { method: 'POST' })
  try {
    await api(`/tickets/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/generate`, { method: 'POST' })
  } catch (error) {
    const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'unknown error'
    throw new Error(`The event is published but its tickets could not be created (${reason}). Press Publish again to retry.`)
  }
}

export const MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024

// Sends a picture to the backend and returns the path to save as the event's imageUrl.
export async function uploadEventImage(file: File): Promise<string> {
  if (!/^image\/(jpeg|png|webp)$/.test(file.type)) throw new Error('Choose a JPG, PNG or WEBP image.')
  if (file.size > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES) throw new Error('The image must be 5 MB or smaller.')

  const form = new FormData()
  form.append('file', file)
  const token = getToken()

  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API}/events/images`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
      body: form,
    })
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Cannot reach the server. Is the backend running?')
  }

  let data: { imageUrl?: string; message?: string | string[] } | null = null
  try {
    data = await response.json()
  } catch {
    data = null
  }
  if (!response.ok || !data?.imageUrl) {
    const message = Array.isArray(data?.message) ? data.message.join('. ') : data?.message
    throw new Error(response.status >= 500 ? 'The image could not be uploaded. Try again.' : message || 'The image could not be uploaded.')
  }
  return data.imageUrl
}

export async function createOrganizerEvent(input: NewEventInput): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  const created = await api<{ id: string }>('/events', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: {
      title: input.name.trim(),
      description: input.description.trim(),
      venue: input.venue.trim(),
      location: input.location.trim(),
      startAt: input.startAt,
      endAt: input.endAt,
      ...(input.imageUrl ? { imageUrl: input.imageUrl } : {}),
      capacity: totalCapacity(input.ticketTypes),
      ticketTypes: input.ticketTypes.map((type) => ({
        name: type.name.trim(),
        price: type.price,
        currency: 'MWK',
        quantity: type.quantity,
        perOrderLimit: type.perOrderLimit ?? 10,
      })),
    },
  })
  if (input.status === 'Published') await publishEvent(created.id)
  return { id: String(created.id) }
}

export async function cancelOrganizerEvent(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
  await setOrganizerEventStatus(eventId, 'CANCELLED')
}

export async function restoreOrganizerEvent(
  eventId: string,
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED',
): Promise<void> {
  await setOrganizerEventStatus(eventId, status)
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/lib/backendUsers.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from './backend'

export type ManagedRole = 'customer' | 'organizer' | 'staff' | 'admin'

export interface BackendManagedUser {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: ManagedRole
  createdAt?: string
  status?: 'active' | 'disabled'
}

type BackendRole = 'ATTENDEE' | 'ORGANIZER' | 'CHECK_IN_STAFF' | 'ADMIN'

const ROLE_FROM_BACKEND: Record<BackendRole, ManagedRole> = {
  ATTENDEE: 'customer',
  ORGANIZER: 'organizer',
  CHECK_IN_STAFF: 'staff',
  ADMIN: 'admin',
}

const ROLE_TO_BACKEND: Record<ManagedRole, BackendRole> = {
  customer: 'ATTENDEE',
  organizer: 'ORGANIZER',
  staff: 'CHECK_IN_STAFF',
  admin: 'ADMIN',
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
}

function mapUser(value: unknown): BackendManagedUser {
  if (!isRecord(value)) throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid user record.')
  const { id, name, email, role, createdAt, created_at } = value
  if (
    (typeof id !== 'string' && typeof id !== 'number') ||
    typeof name !== 'string' ||
    typeof email !== 'string' ||
    typeof role !== 'string'
  ) {
    throw new Error('The backend returned a user missing its ID, name, email, or role.')
  }
  if (!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ROLE_FROM_BACKEND, role)) {
    throw new Error(`The backend returned an unsupported user role: ${role}`)
  }
  return {
    id: String(id),
    name,
    email,
    role: ROLE_FROM_BACKEND[role as BackendRole],
    createdAt: typeof createdAt === 'string' ? createdAt : typeof created_at === 'string' ? created_at : undefined,
    status: value.isActive === true || value.status === 'ACTIVE'
      ? 'active'
      : value.isActive === false || value.status === 'DISABLED'
        ? 'disabled'
        : undefined,
  }
}

export async function fetchManagedUsers(): Promise<BackendManagedUser[]> {
  const users: unknown[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages: number | undefined

  do {
    const result: unknown = await api<unknown>(`/users?page=${page}&limit=100`)
    const list = Array.isArray(result)
      ? result
      : isRecord(result) && Array.isArray(result.data)
        ? result.data
        : isRecord(result) && Array.isArray(result.items)
          ? result.items
          : isRecord(result) && Array.isArray(result.users)
            ? result.users
            : null
    if (!list) {
      throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid user list.')
    }
    users.push(...list)

    const meta = isRecord(result) && isRecord(result.meta) ? result.meta : null
    const pagination = isRecord(result) && isRecord(result.pagination) ? result.pagination : null
    const reportedPages = meta?.totalPages ?? pagination?.totalPages ??
      (isRecord(result) ? result.totalPages : undefined)
    if (reportedPages !== undefined) {
      if (typeof reportedPages !== 'number' || !Number.isInteger(reportedPages) || reportedPages < 0) {
        throw new Error('The backend returned invalid user pagination details.')
      }
      totalPages = reportedPages
    } else if (list.length < 100) {
      totalPages = page
    }

    if (list.length === 0) break
    page += 1
  } while (totalPages === undefined || page <= totalPages)

  return users.map(mapUser)
}

export function updateManagedUserRole(userId: string, role: ManagedRole): Promise<void> {
  return api<void>(`/users/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}/role`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    body: { role: ROLE_TO_BACKEND[role] },
  })
}

export function createManagedUser(input: {
  name: string
  email: string
  password: string
  role: ManagedRole
}): Promise<unknown> {
  return api('/users', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: {
      name: input.name.trim(),
      email: input.email.trim(),
      password: input.password,
      role: ROLE_TO_BACKEND[input.role],
    },
  })
}

export function setManagedUserActive(userId: string, isActive: boolean): Promise<void> {
  return api<void>(`/users/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}/status`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    body: { isActive },
  })
}

import { API } from './backend'

// Bundled pictures an organizer can pick instead of uploading their own.
export const DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES = [
  '/images/events/event-1.webp',
  '/images/events/event-2.jpg',
  '/images/events/event-3.webp',
]

// The image of an event: the organizer's upload, a chosen default, or (when nothing was
// chosen) one of the defaults picked from the event id.
export function resolveEventImage(imageUrl: string | null | undefined, eventId: string): string {
  if (imageUrl) {
    // uploads live on the backend, which the browser reaches through the /be proxy
    return imageUrl.startsWith('/uploads/') ? `${API}${imageUrl}` : imageUrl
  }
  let hash = 0
  for (const char of eventId) hash = (hash * 31 + char.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0
  return DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES[hash % DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES.length]
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/lib/paychangu.ts

import { api } from './backend'

export interface PendingPayment {
  txRef: string
  orderNumber: string
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  quantity: number
  holder: string
  userId: string
  createdAt: string
  status: 'pending' | 'completed'
  ticketIds?: string[]
}

export interface VerifiedPayment {
  status: 'success' | 'pending' | 'failed'
  tx_ref: string
}

interface CreateOrderInput {
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  quantity: number
}

export const PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.pending-payments'

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
}

function findOrderNumber(value: unknown, depth = 0): string | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(value) || depth > 3) return undefined

  for (const key of ['orderNumber', 'order_number', 'number']) {
    const candidate = value[key]
    if ((typeof candidate === 'string' || typeof candidate === 'number') && String(candidate).trim()) {
      return String(candidate).trim()
    }
  }

  for (const key of ['data', 'order', 'result']) {
    const nested = findOrderNumber(value[key], depth + 1)
    if (nested) return nested
  }
  return undefined
}

function readPending(): PendingPayment[] {
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY)
  if (!raw) return []

  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw)
  if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error('Saved payment references are invalid. Clear them and try again.')
  return parsed as PendingPayment[]
}

function writePending(items: PendingPayment[]): void {
  window.localStorage.setItem(PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(items.slice(0, 50)))
}

export function savePendingPayment(payment: PendingPayment): void {
  writePending([payment, ...readPending().filter((item) => item.txRef !== payment.txRef)])
}

export function getPendingPayment(txRef: string): PendingPayment | undefined {
  return readPending().find((item) => item.txRef === txRef)
}

export function latestPendingPayment(): PendingPayment | undefined {
  return readPending().find((item) => item.status === 'pending')
}

export function markPaymentCompleted(txRef: string, ticketIds: string[]): void {
  writePending(
    readPending().map((item) => (item.txRef === txRef ? { ...item, status: 'completed', ticketIds } : item)),
  )
}

export function addPendingTickets(txRef: string, ticketIds: string[]): void {
  if (ticketIds.length === 0) return
  writePending(
    readPending().map((item) =>
      item.txRef === txRef
        ? { ...item, ticketIds: Array.from(new Set([...(item.ticketIds ?? []), ...ticketIds])) }
        : item,
    ),
  )
}

export async function createOrder(input: CreateOrderInput): Promise<{ orderNumber: string }> {
  const result = await api<unknown>('/orders', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: {
      eventId: input.eventId,
      items: [{ ticketTypeId: input.ticketTypeId, quantity: input.quantity }],
    },
  })

  const orderNumber = findOrderNumber(result)
  if (!orderNumber) {
    throw new Error('The backend did not return an order number.')
  }
  return { orderNumber }
}

export async function initiatePayment(orderNumber: string): Promise<{ checkout_url: string; tx_ref: string }> {
  const result = await api<unknown>(
    `/payments/orders/${encodeURIComponent(orderNumber)}/checkout`,
    { method: 'POST' },
  )
  const dataObj = isRecord(result) && isRecord(result.data) ? result.data : result;
  const checkoutUrl = isRecord(dataObj) ? (dataObj.checkout_url || dataObj.checkoutUrl) : undefined;
  const txRef = isRecord(dataObj) ? (dataObj.tx_ref || dataObj.txRef) : undefined;

  if (typeof checkoutUrl !== 'string' || typeof txRef !== 'string' || !txRef.trim()) {
    throw new Error('The backend did not return a PayChangu checkout URL and transaction reference.')
  }
  let url: URL
  try {
    url = new URL(checkoutUrl)
  } catch {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid PayChangu checkout URL.')
  }
  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' && url.protocol !== 'http:') {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an unsupported PayChangu checkout URL.')
  }
  return { checkout_url: url.toString(), tx_ref: txRef }
}

export async function verifyPayment(txRef: string): Promise<VerifiedPayment> {
  const result = await api<unknown>(`/payments/${encodeURIComponent(txRef)}/verify`)
  // The backend reports the payment as paymentStatus; older responses used status.
  const rawStatus = isRecord(result)
    ? (typeof result.paymentStatus === 'string' ? result.paymentStatus : result.status)
    : undefined
  if (!isRecord(result) || typeof rawStatus !== 'string') {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid payment verification response.')
  }

  const status = rawStatus.toLowerCase()
  const mappedStatus =
    ['success', 'successful', 'paid', 'completed'].includes(status)
      ? 'success'
      : ['pending', 'processing', 'initiated'].includes(status)
        ? 'pending'
        : 'failed'

  const responseTxRef =
    typeof result.tx_ref === 'string'
      ? result.tx_ref
      : typeof result.txRef === 'string'
        ? result.txRef
        : txRef
  return { status: mappedStatus, tx_ref: responseTxRef }
}

export type TicketStatus = 'valid' | 'used' | 'cancelled' | 'refunded'

export interface TicketRecord {
  id: string
  userId: string
  eventId: string
  eventName: string
  date: string
  time: string
  venue: string
  city: string
  ticketType: string
  price: number
  holder: string
  status: TicketStatus
  createdAt: string
  checkedInAt?: string
  qrCodeDataUrl?: string
}

export type TicketValidationStatus = 'valid' | 'invalid' | 'used' | 'wrong_event'

export interface TicketValidationResult {
  status: TicketValidationStatus
  title: string
  message: string
  ticket?: TicketRecord
  tampered?: boolean
}

export function parseTicketCode(raw: string): { id: string; tag: string | null } {
  const cleaned = raw.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/^TICKET:/, '')
  const dot = cleaned.lastIndexOf('.')
  if (dot === -1) return { id: cleaned, tag: null }
  return { id: cleaned.slice(0, dot), tag: cleaned.slice(dot + 1) }
}

export function normalizeTicketCode(raw: string): string {
  return parseTicketCode(raw).id
}

import { createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { AppLayout } from '../components/AppLayout';
import { LoginPage, requireAuth } from '../features/authentication';
import { EventsPage, EventDetailsPage } from '../features/events';
import { CheckoutPage, PaymentReturnPage } from '../features/checkout';
import { MyTicketsPage, BookingHistoryPage, TicketDetailPage } from '../features/tickets';
import { ScannerPage } from '../features/validation';
import { OrganizerDashboardPage, CreateEventPage, StaffManagementPage } from '../features/organizer';
import { AdminDashboardPage } from '../features/admin/pages/AdminDashboardPage';
import { HomePage } from '../features/landing'
import { validateEventSearch } from '../features/landing/filters';
import { BackendUnavailablePage } from '../components/BackendUnavailablePage';
import NotFoundPage from '../components/NotFoundPage';
import type { ComponentType } from 'react';

const rootRoute = createRootRoute({ component: AppLayout });

const page = <P extends string>(path: P, component: ComponentType<any>, beforeLoad?: ReturnType<typeof requireAuth>) => createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path, component: component as any, beforeLoad });

const indexRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/',
  component: HomePage,
  validateSearch: validateEventSearch,
});
const eventsRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/events',
  component: EventsPage,
  validateSearch: validateEventSearch,
});
const loginRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/login',
  component: LoginPage,
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): { redirect?: string } => ({ redirect: typeof search.redirect === 'string' ? search.redirect : undefined }),
});
const notFoundRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '*', component: NotFoundPage })

const routeTree = rootRoute.addChildren([
  indexRoute,
  loginRoute,
  eventsRoute,
  page('/events/$eventId', EventDetailsPage),
  page('/checkout/return', PaymentReturnPage, requireAuth('customer')), // PayChangu sends customers back here
  page('/checkout/$eventId', CheckoutPage, requireAuth('customer')),
  page('/tickets', MyTicketsPage, requireAuth('customer')),         // <-- Added base tickets route
  page('/tickets/$ticketId', TicketDetailPage, requireAuth('customer')), // <-- Kept specific route
  page('/bookings', BookingHistoryPage, requireAuth('customer')),
  page('/organizer/kyc', () => <BackendUnavailablePage title="Organizer verification unavailable" feature="Organizer verification" backTo="/organizer/dashboard" />, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/dashboard', OrganizerDashboardPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/events/new', CreateEventPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/events/$eventId/edit', CreateEventPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/staff', StaffManagementPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/admin', AdminDashboardPage, requireAuth('admin')),
  page('/scan', ScannerPage, requireAuth('staff')),
  notFoundRoute,
]);

export const router = createRouter({ routeTree });

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register { router: typeof router; }
}

FILE: .connect-backup/20261110-122544/src/types/event.ts

export interface TicketType {
  id: string
  name: string
  price: number
  capacity: number
  sold: number
}

export interface Event {
  id: string
  title: string
  category: string
  city: string
  venue: string
  date: string // ISO date, e.g. 2026-11-14
  time: string // 24h, e.g. 18:00
  description: string
  organizer: string
  ownerId?: string // id of the organizer account that owns the event
  image: string
  ticketTypes: TicketType[]
}

// cheapest ticket type price, used for cards, filters and sorting
export function fromPrice(event: Event): number {
  if (event.ticketTypes.length === 0) return 0
  return Math.min(...event.ticketTypes.map((type) => type.price))
}

export function remainingSeats(type: TicketType): number {
  return Math.max(0, type.capacity - type.sold)
}

export function isSoldOut(event: Event): boolean {
  return (
    event.ticketTypes.length > 0 &&
    event.ticketTypes.every((type) => remainingSeats(type) === 0)
  )
}

// true when the event date is today or later
export function isUpcomingEvent(event: Event): boolean {
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA') // YYYY-MM-DD
  return event.date >= today
}

FILE: PAYCHANGU.md
================================================================================
# PayChangu payments

The frontend does not call PayChangu directly and does not store a PayChangu
secret. It asks the backend to create an order and start checkout; the backend
owns PayChangu credentials, payment verification, and the final order state.

## Backend configuration

Configure the PayChangu secret and callback/webhook URLs in the backend
environment, following the backend deployment documentation. Do not put
PayChangu credentials in this frontend repository or in a `VITE_` variable.

Set `BACKEND_URL` in the frontend `.env` to the backend base URL (default:
`http://localhost:3000`). The Vite development server proxies `/be` requests
to that backend. Restart Vite after changing `.env`.

## Payment flow

1. The frontend submits the selected event and ticket quantities to
   `POST /orders`.
2. It starts hosted checkout through
   `POST /payments/orders/{orderNumber}/checkout`.
3. The customer completes payment on PayChangu and returns to the frontend.
4. The frontend asks the backend to verify the transaction with
   `GET /payments/{txRef}/verify`.
5. Once the backend reports successful payment, the frontend claims tickets
   using `POST /tickets/purchase` with the verified `txRef`. Tickets and
   payment status remain backend-owned.

The frontend requires the backend to return the order number, checkout URL,
transaction reference, and verification status expected by these API calls.
Missing or unsuccessful payment details are reported as errors; the frontend
does not issue tickets as a fallback.

## Development and troubleshooting

- Start the backend and configure its PayChangu test credentials there before
  testing a payment.
- Ensure `BACKEND_URL` points to the reachable backend and that the backend
  has PayChangu callback and webhook routes configured.
- The frontend stores only a pending transaction reference locally to match a
  browser return to the checkout attempt. It does not treat local storage as
  payment confirmation or as a source of order/ticket data.
- If checkout or verification fails, inspect the backend logs and API response.
  Do not add a direct browser-to-PayChangu request or expose backend secrets
  in the frontend.

# COM311 – Group FF6

Event management app built with Vite + React + TypeScript, using Tanstack Query and Tanstack Router.

## Repo

https://github.com/bsc-com-22-24-svg/FF6_Ticket_Master

## Getting started

```bash
git clone https://github.com/bsc-com-22-24-svg/FF6_Ticket_Master.git
cd FF6_Ticket_Master
npm install
npm run dev
```

Other scripts:

```bash
npm run build     # type-check + production build
npm run lint      # oxlint
npm test          # vitest unit tests
```

## Backend

The frontend uses the backend Docker API for authentication, events, orders,
payments, tickets, validation, and supported administration actions. It does
not seed demo accounts, events, bookings, or tickets.

1. Start the backend Docker service.
2. Copy `.env.example` to `.env` and set `BACKEND_URL` to the backend address
   (default: `http://localhost:3000`).
3. Run `npm run dev`. The Vite server forwards `/be/*` requests to that backend.

PayChangu credentials and callback/return URL configuration belong on the
backend; they must not be added to this frontend's environment.
Some dashboard features remain unavailable until the backend exposes their API.

FILE: Ticket_Master_Backend_Deployment_Guide.md
================================================================================
**TICKET MASTER  |  BACKEND DEPLOYMENT**

# **Backend Deployment Guide**

### Ticket Master API • Docker Hub image v1.1

This guide explains how frontend developers can download and run the Ticket Master NestJS backend using Docker. It assumes the published image is public on Docker Hub.

|**Docker image**|alicekayla/ticket-backend:1.1|
|---|---|
|**API base URL**|http://localhost:3000|
|**Swagger documentation**|http://localhost:3000/api|



## **1. Requirements**

- Install Docker Desktop (Windows/macOS) or Docker Engine (Linux), and make sure it is running.

- Have a PostgreSQL database that uses the Ticket Master project's schema.

- Obtain the project's database schema SQL file (event_ticket_booking_schema.sql) or connection details for a database already prepared by the team.

- Have the database host, port, database name, username and password supplied by the team.

Important: The Docker image contains the backend application only. It does not contain PostgreSQL, the database schema, or the team's existing records. Each developer must connect to an accessible database. Do not point a local test backend at a production database unless the team explicitly authorizes it.

## **2. Download the backend image**

Open a terminal (PowerShell, Command Prompt, Git Bash or a Linux/macOS terminal) and run:

<mark>docker pull alicekayla/ticket-backend:1.1</mark>

A Docker Hub login is normally not required if the repository is public. If Docker reports that the image cannot be found or access is denied, confirm that the image has been pushed and that the repository visibility/access permissions are correct.

## **3. Prepare PostgreSQL**

Choose ONE of the following database arrangements. The backend must be able to reach PostgreSQL from inside its Docker container.

### **Option A — Use a database supplied by the team**

- Ask the backend/database team for the database host, port, database name, username and password.

- The database must already contain the Ticket Master tables expected by backend version 1.1.

- Use the hostname/IP and port that are reachable from your computer and Docker environment.

### **Option B — Run your own PostgreSQL container**

Create a dedicated Docker network so the backend can reach PostgreSQL by container name. Use a local development database and a password of your own choice:

docker network create ticket-network docker volume create ticket-db-data docker run -d --name ticket-db --network ticket-network \

Ticket Master Backend • Image version 1.1

**TICKET MASTER  |  BACKEND DEPLOYMENT**

-e POSTGRES_USER=postgres \ -e POSTGRES_PASSWORD=CHANGE_THIS_LOCAL_PASSWORD \ -e POSTGRES_DB=ticket_master \ -p 5433:5432 \ -v ticket-db-data:/var/lib/postgresql/data \ postgres:16

Next, initialize the database using the project's event_ticket_booking_schema.sql file. Ask the team for this file if it is not available in the project repository. For example, from the directory containing the SQL file, run:

<mark>docker exec -i ticket-db psql -U postgres -d ticket_master < event_ticket_booking_schema.sql</mark>

Run the schema import only against a new, empty development database. The import may fail or duplicate objects if the schema was already applied. Do not delete the Docker volume to solve schema problems; ask the team to inspect the database first.

For Option B, the backend's database host is ticket-db and its database port is 5432 (container-to-container port, not the host-mapped port 5433).

## **4. Create the backend environment file**

In the directory where you will run the backend container, create a file named .env.docker. Do not commit this file to Git or share its passwords in chat.

NODE_ENV=production PORT=3000 DB_HOST=ticket-db DB_PORT=5432 DB_USERNAME=postgres DB_PASSWORD=CHANGE_THIS_LOCAL_PASSWORD DB_NAME=ticket_master

JWT_SECRET=REPLACE_WITH_A_RANDOM_SECRET_AT_LEAST_32_CHARACTERS_LONG JWT_EXPIRES_IN=15m

Environment values depend on the database arrangement:

- For Option B above, keep DB_HOST=ticket-db and DB_PORT=5432.

- For a database running directly on the same Windows/Mac host, use DB_HOST=host.docker.internal and the host-published port (for example, 5433).

- For a remote team database, use the host and port supplied by the team. The remote database must allow connections from your environment.

Use the same database password that was configured for PostgreSQL. Generate a strong JWT secret of at least 32 characters. Never use the example placeholders literally.

## **5. Run the backend container**

If using Option B, make sure the backend joins the same Docker network:

docker run -d --name ticket-backend \ --network ticket-network \

Ticket Master Backend • Image version 1.1

**TICKET MASTER  |  BACKEND DEPLOYMENT**

--env-file .env.docker \ -p 3000:3000 \ alicekayla/ticket-backend:1.1

If using Option A (a team/remote database or a host database), the backend usually does not need the ticket-network argument. Run:

docker run -d --name ticket-backend \ --env-file .env.docker \ -p 3000:3000 \ alicekayla/ticket-backend:1.1

If port 3000 is already in use, change the host side of the mapping, for example -p 3001:3000, and open http://localhost:3001/api. Keep PORT=3000 inside the container.

## **6. Verify that the backend is running**

Check the container status:

<mark>docker ps</mark>

View startup logs:

<mark>docker logs ticket-backend</mark>

A successful startup should include: “Nest application successfully started”. If the container exits, inspect the logs before trying to recreate it.

Open the Swagger API documentation in a browser:

<mark>http://localhost:3000/api</mark>

The API base URL for the frontend is:

<mark>http://localhost:3000</mark>

## **7. Configure the frontend**

Set the frontend's API base URL to http://localhost:3000 when the frontend is running on the same developer computer. If the frontend runs in another container, localhost refers to that frontend container; use a shared Docker network and the backend service/container name, or the host address as appropriate. Browser-based frontend code generally calls the host-published URL.

Use the API routes and request/response formats shown in Swagger. Authentication-protected endpoints require the access token returned by the login endpoint, supplied as a Bearer token.

## **8. Useful Docker commands**

|**Task**|**Command**|
|---|---|
|See running containers|docker ps|
|See all containers, including stopped ones|docker ps -a|
|Follow backend logs|docker logs -f ticket-backend|
|Stop the backend|docker stop ticket-backend|



Ticket Master Backend • Image version 1.1

**TICKET MASTER  |  BACKEND DEPLOYMENT**

|Start the existing backend container|docker start ticket-backend|
|---|---|
|Remove only the backend container|docker rm -f ticket-backend|
|Pull a newer published image|docker pull alicekayla/ticket-backend:1.1|



## **9. Troubleshooting**

**ECONNREFUSED / connection refused —** Check that PostgreSQL is running, the host and port are correct, and the database accepts network connections. For two containers on ticket-network, use DB_HOST=ticket-db and DB_PORT=5432.

**password authentication failed —** The DB_USERNAME or DB_PASSWORD in .env.docker does not match the PostgreSQL account. Correct the values, then recreate the backend container so it reads the updated environment file.

**Nest application starts then exits —** Run docker logs ticket-backend and inspect the first error. Common causes are database connectivity, invalid environment variables, or a missing schema.

**port is already allocated —** Choose another host port, such as -p 3001:3000, and use the matching localhost URL. **container name already in use —** If it is your own old backend container, inspect it first. You may remove only that backend container with docker rm -f ticket-backend, then run it again. Do not remove the database container or volume.

**API responds but a route returns an error —** Check the route, HTTP method, authentication/role requirements and request schema in Swagger. A successful container start does not guarantee every endpoint or workflow has been tested.

## **10. Important notes**

- The backend image does not bundle PostgreSQL or database records.

- Do not run TypeORM migrations or enable schema synchronization unless the backend/database team explicitly instructs you to. Version 1.1 is intended to use the project's existing schema.

- A Docker volume stores PostgreSQL data independently of the container. Do not run docker volume rm, docker volume prune, or docker system prune as a troubleshooting shortcut.

- Never publish .env.docker, database passwords, JWT secrets or production credentials.

- The image tag 1.1 is a fixed version. If the team publishes a newer tag, use the exact tag announced by the team.

## **Quick start checklist**

- ☐ Docker is installed and running.

- ☐ The image alicekayla/ticket-backend:1.1 has been pulled.

- ☐ A compatible Ticket Master PostgreSQL database is available and initialized.

- ☐ .env.docker contains the correct DB settings and a strong JWT_SECRET.

- ☐ The backend container is running.

- ☐ docker logs ticket-backend shows successful Nest application startup.

- ☐ Swagger opens at http://localhost:3000/api.

_If database credentials or the schema file are unavailable, contact the backend team before proceeding._

Ticket Master Backend • Image version 1.1

# Functional requirements for event organizer

1. Set and update ticket pricing
2. Manage capacity and availability in real time
3. Track sales and attendance
4. Control access and validate tickets
5. Monitor overall performance of events

Functional requirements identified for ticket generation and door validation:

1. Ticket Generation

//System should be able to:

 a. Generate a digital ticket after successful purchase.
 b. Assign a unique identifier to each ticket.
 c. Generate a QR code/barcode for the ticket.
 d. Display relevant event and ticket information.
 e. Allow the customer to access/view the digital ticket.

2. Door Validation
//System should be able to:

 a. Allow the event player to scan the QR code/barcode.
 b. Verify ticket validity.
 c. Verify that the ticket belongs to the relevant event.
 d. Reject invalid tickets.
 d. Prevent reuse of already validated tickets.
 e. Display the validation result to the event player.

 # Non-Functional Requirements

### Performance & Caching
*   The web application must cache results to reduce API calls.
*   Caching and API data fetching must be handled efficiently using Tanstack Query.
*   The ticket purchasing and event discovery processes must load quickly and operate in as few steps as possible to prevent slow transactions.

### Responsiveness across devices
*   The web application interfaces developed for both the customer and the event organizer must be fully responsive and function seamlessly on mobile, tablet, and desktop screens.

### Usability/accessibility expectations
*   The platform must deliver a unified, low-friction user experience that eliminates the cumbersome, multi-step processes currently faced by users.
*   Event setup and sales tracking interfaces must be streamlined and intuitive so organizers do not lose time to inefficient operations.
*   Pricing information must be clear, transparent, and easy to read for the buyer during the purchasing experience.
*   Routing must be handled smoothly using Tanstack Router to ensure intuitive navigation.

### Basic security expectations
*   The platform must facilitate a secure purchasing environment for tickets.
*   The generated digital tickets (QR/barcode) must be trustworthy, unique, and reliably verifiable at the point of entry to eliminate attendee uncertainty and prevent fraud.
*   The frontend architecture must be robust, utilizing the mandated ReactJS/TypeScript framework to ensure type safety and minimize runtime errors.

# Functional requirements for unregistered and registered users/customers: 

//How it works for a customer or user.

1. Access platform and discover events

   - the system must allow any user/customer to open the web application in any modern browsers like  "microsoft edge" without login to browse events.

2. search, filter and sort events on search bar

   - the system must allow user/customer to search and filter events to discover relevant events    quickly

3. view events details

   - when user/customer clicks or open an event card, the system must display full events details

4. Authentication to book ticket

   - the system shall require login/sign-up before ticket booking to secure purchase

5. book and purchase events ticket

   - all authenticated customer shall be able to book and purchase a ticket

6. generate and display digital ticket with QR code 

   - upon successful booking, the system shall  generate and display digital ticket in detail.

7. save and download ticket

   - the system shall allow customer to save tickets for offline use.

8. ticket and booking history

   - logged-in customer shall be able to view all purchase tickets in one place

FILE: docs/ticket-booking-payment-flows.md

# Ticket Types, Booking & Payments

This document covers the purchase and payment flow for a customer, plus the
organizer controls that determine what can be bought. It is the diagram set for
requirements `FR-09` to `FR-14`, `FR-32`, `FR-33`, `FR-35`, `FR-37`, `NFR-06`
and `NFR-09`.

## Preconditions set by the organizer

- The organizer creates publishes an event with a maximum capacity (`FR-33`).
- The organizer sets or updates the price of each ticket type (`FR-32`).
- A ticket type is only purchasable while it is active, inside its sales window,
  and while it has remaining quantity.
- The backend is the source of truth for stock. A ticket is claimed only after
  the payment has been confirmed and verified; the application does not issue a
  ticket, or reduce availability, before that point (`NFR-09`).

## Sequence diagram: buy a ticket, including rollback on payment failure

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber
    actor Customer
    participant UI as Checkout UI
    participant Pay as PayChangu
    participant PayAPI as Payment adapter
    participant TicketAPI as Ticket backend
    participant DB as Backend database

    Customer->>UI: Open event and choose ticket type and quantity
    UI->>TicketAPI: GET /events/:eventId/ticket-types
    TicketAPI-->>UI: Active ticket types, price, quantity and remaining
    UI->>UI: Check remaining quantity (FR-09, FR-13)
    alt Not enough tickets remaining
        UI-->>Customer: Show "sold out" / reduce quantity, block purchase
    else Tickets available
        UI-->>Customer: Show ticket price subtotal, service fee and total (FR-10, NFR-06)
        Customer->>UI: Confirm order (FR-11)
        UI->>UI: Save pending payment with txRef, user, event, ticket type and quantity
        UI->>Pay: Start PayChangu checkout
        Pay-->>Customer: Payment prompt / checkout page
        Customer->>Pay: Pay or cancel

        alt Payment fails, is cancelled, or remains unconfirmed
            Pay-->>UI: Return with failed/pending status
            UI->>PayAPI: Verify payment by txRef
            PayAPI-->>UI: failed / pending
            UI->>UI: Cancel the pending order; do not call ticket issuance
            UI-->>Customer: Show failure, no ticket issued, availability unchanged (NFR-09)
        else Payment succeeds
            Pay-->>UI: Return with success status and txRef
            UI->>PayAPI: Verify payment by txRef
            PayAPI->>Pay: Verify amount, reference and currency
            Pay-->>PayAPI: Confirmed payment details
            alt Reference, currency or amount does not match the order
                PayAPI-->>UI: Reject confirmation
                UI-->>Customer: Show support message; no ticket issued (NFR-09)
            else Verified payment
                loop Once per seat in the order
                    UI->>TicketAPI: POST /tickets/purchase { eventId, ticketTypeId }
                    TicketAPI->>DB: Lock ticket type row and find an unassigned ISSUED ticket
                    alt No ticket available or sales are closed
                        DB-->>TicketAPI: No stock
                        TicketAPI-->>UI: 400 sold out / sales closed
                        UI->>UI: Stop claiming more seats
                    else Ticket available
                        TicketAPI->>DB: Assign ticket to customer and increment quantity_sold (FR-14)
                        TicketAPI-->>UI: ticketNumber + QR code containing qrToken
                        UI->>UI: Save issued ticket locally for confirmation and My Tickets
                    end
                end
                alt All seats issued
                    UI-->>Customer: Confirm purchase and show digital ticket + QR (FR-12)
                    TicketAPI->>DB: Sales and attendance data updated (FR-35, FR-37)
                else Some seats issued
                    UI-->>Customer: Show purchased tickets and list the seats that failed
                    Note over UI,Customer: Payment succeeded, so support/refund is needed for the missing seats.
                else No seats issued
                    UI-->>Customer: Show failure and support message; no ticket was issued
                end
            end
        end
    end
```

## Activity diagram: payment success versus failure, all branches

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A([Organizer sets ticket price and event capacity]) --> B[Customer selects an active ticket type and quantity]
    B --> C{Enough tickets remaining?}
    C -- No --> C1[Block the purchase and show sold out / lower quantity]
    C1 --> Z([End: no order, no ticket issued])
    C -- Yes --> D[Show price before confirming including service fee]
    D --> E{Customer confirms the order?}
    E -- No --> Z
    E -- Yes --> F[Create pending order and start PayChangu payment]

    F --> G{Payment status from PayChangu}
    G -- Failed or cancelled --> H[Verify payment]
    G -- Pending or processing --> H
    H --> H1[No ticket claim is attempted]
    H1 --> H2[Cancel or release the pending order]
    H2 --> H3[Show failure / retry message]
    H3 --> Z

    G -- Success --> I[Verify txRef, amount, currency and order match]
    I --> J{Confirmation valid?}
    J -- No --> J1[Do not issue a ticket]
    J1 --> J2[Show support message for the charged payment]
    J2 --> Z

    J -- Yes --> K[Claim one backend ticket per seat]
    K --> L{Were all seats claimed?}
    L -- No, none --> L1[No ticket issued]
    L1 --> L2[Show failure and support/refund message]
    L2 --> Z

    L -- No, some --> M1[Keep the tickets that were issued]
    M1 --> M2[Show which seats were bought and which failed]
    M2 --> M3[Offer retry for the missing seats or contact support]
    M3 --> Z

    L -- Yes --> N[Persist the issued tickets and show the QR confirmation]
    N --> O[Availability and sales totals reflect the purchase]
    O --> P[Organizer can view tickets sold, performance and revenue]
    P --> Z
```

## Requirement traceability

| Requirement | Where it is covered |
| --- | --- |
| `FR-09` Select an available ticket | Customer chooses an active ticket type with remaining stock; the backend re-checks stock when claiming. |
| `FR-10` Show price before confirming | Price, quantity, service fee and total are shown before the customer confirms. |
| `FR-11` Purchase a ticket | Confirmed, verified payment triggers one backend claim per seat. |
| `FR-12` Confirm successful purchase | The confirmation page shows issued tickets and their QR images. |
| `FR-13` Block purchase at capacity | The remaining-quantity check blocks the purchase before payment; the backend also refuses a claim when no ticket is available. |
| `FR-14` Update availability after purchase | The backend increments `quantity_sold` when it claims a ticket; the UI refreshes events and tickets after success. |
| `FR-32` Organizer sets/updates ticket prices | Organizer ticket-type management supplies the price used throughout checkout. |
| `FR-33` Organizer sets max tickets for event | Event capacity and ticket-type quantity limit what can be claimed. |
| `FR-35` Organizer views tickets sold | Issued tickets and ticket-type sales are visible in organizer reporting. |
| `FR-37` Organizer sees performance and revenue | Confirmed purchases feed the organizer sales and revenue views. |
| `NFR-06` Price on selection and confirmation | Both the checkout selection screen and the confirmation flow display the amount. |
| `NFR-09` Never oversell; ticket only after confirmed purchase | No ticket is claimed before payment verification; the backend locks stock during the claim and refuses exhausted ticket types. |

#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Run from the frontend root. Restores the organizer dashboard look, and lets the organizer
# view and create staff from the Staff Roster tab (saved in the backend).
set -e
B=$(ls -d .connect-backup/*/ | sort | tail -1)
echo "restoring from $B"
[ -f "${B}src/features/organizer/pages/OrganizerDashboardPage.tsx" ] && cp "${B}src/features/organizer/pages/OrganizerDashboardPage.tsx" src/features/organizer/pages/OrganizerDashboardPage.tsx
CSS=$(cd "$B" && find . -name organiser.css | head -1)
[ -n "$CSS" ] && cp "$B${CSS#./}" "${CSS#./}"
mkdir -p src/features/organizer/components
cat > src/features/organizer/components/StaffRoster.tsx <<'TSX'
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { createMyStaff, listMyStaff } from '../../../lib/backendUsers'

const emptyForm = { name: '', email: '', password: '' }

// The organizer's own staff: listed from the backend, and new staff accounts are created there.
export function StaffRoster() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { data = [], isLoading, error } = useQuery({ queryKey: ['my-staff'], queryFn: listMyStaff })
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

  async function create(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setFormError(null)
    setNotice(null)
    if (!form.name.trim()) return setFormError('Enter the staff member’s name.')
    if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(form.email.trim())) return setFormError('Enter a valid email address.')
    if (form.password.length < 8) return setFormError('The password needs at least 8 characters.')
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await createMyStaff(form)
      setForm(emptyForm)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-staff'] })
      setNotice('Staff account created.')
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not create the account.')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <form onSubmit={create} noValidate style={{ marginBottom: '1.5rem' }}>
        <h3>Add a staff member</h3>
        <div className="form-row">
          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="staff-name">Name</label>
            <input id="staff-name" value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="staff-email">Email</label>
            <input id="staff-email" type="email" value={form.email} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
          <div className="form-group">
            <label htmlFor="staff-password">Password</label>
            <input id="staff-password" type="password" value={form.password} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })} />
          </div>
        </div>
        {formError && <div className="form-error-banner">{formError}</div>}
        {notice && <p className="form-hint">{notice}</p>}
        <div className="form-actions">
          <button type="submit" className="btn-primary-header" disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? 'Creating...' : 'Create staff account'}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>

      {isLoading && <p className="view-subtitle">Loading your staff...</p>}
      {error && <div className="form-error-banner">{error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not load staff.'}</div>}
      {!isLoading && !error && data.length === 0 && (
        <p className="view-subtitle">You have no staff yet. Add one above.</p>
      )}
      {data.length > 0 && (
        <div className="table-responsive">
          <table className="dashboard-table">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th>Name</th>
                <th>Email</th>
                <th>Events</th>
                <th>Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {data.map((member) => (
                <tr key={member.id}>
                  <td>{member.name}</td>
                  <td>{member.email}</td>
                  <td>{member.assignedEventIds.length}</td>
                  <td>
                    <span className={`status-badge ${member.isActive ? 'published' : 'cancelled'}`}>
                      {member.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
      )}
      <p className="form-hint">
        To choose which events a staff member may scan, switch them off or remove them, open{' '}
        <Link to="/organizer/staff">staff management</Link>.
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

cat > src/features/organizer/pages/StaffManagementPage.tsx <<'TSX'
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useAllEvents } from '../../events/hooks/useEvents'
import {
  assignStaffEvents, createMyStaff, listMyStaff, removeStaff, setStaffActive,
  type StaffMember,
} from '../../../lib/backendUsers'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../../../components/ConfirmDialog'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'

const field = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'
const emptyForm = { name: '', email: '', password: '' }

// An organizer creates staff accounts, chooses which events each one may scan,
// and switches them off or removes them. Everything is saved in the backend.
export function StaffManagementPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const { data: staff = [], isLoading, error: loadError } = useQuery({ queryKey: ['my-staff'], queryFn: listMyStaff })
  const { data: events = [] } = useAllEvents()
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [removing, setRemoving] = useState<StaffMember | null>(null)

  const refresh = () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-staff'] })

  async function run(action: () => Promise<unknown>, success: string) {
    try {
      await action()
      await refresh()
      showToast(success, 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Something went wrong', 'error')
    }
  }

  async function create(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setFormError(null)
    if (!form.name.trim()) return setFormError('Enter the staff member’s name.')
    if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(form.email.trim())) return setFormError('Enter a valid email address.')
    if (form.password.length < 8) return setFormError('The password needs at least 8 characters.')
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await createMyStaff(form)
      setForm(emptyForm)
      await refresh()
      showToast('Staff account created', 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not create the account.')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  function toggleEvent(member: StaffMember, eventId: string) {
    const next = member.assignedEventIds.includes(eventId)
      ? member.assignedEventIds.filter((id) => id !== eventId)
      : [...member.assignedEventIds, eventId]
    void run(() => assignStaffEvents(member.id, next), 'Assignments saved')
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-4xl px-4 py-20 sm:px-6">
      <Link to="/organizer/dashboard" className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">
        ← Organizer dashboard
      </Link>
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">Staff</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Staff scan tickets at the door. They can only scan the events you assign to them.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={create} className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm" noValidate>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-navy-dark">Add a staff member</h2>
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Name
            <input className={field} value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Email
            <input type="email" className={field} value={form.email} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Temporary password
            <input type="password" className={field} value={form.password} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })} placeholder="At least 8 characters" />
          </label>
        </div>
        {formError && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-600">{formError}</p>}
        <button type="submit" disabled={saving} className="mt-4 rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark disabled:opacity-60">
          {saving ? 'Creating…' : 'Create staff account'}
        </button>
      </form>

      <section className="mt-6 space-y-4" aria-label="Your staff">
        {isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Loading staff…</p>}
        {loadError && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">{loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load staff.'}</p>}
        {!isLoading && !loadError && staff.length === 0 && (
          <p className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">You have no staff yet.</p>
        )}
        {staff.map((member) => (
          <article key={member.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-navy-dark">{member.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{member.email}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${member.isActive ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                  {member.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => void run(() => setStaffActive(member.id, !member.isActive), member.isActive ? 'Staff disabled' : 'Staff enabled')}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50"
                >{member.isActive ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setRemoving(member)}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700"
                >Remove</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <fieldset className="mt-4" disabled={!member.isActive}>
              <legend className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-slate-500">Events they can scan</legend>
              {events.length === 0 && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Create an event first.</p>}
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {events.map((event) => (
                  <label key={event.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={member.assignedEventIds.includes(String(event.id))}
                      onChange={() => toggleEvent(member, String(event.id))}
                    />
                    {event.name}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
          </article>
        ))}
      </section>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!removing}
        onClose={() => setRemoving(null)}
        onConfirm={() => { if (removing) void run(() => removeStaff(removing.id), 'Staff removed') }}
        title={removing ? `Remove ${removing.name}?` : 'Remove staff?'}
        description="Their login is switched off and they can no longer scan tickets."
        confirmLabel="Remove"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </main>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/ConfirmDialog.tsx
================================================================================
import { Modal } from './Modal'

type ConfirmDialogProps = {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  onConfirm: () => void
  title: string
  description: string
  confirmLabel?: string
  cancelLabel?: string
  variant?: 'default' | 'danger'
}

export function ConfirmDialog({
  open,
  onClose,
  onConfirm,
  title,
  description,
  confirmLabel = 'Confirm',
  cancelLabel = 'Cancel',
  variant = 'default',
}: ConfirmDialogProps) {
  function handleConfirm() {
    onConfirm()
    onClose()
  }

  const confirmClasses =
    variant === 'danger'
      ? 'rounded-xl bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-500'
      : 'rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-navy'

  const cancelClasses =
    'rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-50 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-slate-400'

  const footer = (
    <>
      <button type="button" onClick={onClose} className={cancelClasses}>
        {cancelLabel}
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={handleConfirm} className={confirmClasses}>
        {confirmLabel}
      </button>
    </>
  )

  return (
    <Modal open={open} onClose={onClose} title={title} description={description} footer={footer} />
  )
}

FILE: src/components/Header.tsx

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useLocation } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { Menu, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { headerLinks } from '../routes/links'
import SiteLink from './SiteLink'
import { useAuth } from '../features/authentication/useAuth'

export default function Header() {
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false)
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false)
  const location = useLocation()

  // Close menu on route change
  useEffect(() => {
    setMenuOpen(false)
  }, [location.pathname])

  useEffect(() => {
    const onScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 10)
    window.addEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', onScroll)
  }, [])

  // Close menu on Escape
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setMenuOpen(false)
    }
    if (menuOpen) document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    return () => document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
  }, [menuOpen])

  const { user, logout } = useAuth()
  const isAdmin = user?.role === 'admin'
  // Admins don't see the Events or Bookings links; everyone else sees the full menu.
  const visibleLinks = isAdmin ? headerLinks.filter((item) => !/event|booking/i.test(item.label)) : headerLinks
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const closeMenu = () => setMenuOpen(false)
  const handleLogout = () => {
    logout()
    setMenuOpen(false)
    navigate({ to: '/' })
  }

  return (
    <header className={`fixed inset-x-0 top-0 z-50 bg-white ${scrolled ? 'shadow-md' : 'border-b border-slate-100'}`}>
      <div className="mx-auto flex h-16 max-w-7xl items-center justify-between px-4 sm:px-6">
        <Link to="/" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-lg font-extrabold text-amber-300">
          Tic-EM
        </Link>

        {/* Desktop Nav */}
        <nav aria-label="Main navigation" className="hidden items-center gap-6 lg:flex xl:gap-8">
          {visibleLinks.map((item) => (
            <SiteLink key={item.label} item={item} className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy" />
          ))}
          {user ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-l border-slate-200 pl-5">
              {user.role === 'customer' && <Link to="/tickets" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">My Tickets</Link>}
              {user.role === 'organizer' && <Link to="/organizer/dashboard" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">Dashboard</Link>}
              {user.role === 'staff' && <Link to="/scan" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">Scan</Link>}
              {user.role === 'admin' && <Link to="/admin" className="text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 font-bold">Admin Panel</Link>}
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="grid size-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-50 text-xs font-bold uppercase text-navy">
                  {user.name.trim().charAt(0)}
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{user.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-medium capitalize text-slate-500">{user.role}</span>
                </span>
              </div>
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="rounded-full border px-4 py-1.5 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Logout</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Link to="/login" className="rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">Login</Link>
          )}
        </nav>

        {/* Mobile Toggle */}
        <button 
          aria-label={menuOpen ? 'Close menu' : 'Open menu'}
          aria-expanded={menuOpen}
          aria-controls="mobile-menu"
          onClick={() => setMenuOpen(!menuOpen)} 
          className="text-navy lg:hidden"
        >
          {menuOpen ? <X /> : <Menu />}
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Mobile Nav */}
      {menuOpen && (
        <nav id="mobile-menu" aria-label="Mobile navigation" className="flex flex-col border-t bg-white px-4 pb-4 lg:hidden">
          {visibleLinks.map((item) => (
            <SiteLink key={item.label} item={item} onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-3 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50" />
          ))}
          {user ? (
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 pt-2">
              {user.role === 'customer' && <Link to="/tickets" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50">My Tickets</Link>}
              {user.role === 'organizer' && <Link to="/organizer/dashboard" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50">Dashboard</Link>}
              {user.role === 'staff' && <Link to="/scan" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium hover:bg-navy-50">Scan</Link>}
              {user.role === 'admin' && <Link to="/admin" onClick={closeMenu} className="rounded-lg px-3 py-2 font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50">Admin Panel</Link>}
              <div className="mt-1 flex items-center gap-2.5 border-t border-slate-100 px-3 pt-3">
                <span aria-hidden="true" className="grid size-8 shrink-0 place-items-center rounded-full bg-navy-50 text-xs font-bold uppercase text-navy">
                  {user.name.trim().charAt(0)}
                </span>
                <span className="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                  <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-700">{user.name}</span>
                  <span className="text-[11px] font-medium capitalize text-slate-500">{user.role}</span>
                </span>
              </div>
              <button onClick={handleLogout} className="mt-2 rounded-full border py-2 text-center font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Logout</button>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <Link to="/login" onClick={closeMenu} className="mt-2 rounded-full bg-navy py-3 text-center font-semibold text-white">Login</Link>
          )}
        </nav>
      )}
    </header>
  )
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/Modal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode } from 'react'

type ModalProps = {
  open: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  title: string
  description?: string
  children?: ReactNode
  footer?: ReactNode
}

export function Modal({ open, onClose, title, description, children, footer }: ModalProps) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const closeButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  const lastFocusedRef = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return

    lastFocusedRef.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'

    // Focus first focusable element or close button
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (closeButtonRef.current) {
        closeButtonRef.current.focus()
      } else if (dialogRef.current) {
        const focusable = dialogRef.current.querySelector<HTMLElement>(
          'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
        )
        focusable?.focus()
      }
    }, 0)

    function handleKeyDown(e: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') {
        e.preventDefault()
        onClose()
        return
      }
      if (e.key === 'Tab' && dialogRef.current) {
        const focusableElements = Array.from(
          dialogRef.current.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
            'button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
          ),
        ).filter((el) => !el.hasAttribute('disabled') && el.getAttribute('aria-hidden') !== 'true')

        if (focusableElements.length === 0) return

        const first = focusableElements[0]
        const last = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1]
        const active = document.activeElement as HTMLElement

        if (e.shiftKey) {
          if (active === first || !dialogRef.current.contains(active)) {
            e.preventDefault()
            last.focus()
          }
        } else {
          if (active === last || !dialogRef.current.contains(active)) {
            e.preventDefault()
            first.focus()
          }
        }
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
      document.body.style.overflow = ''
      lastFocusedRef.current?.focus()
    }
  }, [open, onClose])

  if (!open) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4" role="presentation">
      <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50" onClick={onClose} aria-hidden="true" />
      <div
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        aria-describedby={description ? 'modal-description' : undefined}
        className="relative w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-xl"
      >
        <div className="mb-4">
          <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">
            {title}
          </h2>
          {description && (
            <p id="modal-description" className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
              {description}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
        {children}
        {footer && <div className="mt-6 flex flex-col-reverse gap-3 sm:flex-row sm:justify-end">{footer}</div>}
        <button
          ref={closeButtonRef}
          type="button"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Close dialog"
          className="absolute right-4 top-4 rounded-full p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-600"
        >
          ×
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/components/QrCode.tsx

interface QrCodeProps {
  value: string
  size?: number
  dataUrl?: string
}

// The QR image is issued by the backend, which keeps the secret behind it.
export function QrCode({ value, size = 170, dataUrl }: QrCodeProps) {
  return (
    <div className="qr" role="img" aria-label={`QR code for ${value}`} style={{ width: size + 18 }}>
      {dataUrl ? (
        <img
          src={dataUrl}
          width={size}
          height={size}
          alt=""
          style={{ width: size, height: size, objectFit: 'contain' }}
        />
      ) : (
        <p style={{ fontSize: 12, textAlign: 'center' }}>QR code unavailable</p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/Reveal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef, type ReactNode, type CSSProperties } from 'react'

interface RevealProps {
  children: ReactNode
  delay?: number
  className?: string
}

export default function Reveal({ children, delay = 0, className = '' }: RevealProps) {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = ref.current
    if (!el) return
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (entry.isIntersecting) {
          el.classList.add('is-visible')
          observer.disconnect()
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.12 },
    )
    observer.observe(el)
    return () => observer.disconnect()
  }, [])

  const style = { '--d': `${delay}ms` } as CSSProperties

  return (
    <div ref={ref} style={style} className={`reveal ${className}`}>
      {children}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/SiteLink.tsx
================================================================================
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import type { NavLink } from '../routes/links'

interface SiteLinkProps {
  item: NavLink
  className?: string
  onClick?: () => void
}

export default function SiteLink({ item, className, onClick }: SiteLinkProps) {
  if (item.slug) {
    return (
      <a href={`#${item.slug}`} className={className} onClick={onClick}>
        {item.label}
      </a>
    )
  }
  if (item.to) {
    return (
      <Link to={item.to} className={className} onClick={onClick}>
        {item.label}
      </Link>
    )
  }
  return (
    <a href={item.href} className={className} onClick={onClick}>
      {item.label}
    </a>
  )
}
