import type { Role } from '../features/authentication/authStore'

// Which navigation links each kind of visitor may see (issue #89).
// The header asks this once, so desktop and mobile menus always agree.
export interface NavAccess {
  publicLinks: boolean // Home / Events / site links
  bookings: boolean
  tickets: boolean
  dashboard: boolean
  staffManagement: boolean
  scan: boolean
  admin: boolean
}

export function navAccess(role: Role | undefined): NavAccess {
  return {
    publicLinks: role === undefined || role === 'customer',
    bookings: role === 'customer',
    tickets: role === 'customer',
    dashboard: role === 'organizer',
    staffManagement: role === 'organizer',
    scan: role === 'staff',
    admin: role === 'admin',
  }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/components/ui/Modal.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { createPortal } from 'react-dom'
import { X } from 'lucide-react'

interface ModalProps {
  isOpen: boolean
  onClose: () => void
  title: string
  children: React.ReactNode
}

export function Modal({ isOpen, onClose, title, children }: ModalProps) {
  const modalRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null)
  const previousFocus = useRef<HTMLElement | null>(null)
  // keep the latest onClose without re-running the focus effect on every render
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose)
  useEffect(() => {
    onCloseRef.current = onClose
  })

  // Handle Escape key & Body Scroll Lock
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOpen) return

    const handleKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') onCloseRef.current()
    }
    
    // Save current focus and lock scroll
    previousFocus.current = document.activeElement as HTMLElement
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden'
    document.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
    
    // Move focus into the modal
    setTimeout(() => {
      const focusable = modalRef.current?.querySelectorAll('button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')
      if (focusable && focusable.length > 0) {
        (focusable[0] as HTMLElement).focus()
      }
    }, 10)

    return () => {
      document.body.style.overflow = ''
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeyDown)
      // Restore focus to where it was
      if (previousFocus.current) previousFocus.current.focus()
    }
  }, [isOpen])

  // Trap focus inside the modal
  const handleTabKey = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Tab' || !modalRef.current) return
    
    const focusableElements = modalRef.current.querySelectorAll('button, [href], input, select, textarea, [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')
    if (focusableElements.length === 0) return
    
    const first = focusableElements[0] as HTMLElement
    const last = focusableElements[focusableElements.length - 1] as HTMLElement

    if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
      e.preventDefault()
      last.focus()
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
      e.preventDefault()
      first.focus()
    }
  }

  if (!isOpen) return null

  return createPortal(
    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4">
      <div 
        ref={modalRef}
        role="dialog"
        aria-modal="true"
        aria-labelledby="modal-title"
        onKeyDown={handleTabKey}
        className="w-full max-w-md rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-xl flex flex-col max-h-[90vh]"
      >
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b px-6 py-4">
          <h2 id="modal-title" className="text-lg font-bold text-navy-dark">{title}</h2>
          <button onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close modal" className="rounded-full p-2 hover:bg-slate-100">
            <X size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>
        <div className="overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {children}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>,
    document.body
  )
}

FILE: src/features/admin/components/AdminEventModeration.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { useMutation, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'
import { updateAdminEventStatus, type AdminEventStatus } from '../../../lib/backendAdmin'

const fieldClass = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'

export function AdminEventModeration() {
  const [eventId, setEventId] = useState('')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AdminEventStatus>('CANCELLED')
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const mutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => updateAdminEventStatus(eventId.trim(), status),
    onSuccess: async () => {
      await Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['events'] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['organizer-events'] }),
      ])
      showToast('Event status updated', 'success')
    },
    onError: (error) => showToast(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not update event status', 'error'),
  })

  function submit(event: FormEvent) {
    event.preventDefault()
    if (!eventId.trim()) {
      showToast('Enter the backend event ID.', 'error')
      return
    }
    mutation.mutate()
  }

  return (
    <section className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">Event moderation</h2>
      <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Set an event status using the backend event ID. The backend authorizes this admin operation.
      </p>
      <form onSubmit={submit} className="mt-4 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_200px_auto] sm:items-end">
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
          Event ID
          <input
            aria-label="Event ID"
            className={fieldClass}
            value={eventId}
            onChange={(event) => setEventId(event.target.value)}
            required
          />
        </label>
        <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-slate-700">
          New status
          <select
            aria-label="New event status"
            className={fieldClass}
            value={status}
            onChange={(event) => setStatus(event.target.value as AdminEventStatus)}
          >
            <option value="DRAFT">Draft</option>
            <option value="PUBLISHED">Published</option>
            <option value="CANCELLED">Cancelled</option>
          </select>
        </label>
        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={mutation.isPending}
          className="rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {mutation.isPending ? 'Saving…' : 'Update status'}
        </button>
      </form>
    </section>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/admin/components/UserManagement.tsx

import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Search, UserPlus, Power, Trash2, Eye } from 'lucide-react'
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import type { AccountStatus, Role } from '../../authentication/authStore'
import {
  createUser, deleteUser, fetchOrganizerKyc, listUsers, setUserActive,
  type AdminUser,
} from '../../../lib/backendUsers'
import { Modal } from '../../../components/ui/Modal'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../../../components/ConfirmDialog'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'

const PAGE_SIZE = 8
const ROLES: Role[] = ['customer', 'organizer', 'staff', 'admin']
const field = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'
const emptyForm = { name: '', email: '', password: '', role: 'customer' as Role, organizerId: '', actorPassword: '' }

const date = (iso: string) => new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })

function Activity({ target }: { target: AdminUser }) {
  const { data: kyc } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['organizer-kyc', target.id],
    queryFn: () => fetchOrganizerKyc(target.id),
    enabled: target.role === 'organizer',
  })
  if (target.role !== 'organizer') return null
  return (
    <dl className="mt-4 grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 text-sm">
      <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2">
        <dt className="text-xs text-slate-500">Business</dt>
        <dd className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{kyc?.businessName ?? 'Not submitted'}</dd>
      </div>
      <div className="rounded-lg bg-slate-50 px-3 py-2">
        <dt className="text-xs text-slate-500">Verification</dt>
        <dd className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{kyc?.status ?? 'Unverified'}</dd>
      </div>
    </dl>
  )
}

export function UserManagement() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')
  const [role, setRole] = useState<Role | 'all'>('all')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AccountStatus | 'all'>('all')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const [viewing, setViewing] = useState<AdminUser | null>(null)
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState<AdminUser | null>(null)
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false)
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const { data: everyone = [], isLoading, error: loadError } = useQuery({ queryKey: ['admin-users'], queryFn: listUsers })
  const organizers = everyone.filter((u) => u.role === 'organizer')

  const q = search.trim().toLowerCase()
  const filtered = everyone
    .filter((u) => (role === 'all' || u.role === role) && (status === 'all' || u.status === status))
    .filter((u) => !q || u.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) || u.email.toLowerCase().includes(q))
    .sort((a, b) => b.createdAt.localeCompare(a.createdAt))

  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(filtered.length / PAGE_SIZE))
  const current = Math.min(page, pages)
  const visible = filtered.slice((current - 1) * PAGE_SIZE, current * PAGE_SIZE)

  if (!user) return null

  async function run(action: () => Promise<unknown>, success: string) {
    try {
      await action()
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin-users'] })
      showToast(success, 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Something went wrong', 'error')
    }
  }

  async function create(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setError(null)
    try {
      await createUser({ ...form, organizerId: form.organizerId || undefined })
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['admin-users'] })
      setCreating(false)
      setForm(emptyForm)
      showToast('Account created', 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not create the account')
    }
  }

  const filterClass = 'rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'

  return (
    <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">Users ({filtered.length})</h2>
        <button onClick={() => { setError(null); setCreating(true) }} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">
          <UserPlus size={16} /> Create user
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-3">
        <label className="relative min-w-56 flex-1">
          <Search size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-slate-400" />
          <input aria-label="Search users" value={search} onChange={(e) => { setSearch(e.target.value); setPage(1) }} placeholder="Search name or email" className={`${filterClass} w-full pl-9`} />
        </label>
        <select aria-label="Filter by role" value={role} onChange={(e) => { setRole(e.target.value as Role | 'all'); setPage(1) }} className={filterClass}>
          <option value="all">All roles</option>
          {ROLES.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r} className="capitalize">{r}</option>)}
        </select>
        <select aria-label="Filter by status" value={status} onChange={(e) => { setStatus(e.target.value as AccountStatus | 'all'); setPage(1) }} className={filterClass}>
          <option value="all">All statuses</option>
          <option value="active">Active</option>
          <option value="disabled">Disabled</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-slate-600">
          <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-xs uppercase text-slate-500">
            <tr>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Name</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Role</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Status</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3">Registered</th>
              <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right">Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {isLoading && (
              <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading users…</td></tr>
            )}
            {loadError && (
              <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-red-600">{loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load users.'}</td></tr>
            )}
            {!isLoading && !loadError && visible.length === 0 && (
              <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">No users match your search.</td></tr>
            )}
            {visible.map((u) => (
              <tr key={u.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0 hover:bg-slate-50">
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="font-semibold text-navy-dark">{u.name}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs">{u.email}</div>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 capitalize">{u.role}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${u.status === 'active' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                    {u.status === 'active' ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                  </span>
                </td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">{date(u.createdAt)}</td>
                <td className="px-4 py-3">
                  <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-end gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => setViewing(u)} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50"><Eye size={14} /> View</button>
                    <button
                      onClick={() => void run(() => setUserActive(u.id, u.status !== 'active'), u.status === 'active' ? 'Account disabled' : 'Account enabled')}
                      className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50"
                    ><Power size={14} /> {u.status === 'active' ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</button>
                    <button onClick={() => setDeleting(u)} className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700"><Trash2 size={14} /> Delete</button>
                  </div>
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-4 flex items-center justify-between text-sm text-slate-600">
        <span>Page {current} of {pages}</span>
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <button disabled={current <= 1} onClick={() => setPage(current - 1)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">Previous</button>
          <button disabled={current >= pages} onClick={() => setPage(current + 1)} className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 font-semibold disabled:opacity-40">Next</button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <Modal isOpen={!!viewing} onClose={() => setViewing(null)} title={viewing?.name ?? ''}>
        {viewing && (
          <div className="text-sm text-slate-600">
            <p>{viewing.email}</p>
            <p className="mt-1 capitalize">{viewing.role} · {viewing.status} · registered {date(viewing.createdAt)}</p>
            <Activity target={viewing} />
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>

      <Modal isOpen={creating} onClose={() => setCreating(false)} title="Create user">
        <form onSubmit={create} className="space-y-3">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Full name
            <input className={field} value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Email
            <input type="email" className={field} value={form.email} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Temporary password
            <input type="password" className={field} value={form.password} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })} placeholder="At least 8 characters" />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Role
            <select className={field} value={form.role} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, role: e.target.value as Role })}>
              {ROLES.map((r) => <option key={r} value={r} className="capitalize">{r}</option>)}
            </select>
          </label>
          {form.role === 'staff' && (
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Organizer
              <select className={field} value={form.organizerId} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, organizerId: e.target.value })}>
                <option value="">Choose an organizer</option>
                {organizers.map((o) => <option key={o.id} value={o.id}>{o.name}</option>)}
              </select>
            </label>
          )}
          {form.role === 'admin' && (
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Your password (to confirm)
              <input type="password" className={field} value={form.actorPassword} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, actorPassword: e.target.value })} />
              <span className="mt-1 block text-xs font-normal text-slate-500">Administrators can manage every account, so we ask for your password again.</span>
            </label>
          )}
          {error && <p role="alert" className="text-sm font-medium text-red-600">{error}</p>}
          <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="button" onClick={() => setCreating(false)} className="rounded-full border px-4 py-2 text-sm font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50">Cancel</button>
            <button type="submit" className="rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark">Create</button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </Modal>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!deleting}
        onClose={() => setDeleting(null)}
        onConfirm={() => { if (deleting) void run(() => deleteUser(deleting.id), 'Account deleted') }}
        title={deleting ? `Delete ${deleting.name}?` : 'Delete account?'}
        description="Their login will be switched off. Their events, tickets and orders stay on record."
        confirmLabel="Delete"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/admin/pages/AdminDashboardPage.tsx

import { useState } from 'react'
import { DollarSign, Ban, ShieldAlert, Ticket } from 'lucide-react'
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { fetchPublishedEvents } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { cancelOrganizerEvent } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../../../components/ConfirmDialog'
import { StatCard } from '../../organizer/components/StatCard'
import { UserManagement } from '../components/UserManagement'
import type { StoredEvent } from '../../../types/event'

type Tab = 'events' | 'users'

const tabClass = (active: boolean) =>
  `pb-3 font-semibold text-sm ${active ? 'border-b-2 border-navy text-navy' : 'text-slate-500 hover:text-slate-700'}`

export function AdminDashboardPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>('events')
  const [cancelling, setCancelling] = useState<StoredEvent | null>(null)

  const { data: events = [], isLoading, error } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['events', 'admin'],
    queryFn: fetchPublishedEvents,
    enabled: user?.role === 'admin',
  })

  if (user?.role !== 'admin') {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 pb-16 pt-20">
        <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 text-center">
          <ShieldAlert className="mx-auto mb-4 text-red-600" size={40} />
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">Access denied</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">
            This area is for administrators only. Sign in with an administrator account to continue.
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const ticketsSold = events.reduce((sum, event) => sum + event.ticketTypes.reduce((s, type) => s + type.sold, 0), 0)
  const revenue = events.reduce(
    (sum, event) => sum + event.ticketTypes.reduce((s, type) => s + type.sold * type.price, 0),
    0,
  )

  async function cancelEvent(event: StoredEvent) {
    try {
      await cancelOrganizerEvent(event.id)
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['events'] })
      showToast('Event cancelled', 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not cancel the event', 'error')
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-slate-50 pb-16 pt-20">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 sm:px-6">
        <div className="mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-extrabold sm:text-4xl text-navy-dark">Admin Control Center</h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">Moderate published events and manage user accounts.</p>
        </div>

        <div className="mb-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <StatCard title="Ticket revenue (published events)" value={`MK ${revenue.toLocaleString()}`} icon={<DollarSign />} />
          <StatCard title="Published events" value={events.length.toString()} icon={<ShieldAlert />} />
          <StatCard title="Tickets sold" value={ticketsSold.toLocaleString()} icon={<Ticket />} />
        </div>

        <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-x-6 gap-y-2 border-b border-slate-200">
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('events')} className={tabClass(activeTab === 'events')}>Event Moderation</button>
          <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('users')} className={tabClass(activeTab === 'users')}>User Management</button>
        </div>

        {activeTab === 'users' && <UserManagement />}

        {activeTab === 'events' && (
          <div className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-6 shadow-sm sm:p-8">
            <h2 className="mb-4 text-xl font-bold text-navy-dark">Event Moderation</h2>
            {error && (
              <p role="alert" className="mb-4 rounded-lg bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
                {error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not load events.'}
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-left text-sm text-slate-600">
                <thead className="bg-slate-50 text-xs uppercase text-slate-500">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="rounded-tl-lg px-4 py-3">Event Name</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Organizer</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Date</th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3">Sold</th>
                    <th className="rounded-tr-lg px-4 py-3 text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {isLoading && (
                    <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">Loading events…</td></tr>
                  )}
                  {!isLoading && events.length === 0 && (
                    <tr><td colSpan={5} className="px-4 py-8 text-center text-slate-500">No published events.</td></tr>
                  )}
                  {events.map((event) => {
                    const sold = event.ticketTypes.reduce((sum, type) => sum + type.sold, 0)
                    const capacity = event.ticketTypes.reduce((sum, type) => sum + type.capacity, 0)
                    return (
                      <tr key={event.id} className="border-b border-slate-100 last:border-0 hover:bg-slate-50">
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 font-semibold text-navy-dark">{event.title}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3">{event.organizer || '-'}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3">{event.date}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3">{sold} / {capacity}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                          <button
                            onClick={() => setCancelling(event)}
                            className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700"
                          >
                            <Ban size={14} /> Cancel event
                          </button>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    )
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!cancelling}
        onClose={() => setCancelling(null)}
        onConfirm={() => cancelling && void cancelEvent(cancelling)}
        title="Cancel this event?"
        description={`"${cancelling?.title ?? ''}" will be cancelled and removed from the public list. This cannot be undone.`}
        confirmLabel="Cancel event"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/authentication/AuthProvider.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { AuthContext } from "./useAuth";
import { getUser,loginRequest,logoutRequest,registerRequest,type User } from "./authStore";


export function AuthProvider({children}:{children: ReactNode}){
    const [user,setUser] = useState<User | null>(getUser);

    const login = async (email:string,password:string)=>{
        const next = await loginRequest(email,password);
        setUser(next);
        return next;
    };
    const register = async (name:string,email:string,password:string, role?: 'customer' | 'organizer') => {
        const next = await registerRequest(name,email,password,role);
        setUser(next);
        return next;
    };
    const logout = () => {
        logoutRequest();
        setUser(null);
    };
    return <AuthContext.Provider value={{user,login,register,logout}}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/authentication/authStore.ts
================================================================================
import { api, setToken } from '../../lib/backend';
import { syncKyc } from '../../lib/verification';

export type Role = 'customer' | 'organizer' | 'staff' | 'admin';
export type AccountStatus = 'active' | 'disabled';

export interface User {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    email: string;
    role: Role;
}

// Only the signed-in user is kept in the browser (so a refresh does not log them out).
// Every account, password and role lives in the backend database.
const SESSION_KEY = 'tm_user';

function readSession(): User | null {
    try {
        const raw = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
        return raw ? (JSON.parse(raw) as User) : null;
    } catch {
        return null;
    }
}

export function getUser(): User | null {
    return readSession();
}

// The backend names roles differently from this app.
type BackendRole = 'ATTENDEE' | 'ORGANIZER' | 'CHECK_IN_STAFF' | 'ADMIN';
const FROM_BACKEND: Record<BackendRole, Role> = {
    ATTENDEE: 'customer',
    ORGANIZER: 'organizer',
    CHECK_IN_STAFF: 'staff',
    ADMIN: 'admin',
};

interface AuthResponse {
    accessToken?: string;
    access_token?: string;
    token?: string;
    user?: { id: string; name?: string; email: string; role: BackendRole };
}

// Older backend builds return only the token, so the account details are read from it.
function decodeJwt(token: string): { sub?: string; email?: string; role?: BackendRole; name?: string } {
    try {
        const part = token.split('.')[1].replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/');
        return JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(part))));
    } catch {
        return {};
    }
}

async function postAuth(path: string, body: unknown): Promise<AuthResponse> {
    try {
        return await api<AuthResponse>(path, { method: 'POST', body });
    } catch (error) {
        const status = (error as { status?: number }).status;
        if (status === 401) throw new Error('Wrong email or password');
        if (status === 409) throw new Error('This account already exists');
        throw error;
    }
}

function startSession(result: AuthResponse, fallbackName = ''): User {
    const token = result.accessToken ?? result.access_token ?? result.token;
    if (!token) throw new Error('The backend did not return a sign-in token');
    const claims = decodeJwt(token);
    const account = result.user ?? { id: claims.sub ?? '', name: claims.name, email: claims.email ?? '', role: claims.role as BackendRole };
    if (!account.id || !account.role) throw new Error('The backend returned an incomplete sign-in response');
    const user: User = {
        id: account.id,
        name: account.name || fallbackName || account.email.split('@')[0],
        email: account.email,
        role: FROM_BACKEND[account.role] ?? 'customer',
    };
    localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(user));
    setToken(token);
    return user;
}

export async function loginRequest(email: string, password: string): Promise<User> {
    const user = startSession(await postAuth('/auth/login', { email: email.trim(), password }));
    // an organizer's saved business details decide whether the KYC page is shown
    if (user.role === 'organizer') await syncKyc(user.id).catch(() => null);
    return user;
}

export async function registerRequest(name: string, email: string, password: string, role: 'customer' | 'organizer' = 'customer'): Promise<User> {
    return startSession(await postAuth('/auth/register', {
        name: name.trim(),
        email: email.trim(),
        password,
        role: role === 'organizer' ? 'ORGANIZER' : 'ATTENDEE',
    }), name.trim());
}

export function logoutRequest(): void {
    localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
    setToken(null);
}

import { redirect } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { getUser, type Role } from './authStore';

export function roleForPath(path: string): Role | undefined {
    if (path.startsWith('/organizer')) return 'organizer';
    if (path.startsWith('/admin')) return 'admin';
    if (path.startsWith('/scan')) return 'staff';
    if (path.startsWith('/checkout') || path.startsWith('/tickets') || path.startsWith('/bookings')) return 'customer';
    return undefined;
}
export function canAccess(role: Role, path: string): boolean {
    const needed = roleForPath(path.split('?')[0]);
    return !needed || needed === role;
}

export function requireVerifiedOrganizer(): (ctx: { location: { href: string } }) => void {
    return requireAuth('organizer')
}

export function requireAuth(role?: Role): (ctx: { location: { href: string } }) => void {
    return ({ location }) => {
        const user = getUser();
        if (!user) throw redirect({ to: '/login', search: { redirect: location.href } });
        if (role && user.role !== role) throw redirect({ to: '/login', search: { redirect: location.href } });
    };
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/authentication/pages/LoginPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useRef, useState, type FormEvent, type RefObject } from 'react';
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import type { User } from '../authStore';
import { useAuth } from '../useAuth';
import { canAccess } from '../guards';
import { useToast } from '../../toast';
import styles from './LoginPage.module.css';

function homeForRole(role: User['role']): string {
  if (role === 'admin') return '/admin';
  if (role === 'organizer') return '/organizer/dashboard';
  if (role === 'staff') return '/scan';
  return '/events';
}

type Mode = 'login' | 'register';
type Field = 'name' | 'email' | 'password';
type FieldErrors = Partial<Record<Field, string>>;

// Order used to decide which invalid field receives focus first
const FIELD_ORDER: Field[] = ['name', 'email', 'password'];

interface Values {
  name: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
}

function validate(mode: Mode, v: Values): FieldErrors {
  const errors: FieldErrors = {};
  if (mode === 'register' && !v.name.trim()) errors.name = 'Enter your full name';
  if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(v.email)) errors.email = 'Enter a valid email address';
  if (v.password.length < 8) errors.password = 'Password must be at least 8 characters';
  return errors;
}

// Only allow redirects to pages inside this app
function safePath(path: string | undefined): string {
  return path && path.startsWith('/') && !path.startsWith('//') ? path : '/events';
}

export function LoginPage() {
  const { login, register } = useAuth();
  const { showToast } = useToast();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { redirect } = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { redirect?: string };

  const [mode, setMode] = useState<Mode>('login');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const organizerArea = !!redirect && redirect.startsWith('/organizer');
  const [accountType, setAccountType] = useState<'customer' | 'organizer'>(organizerArea ? 'organizer' : 'customer');
  const [showPassword, setShowPassword] = useState(false);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FieldErrors>({});

  const nameRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const emailRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const passwordRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const fieldRefs: Record<Field, RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>> = {
    name: nameRef,
    email: emailRef,
    password: passwordRef,
  };

  const goAfterLogin = (user: User) =>
    navigate({ to: redirect && canAccess(user.role, safePath(redirect)) ? safePath(redirect) : homeForRole(user.role) });

  const submit = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () => (mode === 'login' ? login(email, password) : register(name, email, password, accountType)),
    onSuccess: (user) => {
      if (mode === 'register') {
        showToast(`Account created — welcome, ${user.name}!`, 'success');
      }
      goAfterLogin(user);
    },
    onError: (error) => {
      if (mode === 'register') {
        showToast(error.message, 'error');
      }
    },
  });

  const onSubmit = (e: FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const found = validate(mode, { name, email, password });
    setErrors(found);

    const firstInvalid = FIELD_ORDER.find((field) => found[field]);
    if (firstInvalid) {
      fieldRefs[firstInvalid].current?.focus();
      return;
    }

    submit.mutate();
  };

  const clearAll = () => {
    setErrors({});
    setPassword('');
    submit.reset();
  };

  const switchMode = (next: Mode) => {
    setMode(next);
    clearAll();
  };

  // Update a field and clear that field's error as the user corrects it
  const change = (field: Field, value: string) => {
    if (field === 'name') setName(value);
    if (field === 'email') setEmail(value);
    if (field === 'password') setPassword(value);
    if (errors[field]) setErrors((prev) => ({ ...prev, [field]: undefined }));
  };

  // aria attributes linking an input to its own error message
  const a11y = (field: Field, id: string) => ({
    'aria-invalid': errors[field] ? (true as const) : undefined,
    'aria-describedby': errors[field] ? `${id}-error` : undefined,
  });
  const fieldError = (field: Field, id: string) =>
    errors[field] ? (
      <p id={`${id}-error`} className={styles.fieldError}>
        {errors[field]}
      </p>
    ) : null;

  // Server-side problems (e.g. wrong password) are announced as an alert
  const alertMessage = submit.error?.message ?? null;
  const isPending = submit.isPending;

  const title = mode === 'login' ? 'Welcome back' : 'Create your account';
  const subtitle = mode === 'login'
    ? 'Log in to buy tickets and see your bookings.'
    : 'It only takes a minute to get started.';
  const submitLabel = isPending
    ? 'Please wait…'
    : mode === 'login'
      ? 'Log in'
      : 'Create account';

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <aside className={styles.brand}>
        <div className={styles.logo}>
          <span className={styles.logoMark}></span>
          Ticket Master
        </div>

        <div>
          <h1 className={styles.headline}>Your next great night starts here.</h1>
          <p className={styles.tagline}>Discover events, buy tickets in minutes and walk in with a simple scan.</p>
          <ul className={styles.perks}>
            <li><span className={styles.perkIcon}>✓</span>Find events that match your taste</li>
            <li><span className={styles.perkIcon}>✓</span>Pay once and get your ticket instantly</li>
            <li><span className={styles.perkIcon}>✓</span>Show your QR code at the door</li>
          </ul>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <section className={styles.formSide}>
        <div className={styles.card}>
          <h2 className={styles.title}>{title}</h2>
          <p className={styles.subtitle}>{subtitle}</p>

          <div className={styles.tabs}>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === 'login'} onClick={() => switchMode('login')} className={`${styles.tab} ${mode === 'login' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
              Log in
            </button>
            <button type="button" aria-pressed={mode === 'register'} onClick={() => switchMode('register')} className={`${styles.tab} ${mode === 'register' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
              Register
            </button>
          </div>

          <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate>
            {mode === 'register' && (
              <>
                <div role="group" aria-label="Account type" className={styles.tabs}>
                  <button type="button" aria-pressed={accountType === 'customer'} onClick={() => setAccountType('customer')} className={`${styles.tab} ${accountType === 'customer' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
                    I want to buy tickets
                  </button>
                  <button type="button" aria-pressed={accountType === 'organizer'} onClick={() => setAccountType('organizer')} className={`${styles.tab} ${accountType === 'organizer' ? styles.active : ''}`}>
                    I'm an organizer
                  </button>
                </div>

                <label htmlFor="auth-name" className={styles.srOnly}>Full name</label>
                <input
                  id="auth-name"
                  ref={nameRef}
                  type="text"
                  name="name"
                  autoComplete="name"
                  aria-required="true"
                  placeholder="Full name"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(e) => change('name', e.target.value)}
                  className={styles.field}
                  {...a11y('name', 'auth-name')}
                />
                {fieldError('name', 'auth-name')}
              </>
            )}

            <>
              <label htmlFor="auth-email" className={styles.srOnly}>Email address</label>
              <input
                id="auth-email"
                ref={emailRef}
                type="email"
                name="email"
                autoComplete="email"
                aria-required="true"
                placeholder="Email address"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => change('email', e.target.value)}
                className={styles.field}
                {...a11y('email', 'auth-email')}
              />
              {fieldError('email', 'auth-email')}
            </>

            {
              <>
                <label htmlFor="auth-password" className={styles.srOnly}>
                  Password
                </label>
                <div className={styles.passwordRow}>
                  <input
                    id="auth-password"
                    ref={passwordRef}
                    type={showPassword ? 'text' : 'password'}
                    name="password"
                    autoComplete={mode === 'login' ? 'current-password' : 'new-password'}
                    aria-required="true"
                    placeholder="Password"
                    value={password}
                    onChange={(e) => change('password', e.target.value)}
                    className={styles.field}
                    {...a11y('password', 'auth-password')}
                  />
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPassword((s) => !s)}
                    aria-label={showPassword ? 'Hide password' : 'Show password'}
                    className={styles.toggle}
                  >
                    {showPassword ? 'Hide' : 'Show'}
                  </button>
                </div>
                {fieldError('password', 'auth-password')}
              </>
            }


            {alertMessage && <p role="alert" className={styles.error}>{alertMessage}</p>}

            <button type="submit" disabled={isPending} className={styles.submit}>
              {submitLabel}
            </button>

          </form>
        </div>
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

import { createContext,useContext } from "react";
import type {User} from './authStore';


export interface AuthValue{
    user:User|null;
    login :(email:string, password:string) => Promise<User>;
    register: (name: string ,email:string,password:string, role?: 'customer' | 'organizer')=> Promise<User>;
    logout:() => void;
}

export const AuthContext = createContext<AuthValue | null>(null);
export function useAuth(): AuthValue{
    const value = useContext(AuthContext);
    if(!value) throw new Error('UseAuth must be used inside<AuthProvider>');
    return value;
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/completePayment.ts
================================================================================
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { StoredEvent } from '../../types/event'
import type { TicketType } from '../../types/event'
import { backendTicketToRecord, purchaseBackendTicket } from '../../lib/backendTickets'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../lib/backendEvents'
import { fetchTicketRecords } from '../tickets/ticketData'
import {
  addPendingTickets,
  getPendingPayment,
  markPaymentCompleted,
  verifyPayment,
  type PendingPayment,
} from '../../lib/paychangu'

export type CompletionResult =
  | { kind: 'success'; tickets: TicketRecord[]; payment: PendingPayment }
  | {
      kind: 'partial'
      tickets: TicketRecord[]
      message: string
      eventId: string
      payment: PendingPayment
    }
  | { kind: 'pending' }
  | { kind: 'failed'; message: string; eventId?: string }
  | { kind: 'unknown' }

const inFlight = new Map<string, Promise<CompletionResult>>()

async function ticketsFor(payment: PendingPayment): Promise<TicketRecord[]> {
  const ids = new Set(payment.ticketIds ?? [])
  if (ids.size === 0) return []

  const tickets = await fetchTicketRecords(payment.userId, payment.holder)
  return tickets.filter((ticket) => ids.has(ticket.id))
}

async function claimBackendTickets(
  pending: PendingPayment,
  event: StoredEvent,
  ticketType: TicketType,
): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const tickets = await ticketsFor(pending)
  let failure: string | null = null

  for (let index = tickets.length; index < pending.quantity; index += 1) {
    try {
      const purchased = await purchaseBackendTicket(event.id, ticketType.id, pending.txRef)
      const ticket = backendTicketToRecord(purchased, {
        userId: pending.userId,
        event,
        ticketType,
        holder: pending.holder,
      })
      tickets.push(ticket)
      addPendingTickets(pending.txRef, [ticket.id])
    } catch (caught) {
      failure = caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'The ticket service could not issue the ticket.'
      break
    }
  }

  const updated = getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? pending

  if (tickets.length >= pending.quantity) {
    markPaymentCompleted(pending.txRef, tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id))
    return {
      kind: 'success',
      tickets,
      payment: getPendingPayment(pending.txRef) ?? {
        ...updated,
        status: 'completed',
        ticketIds: tickets.map((ticket) => ticket.id),
      },
    }
  }

  const issued = tickets.length
  const message =
    issued > 0
      ? `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through. ${issued} of ${pending.quantity} tickets were issued${failure ? `: ${failure}` : '.'} Contact support with this reference for the remaining ticket(s).`
      : `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but no tickets could be issued${failure ? `: ${failure}` : '.'} Contact support for a refund.`

  if (issued > 0) {
    return { kind: 'partial', tickets, message, eventId: pending.eventId, payment: updated }
  }
  return { kind: 'failed', eventId: pending.eventId, message }
}

async function run(txRef: string): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const pending = getPendingPayment(txRef)
  if (!pending) return { kind: 'unknown' }

  if (pending.status === 'completed') {
    const tickets = await ticketsFor(pending)
    if (tickets.length < pending.quantity) {
      throw new Error('The backend has not returned all tickets recorded for this payment.')
    }
    return { kind: 'success', tickets, payment: pending }
  }

  const verified = await verifyPayment(txRef)

  if (verified.status === 'pending') return { kind: 'pending' }
  if (verified.status !== 'success') {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: 'The payment was not completed. No ticket was issued and you were not charged.',
    }
  }

  if (verified.tx_ref !== pending.txRef) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: 'The backend confirmed a different payment reference. No ticket was issued. Please contact support.',
    }
  }

  let event: StoredEvent
  try {
    event = await fetchBackendEvent(pending.eventId)
  } catch (caught) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) was confirmed, but the event details could not be loaded. Please contact support. ${caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : ''}`.trim(),
    }
  }

  const ticketType = event.ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === pending.ticketTypeId)
  if (!ticketType) {
    return {
      kind: 'failed',
      eventId: pending.eventId,
      message: `Your payment (ref ${pending.txRef}) went through, but this ticket is no longer available. Please contact support for a refund.`,
    }
  }

  return claimBackendTickets(pending, event, ticketType)
}

export function completePayment(txRef: string): Promise<CompletionResult> {
  const existing = inFlight.get(txRef)
  if (existing) return existing
  const promise = run(txRef).finally(() => inFlight.delete(txRef))
  inFlight.set(txRef, promise)
  return promise
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/components/QuantityStepper.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'

interface QuantityStepperProps {
  value: number
  min: number
  max: number
  onChange: (value: number) => void
}

export function QuantityStepper({ value, min, max, onChange }: QuantityStepperProps): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="stepper" role="group" aria-label="Ticket quantity">
      <button
        type="button"
        className="stepper-btn"
        onClick={() => onChange(Math.max(min, value - 1))}
        disabled={value <= min}
        aria-label="Decrease quantity"
      >
        −
      </button>
      <span className="stepper-value" aria-live="polite">
        {value}
      </span>
      <button
        type="button"
        className="stepper-btn"
        onClick={() => onChange(Math.min(max, value + 1))}
        disabled={value >= max}
        aria-label="Increase quantity"
      >
        +
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { formatKwacha } from '../format'
import type { CheckoutEvent, TicketType } from '../types'
import { QuantityStepper } from './QuantityStepper'

interface TicketSummaryCardProps {
  event: CheckoutEvent
  ticketType: TicketType
  quantity: number
  onChangeQuantity: (quantity: number) => void
}

export function TicketSummaryCard({
  event,
  ticketType,
  quantity,
  onChangeQuantity,
}: TicketSummaryCardProps): ReactNode {
  const remaining = ticketType.capacity - ticketType.sold
  const soldOut = remaining <= 0

  return (
    <div className="card row">
      <div>
        <b>{event.name}</b>
        <div className="m">
          {event.date} · {ticketType.name} · {event.venue}, {event.city}
        </div>
        <div className="m">
          {formatKwacha(ticketType.price)} each · {soldOut ? 'Sold out' : `${remaining} left`}
        </div>
      </div>
      <QuantityStepper
        value={quantity}
        min={1}
        max={Math.max(remaining, 1)}
        onChange={onChangeQuantity}
      />
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/format.ts
================================================================================
import { SERVICE_FEE } from './config'

export interface OrderTotals {
  subtotal: number
  serviceFee: number
  total: number
}

export function formatKwacha(amount: number): string {
  return `MK${Math.round(amount).toLocaleString('en-US')}`
}

export function calculateTotals(
  price: number,
  quantity: number,
  serviceFee: number = SERVICE_FEE,
): OrderTotals {
  const subtotal = price * quantity
  return { subtotal, serviceFee, total: subtotal + serviceFee }
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/hooks/useCheckout.ts
================================================================================
import { useMutation } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../../types/event'
import {
  createOrder,
  initiatePayment,
  savePendingPayment,
} from '../../../lib/paychangu'
import type { CheckoutInput } from '../types'

export class CheckoutError extends Error {}

// The backend owns the order, reserves stock, starts PayChangu and verifies payment.
async function startPayment(input: CheckoutInput): Promise<never> {
  const stored = await fetchBackendEvent(input.event.id)
  if (!stored || stored.status !== 'Published') {
    throw new CheckoutError('This event is not available for booking.')
  }
  const ticketType = stored.ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === input.ticketType.id)
  if (!ticketType) throw new CheckoutError('That ticket type no longer exists.')

  if (input.quantity < 1) throw new CheckoutError('Select at least one ticket.')
  const remaining = remainingSeats(ticketType)
  if (remaining <= 0) throw new CheckoutError(`${ticketType.name} tickets are sold out for this event.`)
  if (input.quantity > remaining) {
    throw new CheckoutError(`Only ${remaining} ${ticketType.name} ticket${remaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left.`)
  }

  try {
    const order = await createOrder({
      eventId: stored.id,
      ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
      quantity: input.quantity,
    })
    const { checkout_url: checkoutUrl, tx_ref: txRef } = await initiatePayment(order.orderNumber)
    savePendingPayment({
      txRef,
      orderNumber: order.orderNumber,
      eventId: stored.id,
      ticketTypeId: ticketType.id,
      quantity: input.quantity,
      holder: input.holder,
      userId: input.userId,
      createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      status: 'pending',
    })
    window.location.assign(checkoutUrl)
  } catch (error) {
    throw new CheckoutError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not start the payment.')
  }

  // keep the button on "Redirecting…" until the browser leaves the page
  return new Promise<never>(() => {})
}

export function useCheckout() {
  return useMutation({ mutationFn: startPayment })
}

FILE: src/features/checkout/pages/CheckoutPage.tsx
================================================================================

import { useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useParams, useSearch, Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { calculateTotals, formatKwacha } from '../format'
import { useCheckout } from '../hooks/useCheckout'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import type { CheckoutEvent, TicketType } from '../types'
import { remainingSeats } from '../../../types/event'

const toCheckoutEvent = (ev: import('../../../types/event').Event): CheckoutEvent => ({
  id: ev.id,
  name: ev.title,
  date: ev.date,
  time: ev.time,
  venue: ev.venue,
  city: ev.city,
  organizer: ev.organizer,
})

const toCheckoutTicketTypes = (ev: import('../../../types/event').Event): TicketType[] =>
  ev.ticketTypes.map((t) => ({ ...t }))


export function CheckoutPage(): ReactNode {
  const { eventId } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { eventId?: string }
  const { type, qty } = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { type?: string; qty?: string }
  const parsedQty = parseInt(qty ?? '', 10)

  const [selectedTypeId, setSelectedTypeId] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [quantity, setQuantity] = useState(() =>
    Number.isNaN(parsedQty) || parsedQty < 1 ? 1 : parsedQty,
  )
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const holder = user?.name ?? ''
  const [clientError, setClientError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const { data: eventData, isLoading, isError } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['checkout-event', eventId],
    queryFn: () => fetchBackendEvent(eventId ?? ''),
    enabled: !!eventId,
  })

  const ticketTypes: TicketType[] = eventData ? toCheckoutTicketTypes(eventData) : []
  const activeTypeId = selectedTypeId ?? type ?? ticketTypes[0]?.id
  const ticketType =
    ticketTypes.find((candidate) => candidate.id === activeTypeId) ?? ticketTypes[0]
  const checkoutEvent: CheckoutEvent = eventData
    ? toCheckoutEvent(eventData)
    : {
        id: eventId ?? 'unknown',
        name: 'Event',
        date: '',
        time: '',
        venue: '',
        city: '',
        organizer: '',
      }

  const checkout = useCheckout()

  const totals = ticketType
    ? calculateTotals(ticketType.price, quantity)
    : { subtotal: 0, serviceFee: 0, total: 0 }
  const remaining = ticketType ? remainingSeats(ticketType) : 0
  const soldOut = remaining <= 0
  const exceedsAvailable = quantity > remaining
  function selectTicketType(id: string) {
    setSelectedTypeId(id)
    setQuantity(1)
    setClientError(null)
  }

  function handlePay() {
    if (!ticketType || !user) return
    setClientError(null)
    // goes to PayChangu; tickets are issued on the return page once the payment is confirmed
    checkout.mutate({
      userId: user.id,
      event: checkoutEvent,
      ticketType,
      quantity,
      holder,
    })
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 sm:px-6">
        <div className="h-80 animate-pulse rounded-3xl bg-slate-100" />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (isError || !eventData || eventData.status !== 'Published') {
    return (
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-7xl px-4 py-24 text-center sm:px-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">Event not found</h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-slate-600">We couldn’t find this event to check out.</p>
        <Link to="/events" className="mt-6 inline-block rounded-full bg-navy px-6 py-2 text-white">Browse events</Link>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const mutationError = checkout.error instanceof Error ? checkout.error.message : null
  const errorMessage = clientError ?? mutationError
  const blocked = soldOut || exceedsAvailable

  const MAX_PER_ORDER = 10
  const maxQty = Math.max(1, Math.min(MAX_PER_ORDER, remaining))
  const canPay = !blocked && !checkout.isPending && !!ticketType

  return (
    <div className="bg-slate-50">
      <div className="mx-auto max-w-6xl px-4 py-10 sm:px-6">
        <Link to="/events" className="text-sm font-medium text-slate-500 hover:text-navy">
          ← Back to events
        </Link>
        <h1 className="mt-3 text-3xl font-bold text-navy sm:text-4xl">Confirm your purchase</h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-slate-500">Check your order, then pay securely.</p>

        <div className="mt-8 grid gap-8 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_380px]">
          <div className="space-y-6">
            <section className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <div className="bg-navy px-6 py-5 text-white">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-amber-300">Your event</p>
                <h2 className="mt-1 text-2xl font-bold text-white">{checkoutEvent.name}</h2>
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-200">
                  {checkoutEvent.date}
                  {checkoutEvent.time ? ` · ${checkoutEvent.time}` : ''}
                </p>
                <p className="text-sm text-slate-200">
                  {checkoutEvent.venue}
                  {checkoutEvent.city ? `, ${checkoutEvent.city}` : ''}
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-6 p-6">
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ticket type</h3>
                  <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">
                    {ticketTypes.map((option) => {
                      const left = option.capacity - option.sold
                      const disabled = left <= 0
                      const selected = option.id === ticketType?.id
                      return (
                        <button
                          key={option.id}
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => selectTicketType(option.id)}
                          disabled={disabled}
                          aria-pressed={selected}
                          className={`rounded-xl border-2 p-4 text-left transition ${
                            selected
                              ? 'border-navy bg-navy/5'
                              : 'border-slate-200 hover:border-slate-300'
                          } ${disabled ? 'cursor-not-allowed opacity-50' : ''}`}
                        >
                          <span className="block font-semibold text-slate-900">{option.name}</span>
                          <span className="mt-1 block text-lg font-bold text-navy">{formatKwacha(option.price)}</span>
                          <span className="mt-1 block text-xs text-slate-500">
                            {disabled ? 'Sold out' : `${left} left`}
                          </span>
                        </button>
                      )
                    })}
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Quantity</h3>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">Up to {MAX_PER_ORDER} per order</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Decrease quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.max(1, q - 1))}
                      disabled={quantity <= 1}
                      className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-slate-300 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      −
                    </button>
                    <span className="w-8 text-center text-lg font-bold" aria-live="polite">{quantity}</span>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      aria-label="Increase quantity"
                      onClick={() => setQuantity((q) => Math.min(maxQty, q + 1))}
                      disabled={quantity >= maxQty}
                      className="h-10 w-10 rounded-full border border-slate-300 text-lg font-semibold text-slate-700 hover:bg-slate-100 disabled:opacity-40"
                    >
                      +
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="border-t border-slate-100 pt-6">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wide text-slate-500">Ticket holder</h3>
                  <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 px-4 py-3 ring-1 ring-slate-200">
                    <span className="flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-navy text-sm font-bold text-white">
                      {(holder || '?').charAt(0).toUpperCase()}
                    </span>
                    <div className="min-w-0">
                      <p className="truncate font-medium text-slate-900">{holder}</p>
                      <p className="truncate text-xs text-slate-500">{user?.email}</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </section>

            {soldOut && ticketType && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                <b>Sold out.</b> There are no {ticketType.name} tickets left.
              </div>
            )}
            {!soldOut && exceedsAvailable && ticketType && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                Only {remaining} ticket{remaining === 1 ? '' : 's'} left. Reduce the quantity to continue.
              </div>
            )}
            {errorMessage && (
              <div role="alert" className="rounded-xl bg-red-50 p-4 text-sm text-red-700 ring-1 ring-red-200">
                {errorMessage}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <aside className="lg:sticky lg:top-6 lg:self-start">
            <div className="rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm ring-1 ring-slate-200">
              <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-navy">Order summary</h2>
              <dl className="mt-4 space-y-3 text-sm">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-600">
                  <dt>
                    {quantity} × {ticketType?.name ?? 'Ticket'}
                  </dt>
                  <dd className="font-medium text-slate-900">{formatKwacha(totals.subtotal)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-between text-slate-600">
                  <dt>Service fee</dt>
                  <dd className="font-medium text-slate-900">{formatKwacha(totals.serviceFee)}</dd>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between border-t border-slate-200 pt-4">
                  <dt className="text-base font-semibold text-slate-900">Total</dt>
                  <dd className="text-2xl font-bold text-navy">{formatKwacha(totals.total)}</dd>
                </div>
              </dl>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handlePay}
                disabled={!canPay}
                className="mt-6 w-full rounded-xl bg-navy px-4 py-3.5 text-base font-semibold text-white shadow transition hover:opacity-90 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {checkout.isPending ? 'Redirecting…' : `Pay ${formatKwacha(totals.total)}`}
              </button>
            </div>
          </aside>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/checkout/pages/PaymentReturnPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate, useSearch } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { latestPendingPayment } from '../../../lib/paychangu'
import { completePayment, type CompletionResult } from '../completePayment'
import { TicketConfirmation } from '../components/TicketConfirmation'

const RETRY_EVERY_MS = 3000
const MAX_ATTEMPTS = 10

// PayChangu sends the customer here after paying (or giving up). We never trust the URL:
// we ask PayChangu (through our server) whether the payment really succeeded.
export function PaymentReturnPage(): ReactNode {
  const search = useSearch({ strict: false }) as { tx_ref?: unknown; status?: unknown }
  const txRef = search.tx_ref !== undefined ? String(search.tx_ref) : latestPendingPayment()?.txRef
  const returnedStatus = typeof search.status === 'string' ? search.status.toLowerCase() : ''

  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const [result, setResult] = useState<CompletionResult | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!txRef) return
    let active = true
    let timer: number | undefined

    completePayment(txRef)
      .then((outcome) => {
        if (!active) return
        setError(null)
        setResult(outcome)
        if (outcome.kind === 'success' || outcome.kind === 'partial') {
          for (const key of ['events', 'checkout-event', 'tickets', 'bookings']) {
            queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: [key] })
          }
        }
        if (outcome.kind === 'pending' && attempt < MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
          timer = window.setTimeout(() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1), RETRY_EVERY_MS)
        }
      })
      .catch((caught: unknown) => {
        if (!active) return
        setError(caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'Could not confirm the payment.')
      })

    return () => {
      active = false
      if (timer) window.clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }, [txRef, attempt, queryClient])

  if (!txRef || result?.kind === 'unknown') {
    return (
      <Notice title="We couldn't find your order">
        <p className="m">
          This payment was not started from this browser, so we can't match it to an order. If money left your
          account, contact support with your PayChangu receipt.
        </p>
        <Link to="/events" className="btn">Browse events</Link>
      </Notice>
    )
  }

  if (result?.kind === 'success') {
    return <TicketConfirmation tickets={result.tickets} onBuyAgain={() => navigate({ to: '/events' })} />
  }

  if (result?.kind === 'partial') {
    return (
      <div>
        <div className="alert error" style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
          <p>{result.message}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            className="btn o"
            style={{ marginTop: 8 }}
            onClick={() => {
              setResult(null)
              setAttempt((n) => n + 1)
            }}
          >
            Try missing tickets
          </button>
        </div>
        <TicketConfirmation tickets={result.tickets} onBuyAgain={() => navigate({ to: '/events' })} />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (result?.kind === 'failed' || (returnedStatus === 'failed' && !result)) {
    const failed = result?.kind === 'failed' ? result : null
    return (
      <Notice title="Payment not completed">
        <div className="alert error">
          {failed?.message ?? 'The payment was cancelled or failed. No ticket was issued.'}
        </div>
        {failed?.eventId ? (
          <Link to="/checkout/$eventId" params={{ eventId: failed.eventId }} className="btn">Try again</Link>
        ) : (
          <Link to="/events" className="btn">Browse events</Link>
        )}
      </Notice>
    )
  }

  if (error) {
    return (
      <Notice title="We couldn't confirm your payment yet">
        <div className="alert error">{error}</div>
        <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)}>
          Check again
        </button>
      </Notice>
    )
  }

  const waitedTooLong = result?.kind === 'pending' && attempt >= MAX_ATTEMPTS
  return (
    <Notice title={waitedTooLong ? 'Still waiting for confirmation' : 'Confirming your payment…'}>
      <p className="m">
        {waitedTooLong
          ? 'PayChangu has not confirmed the payment yet. If you approved it on your phone, check again in a moment.'
          : 'Please wait while we confirm the payment with PayChangu. Do not close this page.'}
      </p>
      {waitedTooLong && (
        <button type="button" className="btn" onClick={() => setAttempt(0)}>
          Check again
        </button>
      )}
    </Notice>
  )
}

function Notice({ title, children }: { title: string; children: ReactNode }): ReactNode {
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto max-w-xl px-4 py-24 text-center sm:px-6">
      <div className="card" style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 12, justifyItems: 'center' }}>
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        {children}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'

export interface CheckoutEvent {
  id: string
  name: string
  date: string
  time: string
  venue: string
  city: string
  organizer: string
}

  name: string
  price: number
  capacity: number
  sold: number
}

export interface CheckoutInput {
  userId: string
  event: CheckoutEvent
  ticketType: TicketType
  quantity: number
  holder: string
}

export interface CheckoutResult {
  reference: string
  tickets: TicketRecord[]
}

FILE: src/features/events/hooks/useEvents.ts

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { listMyEvents } from '../../../lib/backendOrganizer'
import { fetchPublishedEvents } from '../../../lib/backendEvents'

// Published events from the backend.
export function useEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['events'],
    queryFn: fetchPublishedEvents,
  })
}

// Events managed by the signed-in organizer.
export function useAllEvents() {
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['events', 'all'],
    queryFn: listMyEvents,
  })
}
