FILE: src/features/organizer/pages/StaffManagementPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react'
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router'
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useAllEvents } from '../../events/hooks/useEvents'
import {
  assignStaffEvents, createMyStaff, listMyStaff, removeStaff, setStaffActive,
  type StaffMember,
} from '../../../lib/backendUsers'
import { ConfirmDialog } from '../../../components/ConfirmDialog'
import { useToast } from '../../toast'

const field = 'mt-1 w-full rounded-xl border border-slate-200 px-3 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-navy'
const emptyForm = { name: '', email: '', password: '' }

// An organizer creates staff accounts, chooses which events each one may scan,
// and switches them off or removes them. Everything is saved in the backend.
export function StaffManagementPage() {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  const { showToast } = useToast()
  const { data: staff = [], isLoading, error: loadError } = useQuery({ queryKey: ['my-staff'], queryFn: listMyStaff })
  const { data: events = [] } = useAllEvents()
  const [form, setForm] = useState(emptyForm)
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [removing, setRemoving] = useState<StaffMember | null>(null)

  const refresh = () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['my-staff'] })

  async function run(action: () => Promise<unknown>, success: string) {
    try {
      await action()
      await refresh()
      showToast(success, 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      showToast(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Something went wrong', 'error')
    }
  }

  async function create(e: FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault()
    setFormError(null)
    if (!form.name.trim()) return setFormError('Enter the staff member’s name.')
    if (!/^\S+@\S+\.\S+$/.test(form.email.trim())) return setFormError('Enter a valid email address.')
    if (form.password.length < 8) return setFormError('The password needs at least 8 characters.')
    setSaving(true)
    try {
      await createMyStaff(form)
      setForm(emptyForm)
      await refresh()
      showToast('Staff account created', 'success')
    } catch (err) {
      setFormError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Could not create the account.')
    } finally {
      setSaving(false)
    }
  }

  function toggleEvent(member: StaffMember, eventId: string) {
    const next = member.assignedEventIds.includes(eventId)
      ? member.assignedEventIds.filter((id) => id !== eventId)
      : [...member.assignedEventIds, eventId]
    void run(() => assignStaffEvents(member.id, next), 'Assignments saved')
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto min-h-[60vh] max-w-4xl px-4 py-20 sm:px-6">
      <Link to="/organizer/dashboard" className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-600 hover:text-navy">
        ← Organizer dashboard
      </Link>
      <h1 className="mt-4 text-3xl font-extrabold text-navy-dark">Staff</h1>
      <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-600">
        Staff scan tickets at the door. They can only scan the events you assign to them.
      </p>

      <form onSubmit={create} className="mt-6 rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm" noValidate>
        <h2 className="text-lg font-bold text-navy-dark">Add a staff member</h2>
        <div className="mt-3 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-3">
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Name
            <input className={field} value={form.name} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, name: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Email
            <input type="email" className={field} value={form.email} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, email: e.target.value })} />
          </label>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium">Temporary password
            <input type="password" className={field} value={form.password} onChange={(e) => setForm({ ...form, password: e.target.value })} placeholder="At least 8 characters" />
          </label>
        </div>
        {formError && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-600">{formError}</p>}
        <button type="submit" disabled={saving} className="mt-4 rounded-full bg-navy px-5 py-2 text-sm font-semibold text-white hover:bg-navy-dark disabled:opacity-60">
          {saving ? 'Creating…' : 'Create staff account'}
        </button>
      </form>

      <section className="mt-6 space-y-4" aria-label="Your staff">
        {isLoading && <p className="text-sm text-slate-500">Loading staff…</p>}
        {loadError && <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-red-600">{loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load staff.'}</p>}
        {!isLoading && !loadError && staff.length === 0 && (
          <p className="rounded-xl border border-slate-200 bg-white p-4 text-sm text-slate-600">You have no staff yet.</p>
        )}
        {staff.map((member) => (
          <article key={member.id} className="rounded-2xl border border-slate-100 bg-white p-5 shadow-sm">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-3">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-navy-dark">{member.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">{member.email}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span className={`rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold ${member.isActive ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : 'bg-slate-200 text-slate-700'}`}>
                  {member.isActive ? 'Active' : 'Disabled'}
                </span>
                <button
                  onClick={() => void run(() => setStaffActive(member.id, !member.isActive), member.isActive ? 'Staff disabled' : 'Staff enabled')}
                  className="rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold hover:bg-slate-50"
                >{member.isActive ? 'Disable' : 'Enable'}</button>
                <button
                  onClick={() => setRemoving(member)}
                  className="rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-white hover:bg-red-700"
                >Remove</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <fieldset className="mt-4" disabled={!member.isActive}>
              <legend className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase text-slate-500">Events they can scan</legend>
              {events.length === 0 && <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-slate-500">Create an event first.</p>}
              <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                {events.map((event) => (
                  <label key={event.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 px-3 py-1.5 text-sm">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={member.assignedEventIds.includes(String(event.id))}
                      onChange={() => toggleEvent(member, String(event.id))}
                    />
                    {event.name}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
          </article>
        ))}
      </section>

      <ConfirmDialog
        open={!!removing}
        onClose={() => setRemoving(null)}
        onConfirm={() => { if (removing) void run(() => removeStaff(removing.id), 'Staff removed') }}
        title={removing ? `Remove ${removing.name}?` : 'Remove staff?'}
        description="Their login is switched off and they can no longer scan tickets."
        confirmLabel="Remove"
        variant="danger"
      />
    </main>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/bookingData.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '../../lib/backend'
import type { Booking, BookingStatus } from './bookingTypes'

type JsonRecord = Record<string, unknown>

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is JsonRecord {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
}

function firstString(...values: unknown[]): string | undefined {
  return values.find((value): value is string => typeof value === 'string' && value.trim().length > 0)
}

function firstNumber(...values: unknown[]): number | undefined {
  return values.find((value): value is number => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value))
}

function normalizeStatus(value: unknown): BookingStatus {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') throw new Error('The backend returned an order without a status.')
  switch (value.toUpperCase()) {
    case 'PAID':
    case 'COMPLETED':
    case 'CONFIRMED':
    case 'SUCCESS':
      return 'confirmed'
    case 'CANCELLED':
    case 'CANCELED':
    case 'REFUNDED':
      return 'cancelled'
    case 'PENDING':
    case 'PROCESSING':
    case 'INITIATED':
      return 'pending'
    case 'FAILED':
    case 'EXPIRED':
      return 'failed'
    default:
      throw new Error(`The backend returned an unsupported order status: ${value}`)
  }
}

function mapOrder(value: unknown): Booking {
  if (!isRecord(value)) throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid order.')

  const event = isRecord(value.event) ? value.event : {}
  const id = firstString(value.orderNumber, value.id, value.orderId)
  const eventTitle = firstString(value.eventTitle, event.title, event.name)
  const venue = firstString(value.venue, event.venue) ?? 'Venue not provided'
  const date = firstString(value.eventDate, event.startAt, event.date, value.createdAt, value.created_at)
  const total = firstNumber(value.totalAmount, value.total, value.amount)
  const items = Array.isArray(value.items) ? value.items : []
  const quantity = firstNumber(value.quantity) ??
    items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + (isRecord(item) ? firstNumber(item.quantity) ?? 0 : 0), 0)

  if (!id || !eventTitle || !date || quantity < 1) {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an order missing its number, event, date, or ticket quantity.')
  }

  return {
    id,
    eventTitle,
    venue,
    date,
    quantity,
    ...(total === undefined ? {} : { total }),
    status: normalizeStatus(value.status ?? value.paymentStatus),
  }
}

export async function fetchBookings(): Promise<Booking[]> {
  const result = await api<unknown>('/orders/mine')
  if (!Array.isArray(result)) throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid order history.')
  return result.map(mapOrder)
}

export type BookingStatus = 'confirmed' | 'cancelled' | 'pending' | 'failed';

export type Booking = {
  id: string;
  eventTitle: string;
  venue: string;
  date: string; // ISO date, e.g. 2026-09-12
  quantity: number;
  total?: number;
  status: BookingStatus;
};

import type { Booking } from '../bookingTypes';
import styles from './BookingCard.module.css';

function formatDate(iso: string) {
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString('en-GB', {
    day: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  });
}

export function BookingCard({ booking }: { booking: Booking }) {
  const statusClass = styles[booking.status]

  return (
    <article className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.info}>
        <h3 className={styles.title}>{booking.eventTitle}</h3>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          {booking.venue} · {formatDate(booking.date)}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.meta}>
          {booking.id} · {booking.quantity} ticket{booking.quantity > 1 ? 's' : ''}
        </p>
      </div>
      <div className={styles.side}>
        <strong className={styles.total}>
          {booking.total === undefined ? 'Total unavailable' : `MK ${booking.total.toLocaleString()}`}
        </strong>
        <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{booking.status}</span>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

import type { Ticket } from '../ticketTypes';
import { formatDate } from '../ticketFormat';
import { downloadTicketImage, ticketFromView } from '../ticketImage';
import styles from './TicketDetail.module.css';

// The ticket itself: status, event, QR code and the download button.
// The full details (venue, price, purchase time...) are in TicketInfoTable.
export function TicketDetail({ ticket }: { ticket: Ticket }) {
  const statusClass = ticket.status === 'valid' ? styles.valid : styles.used;

  return (
    <article className={styles.card}>
      <div className={styles.info}>
        <span className={`${styles.badge} ${statusClass}`}>{ticket.status}</span>
        <h2 className={styles.title}>{ticket.eventTitle}</h2>
        <p className={styles.venue}>
          {ticket.venue}, {ticket.city}
        </p>
        <p className={styles.venue}>
          {formatDate(ticket.date)} · {ticket.time}
        </p>

        <button
          type="button"
          className={styles.download}
          onClick={() => void downloadTicketImage(ticketFromView(ticket), null, ticket.qrCodeDataUrl)}
        >
          Download ticket
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.qrBox}>
        {ticket.qrCodeDataUrl ? (
          <img
            src={ticket.qrCodeDataUrl}
            width={168}
            height={168}
            alt=""
            style={{ width: 168, height: 168, objectFit: 'contain' }}
          />
        ) : (
          <p className={styles.qrNote}>A QR code is unavailable for this ticket.</p>
        )}
        <p className={styles.qrId}>{ticket.id}</p>
        <p className={styles.qrNote}>
          {ticket.status === 'valid'
            ? 'Show this code at the entrance'
            : ticket.status === 'used'
              ? 'This ticket has already been used'
              : `This ticket is ${ticket.status}`}
        </p>
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/components/TicketsTable.tsx

import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import type { Ticket } from '../ticketTypes';
import { formatDate, formatDateTime, formatMoney } from '../ticketFormat';
import styles from './TicketsTable.module.css';

export function TicketsTable({ tickets }: { tickets: Ticket[] }) {
  return (
    <div className={styles.wrap}>
      <table className={styles.table}>
        <caption className="sr-only">Your tickets</caption>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th scope="col">Event</th>
            <th scope="col">Date</th>
            <th scope="col">Venue</th>
            <th scope="col">Type</th>
            <th scope="col">Price</th>
            <th scope="col">Status</th>
            <th scope="col">Purchased</th>
            <th scope="col">
              <span className="sr-only">Open</span>
            </th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {tickets.map((t, index) => (
            <tr key={t.id || index}>
              <td>
                {t.id ? (
                  <Link to="/tickets/$ticketId" params={{ ticketId: t.id }} className={styles.link}>
                    {t.eventTitle || 'Unknown Event'}
                  </Link>
                ) : (
                  <span className={styles.event}>{t.eventTitle || 'Unknown Event'}</span>
                )}
              </td>
              <td>{t.date ? formatDate(t.date) : 'TBD'}</td>
              <td>{t.venue ? `${t.venue}, ${t.city}` : '—'}</td>
              <td>{t.ticketType || 'Standard'}</td>
              <td>{formatMoney(t.price ?? 0)}</td>
              <td>
                <span
                  className={`${styles.badge} ${t.status === 'valid' ? styles.valid : styles.used}`}
                >
                  {t.status || 'valid'}
                </span>
              </td>
              <td>{t.purchasedAt ? formatDateTime(t.purchasedAt) : '—'}</td>
              <td>
                {t.id && (
                  <Link to="/tickets/$ticketId" params={{ ticketId: t.id }} className={styles.link}>
                    View ticket
                  </Link>
                )}
              </td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/hooks/useBookings.ts
================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication'
import { fetchBookings } from '../bookingData'

export function useBookings() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ['bookings', user?.id],
    queryFn: fetchBookings,
    enabled: !!user?.id,
  })
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/hooks/useTickets.ts
================================================================================
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { fetchTickets } from '../ticketData';
import { useAuth } from '../../authentication';

export function useTickets() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const userId = user?.id;
  return useQuery({
    // Keyed per user so switching accounts never shows the previous cache.
    queryKey: ['tickets', userId],
    queryFn: () => fetchTickets(userId, user?.name ?? ''),
    enabled: !!userId,
  });
}

import { BookingCard } from '../components/BookingCard'
import { useBookings } from '../hooks/useBookings'
import styles from './BookingHistoryPage.module.css'

export function BookingHistoryPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, error, refetch } = useBookings()

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <h1 className={styles.heading}>Booking history</h1>
        <p className={styles.sub}>Orders and payment status from your account</p>
      </header>
      {isLoading && <p className={styles.state}>Loading your bookings...</p>}
      {isError && (
        <p className={styles.state} role="alert">
          {error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not load your bookings.'}{' '}
          <button type="button" className={styles.retry} onClick={() => refetch()}>Try again</button>
        </p>
      )}
      {!isLoading && !isError && data?.length === 0 && (
        <p className={styles.state}>You have no bookings yet.</p>
      )}
      {!isLoading && !isError && data && data.length > 0 && (
        <section className={styles.list} aria-label="Your bookings">
          {data.map((booking) => <BookingCard key={booking.id} booking={booking} />)}
        </section>
      )}
    </main>
  )
}

FILE: src/features/tickets/pages/MyTicketsPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useState } from 'react';
import { TicketsTable } from '../components/TicketsTable';
import { useTickets } from '../hooks/useTickets';
import { isUpcoming } from '../ticketFormat';
import styles from './MyTicketsPage.module.css';

type Tab = 'all' | 'upcoming' | 'past';

const tabs: { key: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
  { key: 'upcoming', label: 'Upcoming' },
  { key: 'past', label: 'Past' },
];

export function MyTicketsPage() {
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useTickets();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>('all');

  const tickets = data ?? [];
  const visible = tickets.filter((t) => {
    if (tab === 'all') return true;
    return tab === 'upcoming' ? isUpcoming(t.date) : !isUpcoming(t.date);
  });

  return (
    <div className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <h1 className={styles.heading}>My Tickets</h1>
        <p className={styles.sub}>Open a ticket to see its QR code, details and entry status</p>
      </header>

      {isLoading && <p className={styles.state}>Loading your tickets...</p>}

      {isError && (
        <p className={styles.state}>
          Could not load tickets.{' '}
          <button type="button" className={styles.retry} onClick={() => refetch()}>
            Try again
          </button>
        </p>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && tickets.length === 0 && (
        <p className={styles.state}>You have no tickets yet.</p>
      )}

      {tickets.length > 0 && (
        <section className={styles.section}>
          <div className={styles.filters}>
            {tabs.map((tabItem) => (
              <button
                key={tabItem.key}
                type="button"
                className={`${styles.pill} ${tab === tabItem.key ? styles.pillActive : ''}`}
                onClick={() => setTab(tabItem.key)}
              >
                {tabItem.label}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>

          {visible.length === 0 ? (
            <p className={styles.state}>No tickets to show.</p>
          ) : (
            <TicketsTable tickets={visible} />
          )}
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default MyTicketsPage;

import { Link, useParams } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { TicketDetail } from '../components/TicketDetail';
import { TicketInfoTable } from '../components/TicketInfoTable';
import { useTickets } from '../hooks/useTickets';
import styles from './TicketDetailPage.module.css';

export function TicketDetailPage() {
  const { ticketId } = useParams({ strict: false }) as { ticketId?: string };
  const { data, isLoading, isError, refetch } = useTickets();
  const ticket = data?.find((t) => t.id === ticketId);

  return (
    <div className={styles.page}>
      <Link to="/tickets" className={styles.back}>
        ← My tickets
      </Link>
      <h1 className={styles.heading}>Ticket details</h1>

      {isLoading && <p className={styles.state}>Loading your ticket...</p>}

      {isError && (
        <p className={styles.state}>
          Could not load this ticket.{' '}
          <button type="button" className={styles.retry} onClick={() => refetch()}>
            Try again
          </button>
        </p>
      )}

      {!isLoading && !isError && !ticket && (
        <p className={styles.state}>
          We could not find that ticket. It may belong to a different account.
        </p>
      )}

      {ticket && (
        <>
          <TicketDetail ticket={ticket} />
          <TicketInfoTable ticket={ticket} />
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

export default TicketDetailPage;


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/ticketData.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from '../../lib/backend'
import { fetchBackendEvent } from '../../lib/backendEvents'
import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { Ticket, TicketStatus } from './ticketTypes'

interface BackendHistoryTicket {
  ticketNumber: string
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  status: 'ISSUED' | 'USED' | 'CANCELLED' | 'REFUNDED'
  issuedAt: string
  checkedInAt: string | null
  qrCodeDataUrl?: string
}

const TICKET_STATUS: Record<BackendHistoryTicket['status'], TicketStatus> = {
  ISSUED: 'valid',
  USED: 'used',
  CANCELLED: 'cancelled',
  REFUNDED: 'refunded',
}

export function toTicketView(record: TicketRecord): Ticket {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    eventTitle: record.eventName,
    venue: record.venue,
    city: record.city,
    date: record.date,
    time: record.time,
    ticketType: record.ticketType,
    seat: `${record.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: record.price,
    holder: record.holder,
    status: record.status,
    purchasedAt: record.createdAt,
    qrCodeDataUrl: record.qrCodeDataUrl,
  }
}

export async function fetchTicketRecords(userId?: string, userName = ''): Promise<TicketRecord[]> {
  if (!userId) return []
  const records = await api<BackendHistoryTicket[]>('/tickets/history')
  const eventIds = [...new Set(records.map((ticket) => ticket.eventId))]
  const events = await Promise.all(eventIds.map((id) => fetchBackendEvent(id)))
  const eventById = new Map(events.map((event) => [event.id, event]))

  return records.flatMap((record) => {
    const event = eventById.get(record.eventId)
    const ticketType = event?.ticketTypes.find((type) => type.id === record.ticketTypeId)
    if (!event || !ticketType) return []

    return [{
      id: record.ticketNumber,
      userId,
      eventId: event.id,
      eventName: event.title,
      date: event.date,
      time: event.time,
      venue: event.venue,
      city: event.city,
      ticketType: ticketType.name,
      price: ticketType.price,
      holder: userName,
      status: TICKET_STATUS[record.status],
      createdAt: record.issuedAt,
      qrCodeDataUrl: record.qrCodeDataUrl,
    } satisfies TicketRecord]
  })
}

export async function fetchTickets(userId?: string, userName = ''): Promise<Ticket[]> {
  const records = await fetchTicketRecords(userId, userName)
  return records.map(toTicketView)
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/features/tickets/ticketFormat.ts
================================================================================
// Event dates are calendar dates without a time. Format them in UTC so the
// same ticket never renders on two different days (issue #73).
export function formatDate(iso: string): string {
  if (!iso) return 'TBD';
  const date = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return 'TBD';
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
    timeZone: 'UTC',
  }).format(date);
}

export function formatMoney(amount: number | undefined | null): string {
  if (amount === undefined || amount === null || isNaN(amount)) return 'MK 0';
  return `MK ${amount.toLocaleString()}`;
}

// A moment in time (for example when a ticket was bought), shown in the
// viewer's own time zone: "24 Oct 2026, 18:00".
export function formatDateTime(iso: string | undefined | null): string {
  if (!iso) return '—';
  const moment = new Date(iso);
  if (Number.isNaN(moment.getTime())) return '—';
  const day = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    day: '2-digit',
    month: 'short',
    year: 'numeric',
  }).format(moment);
  const time = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
    hour12: false,
  }).format(moment);
  return `${day}, ${time}`;
}

// true when the event date is today or later
export function isUpcoming(iso: string): boolean {
  if (!iso) return false;
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA'); // YYYY-MM-DD
  return iso >= today;
}

import type { TicketRecord } from '../../lib/tickets'
import type { Ticket, TicketStatus } from './ticketTypes'
import { formatDate, formatMoney } from './ticketFormat'

// One normalised shape used by both call sites (My Tickets and checkout).
export interface TicketImageData {
  id: string
  eventTitle: string
  venue: string
  city: string
  date: string
  time: string
  ticketType: string
  entry: string
  price: number
  holder: string
  status: TicketStatus
}

export function ticketFromRecord(record: TicketRecord): TicketImageData {
  return {
    id: record.id,
    eventTitle: record.eventName,
    venue: record.venue,
    city: record.city,
    date: record.date,
    time: record.time,
    ticketType: record.ticketType,
    entry: `${record.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: record.price,
    holder: record.holder,
    status: record.status,
  }
}

export function ticketFromView(ticket: Ticket): TicketImageData {
  return {
    id: ticket.id,
    eventTitle: ticket.eventTitle,
    venue: ticket.venue,
    city: ticket.city,
    date: ticket.date,
    time: ticket.time,
    ticketType: ticket.ticketType,
    // Keep this identical to ticketFromRecord so both download paths match.
    entry: `${ticket.ticketType} Admission`,
    price: ticket.price,
    holder: ticket.holder,
    status: ticket.status,
  }
}

// Logical size of the ticket card. It is rendered at 2x so the PNG stays
// crisp on phones and high-DPI displays.
export const TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH = 1000
export const TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT = 420
export const TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE = 2

type CanvasFactory = (width: number, height: number) => HTMLCanvasElement

interface RenderOptions {
  scale?: number
  createCanvas?: CanvasFactory
}

function createCanvasElement(width: number, height: number): HTMLCanvasElement {
  const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
  canvas.width = width
  canvas.height = height
  return canvas
}

function roundRect(
  context: CanvasRenderingContext2D,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  radius: number,
): void {
  context.beginPath()
  context.roundRect(x, y, width, height, radius)
}

export function renderTicketImage(
  ticket: TicketImageData,
  qrCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  options: RenderOptions = {},
): HTMLCanvasElement | null {
  const scale = options.scale ?? TICKET_IMAGE_SCALE
  const createCanvas = options.createCanvas ?? createCanvasElement
  const canvas = createCanvas(
    Math.round(TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH * scale),
    Math.round(TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT * scale),
  )
  const context = canvas.getContext('2d')
  if (!context) return null

  // Draw in logical units; the scale handles high-DPI output.
  context.scale(scale, scale)

  // Card background and border
  context.fillStyle = '#ffffff'
  context.fillRect(0, 0, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH, TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT)
  context.strokeStyle = '#d9e2ef'
  context.lineWidth = 2
  roundRect(context, 1, 1, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH - 2, TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT - 2, 20)
  context.stroke()

  // Navy header band, clipped to the rounded card
  context.save()
  roundRect(context, 1, 1, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH - 2, TICKET_IMAGE_HEIGHT - 2, 20)
  context.clip()
  context.fillStyle = '#12326b'
  context.fillRect(0, 0, TICKET_IMAGE_WIDTH, 70)
  context.restore()

  context.fillStyle = '#ffffff'
  context.font = 'bold 24px sans-serif'
  context.textBaseline = 'middle'
  context.fillText('TICKET MASTER', 32, 36)

  // Status badge
  const valid = ticket.status === 'valid'
  context.fillStyle = valid ? '#dcfce7' : '#e2e8f0'
  roundRect(context, 834, 20, 134, 32, 16)
  context.fill()
  context.fillStyle = valid ? '#15803d' : '#475569'
  context.font = 'bold 16px sans-serif'
  context.textAlign = 'center'
  context.fillText(valid ? 'Valid' : 'Used', 901, 37)
  context.textAlign = 'start'
  context.textBaseline = 'alphabetic'

  // Event title and place
  context.fillStyle = '#0a1f47'
  context.font = 'bold 30px sans-serif'
  context.fillText(ticket.eventTitle, 32, 132, 600)
  context.fillStyle = '#64748b'
  context.font = '20px sans-serif'
  context.fillText(`${ticket.venue}, ${ticket.city}`, 32, 164, 600)

  // Details grid: date/time/ticket then entry/price/holder
  const details: Array<[string, string, number, number, number]> = [
    ['Date', formatDate(ticket.date), 32, 222, 190],
    ['Time', ticket.time, 250, 222, 170],
    ['Ticket', ticket.ticketType, 448, 222, 200],
    ['Entry', ticket.entry, 32, 300, 190],
    ['Price', formatMoney(ticket.price), 250, 300, 170],
    ['Holder', ticket.holder, 448, 300, 200],
  ]

  for (const [label, value, x, y, maxWidth] of details) {
    context.fillStyle = '#64748b'
    context.font = '16px sans-serif'
    context.fillText(label, x, y)
    context.fillStyle = '#0a1f47'
    context.font = 'bold 18px sans-serif'
    context.fillText(value, x, y + 24, maxWidth)
  }

  // QR panel
  context.strokeStyle = '#d9e2ef'
  context.lineWidth = 2
  context.setLineDash([6, 5])
  roundRect(context, 690, 40, 270, 340, 18)
  context.stroke()
  context.setLineDash([])

  if (qrCanvas) {
    context.drawImage(qrCanvas, 735, 66, 180, 180)
  }

  context.textAlign = 'center'
  context.fillStyle = '#0a1f47'
  context.font = 'bold 17px sans-serif'
  context.fillText(ticket.id, 825, 278, 240)
  context.fillStyle = '#64748b'
  context.font = '15px sans-serif'
  context.fillText('Show this code at the entrance', 825, 306, 240)
  context.textAlign = 'start'

  return canvas
}

export function ticketImageBlob(
  ticket: TicketImageData,
  qrCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  options: RenderOptions = {},
): Promise<Blob | null> {
  const canvas = renderTicketImage(ticket, qrCanvas, options)
  if (!canvas) return Promise.resolve(null)

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    canvas.toBlob((blob) => resolve(blob), 'image/png')
  })
}

function qrCanvasFromDataUrl(dataUrl: string): Promise<HTMLCanvasElement | null> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const image = new Image()
    image.onload = () => {
      const canvas = createCanvasElement(360, 360)
      const context = canvas.getContext('2d')
      if (!context) {
        resolve(null)
        return
      }
      context.fillStyle = '#ffffff'
      context.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
      context.drawImage(image, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
      resolve(canvas)
    }
    image.onerror = () => resolve(null)
    image.src = dataUrl
  })
}

export async function downloadTicketImage(
  ticket: TicketImageData,
  qrCanvas: HTMLCanvasElement | null,
  qrDataUrl?: string,
): Promise<void> {
  const codeCanvas = qrCanvas ?? (qrDataUrl ? await qrCanvasFromDataUrl(qrDataUrl) : null)
  const blob = await ticketImageBlob(ticket, codeCanvas)
  if (!blob) return

  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  const link = document.createElement('a')
  link.href = url
  link.download = `${ticket.id}.png`
  link.click()
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url)
}

FILE: src/features/toast/ToastProvider.tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';
import { ToastContext, type Toast, type ToastVariant } from './useToast';
import { ToastViewport } from './components/ToastViewport';

const AUTO_DISMISS_MS = 4000;

export function ToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<Toast[]>([]);
  const nextId = useRef(1);
  const timeoutIds = useRef<Set<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>>(new Set());

  const dismissToast = useCallback((id: number) => {
    setToasts((current) => current.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id));
  }, []);

  const showToast = useCallback(
    (message: string, variant: ToastVariant = 'info') => {
      const id = nextId.current++;
      setToasts((current) => [...current, { id, message, variant }]);
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        timeoutIds.current.delete(timeoutId);
        dismissToast(id);
      }, AUTO_DISMISS_MS);
      timeoutIds.current.add(timeoutId);
    },
    [dismissToast],
  );

  // clear pending timers when the provider unmounts
  useEffect(() => {
    const timeouts = timeoutIds.current;
    return () => {
      timeouts.forEach((timeoutId) => clearTimeout(timeoutId));
      timeouts.clear();
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ toasts, showToast, dismissToast }}>
      {children}
      <ToastViewport toasts={toasts} onDismiss={dismissToast} />
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

import type { Toast } from '../useToast';
import styles from './ToastViewport.module.css';

const ICONS: Record<Toast['variant'], string> = {
  success: '✓',
  error: '!',
  info: 'i',
};

export function ToastViewport({
  toasts,
  onDismiss,
}: {
  toasts: Toast[];
  onDismiss: (id: number) => void;
}) {
  if (toasts.length === 0) return null;

  return (
    <div className={styles.viewport} role="region" aria-label="Notifications">
      {toasts.map((toast) => (
        <div
          key={toast.id}
          role="status"
          aria-live="polite"
          className={`${styles.toast} ${styles[toast.variant]}`}
        >
          <span className={styles.icon}>{ICONS[toast.variant]}</span>
          <p className={styles.message}>{toast.message}</p>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-label="Dismiss notification"
            onClick={() => onDismiss(toast.id)}
            className={styles.close}
          >
            ×
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

FILE: src/features/toast/useToast.ts
================================================================================
import { createContext, useContext } from 'react';

export type ToastVariant = 'success' | 'error' | 'info';

export interface Toast {
  id: number;
  message: string;
  variant: ToastVariant;
}

export interface ToastValue {
  toasts: Toast[];
  showToast: (message: string, variant?: ToastVariant) => void;
  dismissToast: (id: number) => void;
}

export const ToastContext = createContext<ToastValue | null>(null);

export function useToast(): ToastValue {
  const value = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!value) throw new Error('useToast must be used inside <ToastProvider>');
  return value;
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/features/validation/hooks/useQrScanner.ts
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'

interface DetectedBarcode {
  rawValue: string
}

interface BarcodeDetectorLike {
  detect: (source: CanvasImageSource) => Promise<DetectedBarcode[]>
}

interface BarcodeDetectorConstructor {
  new (options?: { formats?: string[] }): BarcodeDetectorLike
}

export type QrScannerStatus =
  | 'idle'
  | 'starting'
  | 'scanning'
  | 'unsupported'
  | 'denied'
  | 'error'

const SCAN_INTERVAL_MS = 400
const REPEAT_COOLDOWN_MS = 2500

function getBarcodeDetectorConstructor(): BarcodeDetectorConstructor | undefined {
  return (window as unknown as { BarcodeDetector?: BarcodeDetectorConstructor }).BarcodeDetector
}

export function useQrScanner(onDetected: (code: string) => void) {
  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement>(null)
  const streamRef = useRef<MediaStream | null>(null)
  const detectorRef = useRef<BarcodeDetectorLike | null>(null)
  const timerRef = useRef<number | null>(null)
  const lastCodeRef = useRef<{ code: string; at: number }>({ code: '', at: 0 })
  const onDetectedRef = useRef(onDetected)

  const [status, setStatus] = useState<QrScannerStatus>('idle')
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    onDetectedRef.current = onDetected
  }, [onDetected])

  const stop = useCallback(() => {
    if (timerRef.current !== null) {
      window.clearInterval(timerRef.current)
      timerRef.current = null
    }
    if (streamRef.current) {
      streamRef.current.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop())
      streamRef.current = null
    }
    if (videoRef.current) {
      videoRef.current.srcObject = null
    }
    detectorRef.current = null
    setStatus('idle')
  }, [])

  const start = useCallback(async () => {
    setError(null)

    const Detector = getBarcodeDetectorConstructor()
    if (!Detector) {
      setStatus('unsupported')
      setError('QR scanning is not supported in this browser. Enter the ticket ID manually below.')
      return
    }
    if (!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia) {
      setStatus('unsupported')
      setError('Camera access is not available here. Enter the ticket ID manually below.')
      return
    }

    setStatus('starting')
    try {
      const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
        video: { facingMode: 'environment' },
        audio: false,
      })
      streamRef.current = stream

      const video = videoRef.current
      if (!video) {
        stop()
        return
      }
      video.srcObject = stream
      await video.play()

      detectorRef.current = new Detector({ formats: ['qr_code'] })
      setStatus('scanning')

      timerRef.current = window.setInterval(async () => {
        const currentVideo = videoRef.current
        const detector = detectorRef.current
        if (!currentVideo || !detector || currentVideo.readyState < 2) return
        try {
          const codes = await detector.detect(currentVideo)
          const value = codes[0]?.rawValue?.trim()
          if (!value) return
          const now = Date.now()
          if (value === lastCodeRef.current.code && now - lastCodeRef.current.at < REPEAT_COOLDOWN_MS) {
            return
          }
          lastCodeRef.current = { code: value, at: now }
          onDetectedRef.current(value)
        } catch {
          // Ignore transient decoding errors and keep scanning.
        }
      }, SCAN_INTERVAL_MS)
    } catch (caught) {
      stop()
      const denied = caught instanceof DOMException && caught.name === 'NotAllowedError'
      setStatus(denied ? 'denied' : 'error')
      setError(
        denied
          ? 'Camera permission was denied. Enter the ticket ID manually below.'
          : 'Could not start the camera. Enter the ticket ID manually below.',
      )
    }
  }, [stop])

  useEffect(() => stop, [stop])

  return { videoRef, status, error, start, stop }
}

import type { TicketValidationResult } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { backendValidationToResult, validateBackendTicket } from '../../../lib/backendTickets'

export async function validateTicket(rawCode: string, eventId: string): Promise<TicketValidationResult> {
  const response = await validateBackendTicket(rawCode, eventId)
  return backendValidationToResult(response)
}

export { ScannerPage } from './pages/ScannerPage'

FILE: src/features/validation/pages/ScannerPage.tsx
================================================================================
import { useCallback, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { FormEvent, ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import type { TicketValidationResult } from '../../../lib/tickets'
import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query'
import { listMyAssignedEvents } from '../../../lib/backendUsers'
import { ScanResult } from '../components/ScanResult'
import { useQrScanner } from '../hooks/useQrScanner'
import { validateTicket } from '../hooks/useTicketValidator'

export function ScannerPage(): ReactNode {
  const [result, setResult] = useState<TicketValidationResult | null>(null)
  const [history, setHistory] = useState<TicketValidationResult[]>([])
  const [manualCode, setManualCode] = useState('')
  const [checking, setChecking] = useState(false)
  const checkingRef = useRef(false)
  const queryClient = useQueryClient()
  // only the events the organizer assigned to this staff account
  const { data: events = [], isLoading: loadingEvents } = useQuery({
    queryKey: ['staff-assigned-events'],
    queryFn: listMyAssignedEvents,
  })
  const [selectedEventId, setSelectedEventId] = useState(events[0]?.id ?? '')
  const selectedEvent = events.find((e) => e.id === selectedEventId) ?? events[0]
  const activeEventId = selectedEvent?.id ?? ''

  const handleCode = useCallback(
    async (rawCode: string) => {
      const outcome = await (async (): Promise<TicketValidationResult> => {
        if (!activeEventId) {
          return {
            status: 'invalid',
            title: 'No event selected',
            message: 'Choose the event you are checking tickets for.',
          }
        }
        try {
          return await validateTicket(rawCode, activeEventId)
        } catch (caught) {
          return {
            status: 'invalid',
            title: 'Could not check ticket',
            message: caught instanceof Error ? caught.message : 'The ticket could not be checked. Try again.',
          }
        }
      })()

      setResult(outcome)
      setHistory((previous) => [outcome, ...previous].slice(0, 6))
      setManualCode('')
      if (outcome.status === 'valid') {
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['event-stats'] })
      }
    },
    [queryClient, activeEventId],
  )

  const checkCode = useCallback(
    (rawCode: string) => {
      if (checkingRef.current) return
      checkingRef.current = true
      setChecking(true)
      void handleCode(rawCode).finally(() => {
        checkingRef.current = false
        setChecking(false)
      })
    },
    [handleCode],
  )

  const { videoRef, status, error, start, stop } = useQrScanner(checkCode)

  function handleManualSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault()
    checkCode(manualCode)
  }

  const checkedIn = history.filter((item) => item.status === 'valid').length

  const scanning = status === 'scanning'

  if (!selectedEvent && loadingEvents) {
    return (
      <div>
        <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
        <div className="alert info" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>Loading events…</div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!selectedEvent) {
    return (
      <div>
        <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
        <div className="alert info" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
          No events are assigned to you yet. Ask your organizer to assign you to an event.
        </div>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="row">
        <div>
          <h1>Scan tickets</h1>
          <p className="m">{selectedEvent.venue}</p>
          <label className="m" style={{ display: 'block', marginTop: 8 }}>
            Event gate
            <select
              className="in"
              style={{ marginTop: 4 }}
              value={selectedEvent.id}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setSelectedEventId(event.target.value)
                setResult(null)
                setHistory([])
              }}
            >
              {events.map((event) => (
                <option key={event.id} value={event.id}>
                  {event.title}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </label>
        </div>
        <div className="card stat" style={{ minWidth: 160 }}>
          <span className="m">Checked in this session</span>
          <b>{checkedIn}</b>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="split" style={{ marginTop: 14 }}>
        <div className="g">
          <div className="cam">
            <video ref={videoRef} playsInline muted className="cam-video" />
            {!scanning && (
              <div className="cam-hint">
                <div className="cam-frame" />
                <p>Point the camera at a ticket QR code to validate it.</p>
              </div>
            )}
          </div>

          <div className="row" style={{ gap: 10 }}>
            {scanning ? (
              <button type="button" className="btn o" onClick={stop}>
                Stop camera
              </button>
            ) : (
              <button
                type="button"
                className="btn"
                onClick={start}
                disabled={status === 'starting' || checking}
              >
                {status === 'starting' ? 'Starting camera…' : 'Start camera'}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>

          {error && <div className="alert error">{error}</div>}

          <form className="row" style={{ gap: 8 }} onSubmit={handleManualSubmit}>
            <input
              className="in"
              style={{ margin: 0 }}
              placeholder="Paste or scan the ticket QR token"
              value={manualCode}
              onChange={(event) => setManualCode(event.target.value)}
            />
            <button type="submit" className="btn" disabled={!manualCode.trim() || checking}>
              {checking ? 'Checking…' : 'Validate'}
            </button>
          </form>

          <div className="alert info">
            <>
              <b>Server-checked tickets</b>
              <p className="m">The backend records each scan, so a ticket is accepted only once.</p>
            </>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          {result ? (
            <ScanResult result={result} />
          ) : (
            <div className="alert info">Scan or enter a ticket to see the validation result.</div>
          )}

          <h4>Other results</h4>
          {history.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="m">No scans yet.</p>
          ) : (
            history.map((item, index) => (
              <ScanResult key={`${item.status}-${index}`} result={item} compact />
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

FILE: src/lib/backend.ts
================================================================================

// The dev server forwards /be/* to the backend (see vite.config.ts), so the browser
// never calls port 3000 directly and there is no CORS problem.
export const API = '/be'

const TOKEN_KEY = 'tm_token'
export const NO_SERVER = 'Cannot reach the server. Is the backend running?'

export function getToken(): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(TOKEN_KEY)
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function setToken(token: string | null): void {
  try {
    if (token) localStorage.setItem(TOKEN_KEY, token)
    else localStorage.removeItem(TOKEN_KEY)
  } catch {
    // storage unavailable: the user simply has to log in again
  }
}

function messageFrom(status: number, data: unknown): string {
  const message = data && typeof data === 'object' && 'message' in data ? (data as { message: unknown }).message : null
  if (status >= 500) return NO_SERVER
  if (Array.isArray(message)) return message.join('. ')
  if (typeof message === 'string') return message
  return `Request failed (${status})`
}

// JSON request to the backend. Adds the login token when there is one.
export async function api<T>(path: string, options: { method?: string; body?: unknown } = {}): Promise<T> {
  const headers: Record<string, string> = {}
  const token = getToken()
  if (token) headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${token}`
  if (options.body !== undefined) headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json'

  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(API + path, {
      method: options.method ?? 'GET',
      headers,
      body: options.body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(options.body),
    })
  } catch {
    throw new Error(NO_SERVER)
  }

  let data: unknown = null
  try {
    data = await response.json()
  } catch {
    data = null
  }
  if (!response.ok) {
    const error = new Error(messageFrom(response.status, data)) as Error & { status: number }
    error.status = response.status
    throw error
  }
  return data as T
}

import { api } from './backend'

export type AdminEventStatus = 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'

export function updateAdminEventStatus(eventId: string, status: AdminEventStatus): Promise<void> {
  return api<void>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    body: { status },
  })
}


================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/backendEvents.ts
================================================================================
import { API, api } from './backend'
import type { StoredEvent, TicketType } from '../types/event'

// Published events from the backend, converted to the shape the pages already use.
const KNOWN_CITIES = ['Lilongwe', 'Blantyre', 'Mzuzu', 'Zomba', 'Mangochi', 'Salima']
// Bundled pictures an organizer can pick instead of uploading their own.
export const DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES = [
  '/images/events/event-1.webp',
  '/images/events/event-2.jpg',
  '/images/events/event-3.webp',
]

// The image of an event: the organizer's upload, a chosen default, or (when nothing was
// chosen) one of the defaults picked from the event id.
export function resolveEventImage(imageUrl: string | null | undefined, eventId: string): string {
  if (imageUrl) {
    // uploads live on the backend, which the browser reaches through the /be proxy
    return imageUrl.startsWith('/uploads/') ? `${API}${imageUrl}` : imageUrl
  }
  let hash = 0
  for (const char of eventId) hash = (hash * 31 + char.charCodeAt(0)) >>> 0
  return DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES[hash % DEFAULT_EVENT_IMAGES.length]
}

interface BackendEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  description: string
  venue: string
  location: string
  startAt: string
  status?: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'
  category?: string | null
  imageUrl?: string | null
  organizerName?: string | null
}

interface BackendTicketType {
  ticketTypeId: string
  name: string
  price: number | string
  quantity: number
  remaining: number
}

interface Page {
  data: BackendEvent[]
  meta: { totalPages: number }
}

function toTicketType(type: BackendTicketType): TicketType {
  return {
    id: String(type.ticketTypeId),
    name: type.name,
    price: Number(type.price),
    capacity: type.quantity,
    sold: Math.max(0, type.quantity - type.remaining),
  }
}

function toStoredEvent(event: BackendEvent, ticketTypes: BackendTicketType[]): StoredEvent {
  const start = new Date(event.startAt)
  const location = event.location ?? ''
  return {
    id: String(event.id),
    title: event.title,
    description: event.description,
    category: event.category ?? '',
    city: KNOWN_CITIES.find((city) => location.toLowerCase().includes(city.toLowerCase())) ?? location,
    venue: event.venue,
    date: start.toLocaleDateString('en-CA'),
    time: start.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }),
    organizer: event.organizerName ?? '',
    image: resolveEventImage(event.imageUrl, String(event.id)),
    status: event.status === 'DRAFT' ? 'Draft' : event.status === 'CANCELLED' ? 'Cancelled' : 'Published',
    ticketTypes: ticketTypes.map(toTicketType),
  }
}

// All published events with their ticket types.
export async function fetchPublishedEvents(): Promise<StoredEvent[]> {
  const events: BackendEvent[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages = 1
  do {
    const result = await api<Page>(`/events?page=${page}&limit=50`)
    events.push(...result.data)
    totalPages = result.meta.totalPages
    page += 1
  } while (page <= totalPages)

  return Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => toStoredEvent(event, await api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${event.id}/ticket-types`))),
  )
}

export async function fetchBackendEvent(eventId: string): Promise<StoredEvent> {
  const event = await api<BackendEvent>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`)
  const ticketTypes = await api<BackendTicketType[]>(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/ticket-types`)
  return toStoredEvent(event, ticketTypes)
}




================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/backendOrganizer.ts
================================================================================
import { API, api, getToken } from './backend'
import { resolveEventImage } from './backendEvents'
import type { EventItem } from '../features/organizer/Data/Events'

// Organizer side of the backend: create, publish, cancel and list the organizer's own events.

export interface NewTicketType {
  name: string
  price: number
  quantity: number
  perOrderLimit?: number
}

export interface NewEventInput {
  name: string
  description: string
  venue: string
  location: string
  startAt: string // ISO date-time
  endAt: string // ISO date-time
  ticketTypes: NewTicketType[]
  status: 'Published' | 'Draft'
  imageUrl?: string // an uploaded image (/uploads/..), a bundled default, or nothing
}

interface BackendMyEvent {
  id: string
  title: string
  venue: string
  startAt: string
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED'
  publishedAt?: string | null
  imageUrl?: string | null
}

interface MyEventsPage {
  data: BackendMyEvent[]
  meta: { totalPages: number }
}

interface SalesSummary {
  totals: { sold: number }
}

const STATUS: Record<BackendMyEvent['status'], EventItem['status']> = {
  DRAFT: 'Draft',
  PUBLISHED: 'Published',
  CANCELLED: 'Cancelled',
}

// "10 May 2026 • 19:00", the same style the dashboard table already shows
export function formatEventDate(iso: string): string {
  const date = new Date(iso)
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return iso
  const day = date.toLocaleDateString('en-GB', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric' })
  const time = date.toLocaleTimeString('en-GB', { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
  return `${day} • ${time}`
}

// The backend wants the total capacity as well. It is simply the sum of the ticket types.
export function totalCapacity(ticketTypes: NewTicketType[]): number {
  return ticketTypes.reduce((sum, type) => sum + type.quantity, 0)
}

async function soldFor(eventId: string): Promise<number> {
  const summary = await api<SalesSummary>(`/events/${eventId}/sales-summary`)
  return summary.totals.sold
}

// Every event of the logged-in organizer, in all statuses, with tickets sold.
export async function listMyEvents(): Promise<EventItem[]> {
  const events: BackendMyEvent[] = []
  let page = 1
  let totalPages = 1
  do {
    const result = await api<MyEventsPage>(`/events/mine?page=${page}&limit=50`)
    events.push(...result.data)
    totalPages = result.meta.totalPages
    page += 1
  } while (page <= totalPages)

  return Promise.all(
    events.map(async (event) => ({
      id: String(event.id),
      name: event.title,
      date: formatEventDate(event.startAt),
      venue: event.venue,
      ticketsSold: event.status === 'PUBLISHED' ? await soldFor(event.id) : 0,
      status: STATUS[event.status] ?? 'Draft',
      ...(event.status === 'CANCELLED'
        ? { restoreStatus: event.publishedAt ? 'Published' as const : 'Draft' as const }
        : {}),
      image: resolveEventImage(event.imageUrl, String(event.id)),
    })),
  )
}

export async function setOrganizerEventStatus(
  eventId: string,
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED' | 'CANCELLED',
): Promise<void> {
  await api(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}`, {
    method: 'PATCH',
    body: { status },
  })
}

// Publishing makes the event public. Tickets only exist after "generate", so both run together.
// Generating again is safe: it only creates tickets that are still missing.
export async function publishEvent(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
  await api(`/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/publish`, { method: 'POST' })
  try {
    await api(`/tickets/events/${encodeURIComponent(eventId)}/generate`, { method: 'POST' })
  } catch (error) {
    const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'unknown error'
    throw new Error(`The event is published but its tickets could not be created (${reason}). Press Publish again to retry.`)
  }
}

export const MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024

// Sends a picture to the backend and returns the path to save as the event's imageUrl.
export async function uploadEventImage(file: File): Promise<string> {
  if (!/^image\/(jpeg|png|webp)$/.test(file.type)) throw new Error('Choose a JPG, PNG or WEBP image.')
  if (file.size > MAX_IMAGE_BYTES) throw new Error('The image must be 5 MB or smaller.')

  const form = new FormData()
  form.append('file', file)
  const token = getToken()

  let response: Response
  try {
    response = await fetch(`${API}/events/images`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: token ? { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` } : {},
      body: form,
    })
  } catch {
    throw new Error('Cannot reach the server. Is the backend running?')
  }

  let data: { imageUrl?: string; message?: string | string[] } | null = null
  try {
    data = await response.json()
  } catch {
    data = null
  }
  if (!response.ok || !data?.imageUrl) {
    const message = Array.isArray(data?.message) ? data.message.join('. ') : data?.message
    throw new Error(response.status >= 500 ? 'The image could not be uploaded. Try again.' : message || 'The image could not be uploaded.')
  }
  return data.imageUrl
}

export async function createOrganizerEvent(input: NewEventInput): Promise<{ id: string }> {
  const created = await api<{ id: string }>('/events', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: {
      title: input.name.trim(),
      description: input.description.trim(),
      venue: input.venue.trim(),
      location: input.location.trim(),
      startAt: input.startAt,
      endAt: input.endAt,
      ...(input.imageUrl ? { imageUrl: input.imageUrl } : {}),
      capacity: totalCapacity(input.ticketTypes),
      ticketTypes: input.ticketTypes.map((type) => ({
        name: type.name.trim(),
        price: type.price,
        currency: 'MWK',
        quantity: type.quantity,
        perOrderLimit: type.perOrderLimit ?? 10,
      })),
    },
  })
  if (input.status === 'Published') await publishEvent(created.id)
  return { id: String(created.id) }
}

export async function cancelOrganizerEvent(eventId: string): Promise<void> {
  await setOrganizerEventStatus(eventId, 'CANCELLED')
}

export async function restoreOrganizerEvent(
  eventId: string,
  status: 'DRAFT' | 'PUBLISHED',
): Promise<void> {
  await setOrganizerEventStatus(eventId, status)
}

FILE: src/lib/backendTickets.ts
================================================================================
import type { Event, TicketType } from '../types/event'
import type { TicketRecord, TicketValidationResult } from './tickets'
import { api } from './backend'

// Endpoints and response shapes from the ticket service. The backend owns ticket
// issuance, the secret QR token and the single-use check-in state.

export interface BackendPurchasedTicket {
  ticketNumber: string
  qrCodeDataUrl: string
  status: string
}

export interface BackendScanDetails {
  ticketNumber?: string
  ticketType?: string
  eventTitle?: string
  checkedInAt?: string | null
  customer?: { name: string; email: string } | null
}

export interface BackendValidationResponse {
  accepted: boolean
  reason: string | null
  ticketId: string | null
  eventId: string | null
  validatedAt: string | null
  details?: BackendScanDetails | null
}

// A backend QR code contains the secret UUID. Accept the raw value as well as
// older TICKET:<id>.<tag> strings so manually pasted codes still work.
export function extractQrToken(raw: string): string {
  const cleaned = raw.trim().replace(/^TICKET:/i, '')
  const dot = cleaned.lastIndexOf('.')
  return (dot === -1 ? cleaned : cleaned.slice(0, dot)).trim()
}

export function purchaseBackendTicket(
  eventId: string,
  ticketTypeId: string,
  txRef: string,
): Promise<BackendPurchasedTicket> {
  return api<BackendPurchasedTicket>('/tickets/purchase', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { eventId, ticketTypeId, txRef },
  })
}

export function validateBackendTicket(
  rawCode: string,
  eventId: string,
): Promise<BackendValidationResponse> {
  const qrToken = extractQrToken(rawCode)
  if (!qrToken) {
    return Promise.reject(new Error('Enter or scan a ticket QR code.'))
  }
  return api<BackendValidationResponse>('/tickets/validate', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: { qrToken, ...(eventId ? { eventId } : {}) },
  })
}

export function backendTicketToRecord(
  purchased: BackendPurchasedTicket,
  input: {
    userId: string
    event: Event
    ticketType: TicketType
    holder: string
  },
): TicketRecord {
  const { userId, event, ticketType, holder } = input
  return {
    id: purchased.ticketNumber,
    userId,
    eventId: event.id,
    eventName: event.title,
    date: event.date,
    time: event.time,
    venue: event.venue,
    city: event.city,
    ticketType: ticketType.name,
    price: ticketType.price,
    holder: holder.trim() || 'Guest',
    status: purchased.status === 'USED' ? 'used' : 'valid',
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    qrCodeDataUrl: purchased.qrCodeDataUrl,
  }
}

export function backendValidationToResult(
  response: BackendValidationResponse,
): TicketValidationResult {
  const details = response.details
  const ticketNumber = details?.ticketNumber ?? response.ticketId ?? 'This ticket'

  if (response.accepted) {
    const type = details?.ticketType ? ` · ${details.ticketType}` : ''
    return {
      status: 'valid',
      title: 'Valid',
      message: `${ticketNumber}${type} · admitted, attendance recorded.`,
    }
  }

  switch (response.reason) {
    case 'ALREADY_USED':
      return {
        status: 'used',
        title: 'Already used',
        message: `${ticketNumber} has already been admitted and cannot be accepted again.`,
      }
    case 'WRONG_EVENT':
      return {
        status: 'wrong_event',
        title: 'Wrong event',
        message: `${ticketNumber} is for ${details?.eventTitle ?? 'a different event'}, not this event.`,
      }
    case 'CANCELLED':
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Cancelled ticket',
        message: `${ticketNumber} has been cancelled and cannot be admitted.`,
      }
    case 'EVENT_CANCELLED':
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Event cancelled',
        message: 'This event has been cancelled, so the ticket cannot be admitted.',
      }
    case 'NOT_FOUND':
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Invalid ticket',
        message: 'This QR code is not a ticket issued for this platform.',
      }
    default:
      return {
        status: 'invalid',
        title: 'Invalid ticket',
        message: 'The ticket could not be accepted for this event.',
      }
  }
}

import { api } from './backend'
import type { AccountStatus, Role } from '../features/authentication/authStore'

// Admin user management and organizer staff, served by the backend.

type BackendRole = 'ATTENDEE' | 'ORGANIZER' | 'CHECK_IN_STAFF' | 'ADMIN'

const FROM_BACKEND: Record<BackendRole, Role> = {
  ATTENDEE: 'customer',
  ORGANIZER: 'organizer',
  CHECK_IN_STAFF: 'staff',
  ADMIN: 'admin',
}

const TO_BACKEND: Record<Role, BackendRole> = {
  customer: 'ATTENDEE',
  organizer: 'ORGANIZER',
  staff: 'CHECK_IN_STAFF',
  admin: 'ADMIN',
}

export interface AdminUser {
  id: string
  name: string
  email: string
  role: Role
  status: AccountStatus
  createdAt: string
}

  email: string

  isActive: boolean
  createdAt: string

    status: user.isActive ? 'active' : 'disabled',

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/paychangu.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from './backend'

export interface PendingPayment {
  txRef: string
  orderNumber: string
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  quantity: number
  holder: string
  userId: string
  createdAt: string
  status: 'pending' | 'completed'
  ticketIds?: string[]
}

export interface VerifiedPayment {
  status: 'success' | 'pending' | 'failed'
  tx_ref: string
}

interface CreateOrderInput {
  eventId: string
  ticketTypeId: string
  quantity: number
}

export const PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.pending-payments'

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return value !== null && typeof value === 'object' && !Array.isArray(value)
}

function findOrderNumber(value: unknown, depth = 0): string | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(value) || depth > 3) return undefined

  for (const key of ['orderNumber', 'order_number', 'number']) {
    const candidate = value[key]
    if ((typeof candidate === 'string' || typeof candidate === 'number') && String(candidate).trim()) {
      return String(candidate).trim()
    }
  }

  for (const key of ['data', 'order', 'result']) {
    const nested = findOrderNumber(value[key], depth + 1)
    if (nested) return nested
  }
  return undefined
}

function readPending(): PendingPayment[] {
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY)
  if (!raw) return []

  const parsed: unknown = JSON.parse(raw)
  if (!Array.isArray(parsed)) throw new Error('Saved payment references are invalid. Clear them and try again.')
  return parsed as PendingPayment[]
}

function writePending(items: PendingPayment[]): void {
  window.localStorage.setItem(PENDING_PAYMENTS_KEY, JSON.stringify(items.slice(0, 50)))
}

export function savePendingPayment(payment: PendingPayment): void {
  writePending([payment, ...readPending().filter((item) => item.txRef !== payment.txRef)])
}

export function getPendingPayment(txRef: string): PendingPayment | undefined {
  return readPending().find((item) => item.txRef === txRef)
}

export function latestPendingPayment(): PendingPayment | undefined {
  return readPending().find((item) => item.status === 'pending')
}

export function markPaymentCompleted(txRef: string, ticketIds: string[]): void {
  writePending(
    readPending().map((item) => (item.txRef === txRef ? { ...item, status: 'completed', ticketIds } : item)),
  )
}

export function addPendingTickets(txRef: string, ticketIds: string[]): void {
  if (ticketIds.length === 0) return
  writePending(
    readPending().map((item) =>
      item.txRef === txRef
        ? { ...item, ticketIds: Array.from(new Set([...(item.ticketIds ?? []), ...ticketIds])) }
        : item,
    ),
  )
}

export async function createOrder(input: CreateOrderInput): Promise<{ orderNumber: string }> {
  const result = await api<unknown>('/orders', {
    method: 'POST',
    body: {
      eventId: input.eventId,
      items: [{ ticketTypeId: input.ticketTypeId, quantity: input.quantity }],
    },
  })

  const orderNumber = findOrderNumber(result)
  if (!orderNumber) {
    throw new Error('The backend did not return an order number.')
  }
  return { orderNumber }
}

export async function initiatePayment(orderNumber: string): Promise<{ checkout_url: string; tx_ref: string }> {
  const result = await api<unknown>(
    `/payments/orders/${encodeURIComponent(orderNumber)}/checkout`,
    { method: 'POST' },
  )
  const dataObj = isRecord(result) && isRecord(result.data) ? result.data : result;
  const checkoutUrl = isRecord(dataObj) ? (dataObj.checkout_url || dataObj.checkoutUrl) : undefined;
  const txRef = isRecord(dataObj) ? (dataObj.tx_ref || dataObj.txRef) : undefined;

  if (typeof checkoutUrl !== 'string' || typeof txRef !== 'string' || !txRef.trim()) {
    throw new Error('The backend did not return a PayChangu checkout URL and transaction reference.')
  }
  let url: URL
  try {
    url = new URL(checkoutUrl)
  } catch {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid PayChangu checkout URL.')
  }
  if (url.protocol !== 'https:' && url.protocol !== 'http:') {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an unsupported PayChangu checkout URL.')
  }
  return { checkout_url: url.toString(), tx_ref: txRef }
}

export async function verifyPayment(txRef: string): Promise<VerifiedPayment> {
  const result = await api<unknown>(`/payments/${encodeURIComponent(txRef)}/verify`)
  // The backend reports the payment as paymentStatus; older responses used status.
  const rawStatus = isRecord(result)
    ? (typeof result.paymentStatus === 'string' ? result.paymentStatus : result.status)
    : undefined
  if (!isRecord(result) || typeof rawStatus !== 'string') {
    throw new Error('The backend returned an invalid payment verification response.')
  }

  const status = rawStatus.toLowerCase()
  const mappedStatus =
    ['success', 'successful', 'paid', 'completed'].includes(status)
      ? 'success'
      : ['pending', 'processing', 'initiated'].includes(status)
        ? 'pending'
        : 'failed'

  const responseTxRef =
    typeof result.tx_ref === 'string'
      ? result.tx_ref
      : typeof result.txRef === 'string'
        ? result.txRef
        : txRef
  return { status: mappedStatus, tx_ref: responseTxRef }
}

// Shapes used by the ticket screens. Tickets, their QR codes and check-in state all

export type { TicketStatus } from '../features/tickets/ticketTypes'

================================================================================
FILE: src/lib/verification.ts
================================================================================
import { api } from './backend';

export type KYCStatus = 'Unverified' | 'Pending' | 'Verified' | 'Rejected';

export interface OrganizerKYC {
  userId: string;
  businessName: string;
  phoneNumber: string;
  nationalId: string;
  status: KYCStatus;
  submittedAt?: string;
}

const KYC_KEY = 'tm.ticket-master.kyc';

function hasStorage(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && typeof window.localStorage !== 'undefined';
}

export function getKYC(userId: string): OrganizerKYC | null {
  if (!hasStorage()) return null;
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(KYC_KEY);
  if (!raw) return null;
  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as OrganizerKYC[];
    return parsed.find((k) => k.userId === userId) ?? null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function saveKYC(data: OrganizerKYC): void {
  if (!hasStorage()) return;
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(KYC_KEY);
  let parsed: OrganizerKYC[] = [];
  if (raw) {
    try { parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as OrganizerKYC[]; } catch {}
  }
  const existingIndex = parsed.findIndex((k) => k.userId === data.userId);
  if (existingIndex >= 0) {
    parsed[existingIndex] = data;
  } else {
    parsed.push(data);
  }
  window.localStorage.setItem(KYC_KEY, JSON.stringify(parsed));
}

export function listAllKYC(): OrganizerKYC[] {
  if (!hasStorage()) return [];
  const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(KYC_KEY);
  if (!raw) return [];
  try { return JSON.parse(raw) as OrganizerKYC[]; } catch { return []; }
}

interface BackendKyc {
  businessName: string;
  phoneNumber: string;
  nationalId: string;
  status: string;
  submittedAt?: string;
}

function toKyc(userId: string, remote: BackendKyc): OrganizerKYC {
  return {
    userId,
    businessName: remote.businessName,
    phoneNumber: remote.phoneNumber,
    nationalId: remote.nationalId,
    status: remote.status as KYCStatus,
    submittedAt: remote.submittedAt,
  };
}

// The backend keeps the organizer's details. This copies them into the browser, so the
// route guards (which read getKYC) work without waiting for the network.
export async function syncKyc(userId: string): Promise<OrganizerKYC | null> {
  const remote = await api<BackendKyc | null>('/organizers/me/kyc');
  if (!remote) {
    if (hasStorage()) {
      const kept = listAllKYC().filter((k) => k.userId !== userId);
      window.localStorage.setItem(KYC_KEY, JSON.stringify(kept));
    }
    return null;
  }
  const kyc = toKyc(userId, remote);
  saveKYC(kyc);
  return kyc;
}

export async function submitKyc(
  userId: string,
  input: { businessName: string; phoneNumber: string; nationalId: string },
): Promise<OrganizerKYC> {
  const remote = await api<BackendKyc>('/organizers/me/kyc', { method: 'PUT', body: input });
  const kyc = toKyc(userId, remote);
  saveKYC(kyc);
  return kyc;
}

import { createRootRoute, createRoute, createRouter } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { AppLayout } from '../components/AppLayout';
import { LoginPage, requireAuth } from '../features/authentication';
import { EventsPage, EventDetailsPage } from '../features/events';
import { CheckoutPage, PaymentReturnPage } from '../features/checkout';
import { MyTicketsPage, BookingHistoryPage, TicketDetailPage } from '../features/tickets';
import { ScannerPage } from '../features/validation';
import { OrganizerDashboardPage, CreateEventPage, StaffManagementPage } from '../features/organizer';
import { OrganizerKycPage } from '../features/organizer/pages/OrganizerKycPage';
import { AdminDashboardPage } from '../features/admin/pages/AdminDashboardPage';
import { HomePage } from '../features/landing'
import { validateEventSearch } from '../features/landing/filters';
import NotFoundPage from '../components/NotFoundPage';
import type { ComponentType } from 'react';

const rootRoute = createRootRoute({ component: AppLayout });

const page = <P extends string>(path: P, component: ComponentType<any>, beforeLoad?: ReturnType<typeof requireAuth>) => createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path, component: component as any, beforeLoad });

const indexRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/',
  component: HomePage,
  validateSearch: validateEventSearch,
});
const eventsRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/events',
  component: EventsPage,
  validateSearch: validateEventSearch,
});
const loginRoute = createRoute({
  getParentRoute: () => rootRoute,
  path: '/login',
  component: LoginPage,
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): { redirect?: string } => ({ redirect: typeof search.redirect === 'string' ? search.redirect : undefined }),
});
const notFoundRoute = createRoute({ getParentRoute: () => rootRoute, path: '*', component: NotFoundPage })

const routeTree = rootRoute.addChildren([
  indexRoute,
  loginRoute,
  eventsRoute,
  page('/events/$eventId', EventDetailsPage),
  page('/checkout/return', PaymentReturnPage, requireAuth('customer')), // PayChangu sends customers back here
  page('/checkout/$eventId', CheckoutPage, requireAuth('customer')),
  page('/tickets', MyTicketsPage, requireAuth('customer')),         // <-- Added base tickets route
  page('/tickets/$ticketId', TicketDetailPage, requireAuth('customer')), // <-- Kept specific route
  page('/bookings', BookingHistoryPage, requireAuth('customer')),
  page('/organizer/kyc', OrganizerKycPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/dashboard', OrganizerDashboardPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/events/new', CreateEventPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/events/$eventId/edit', CreateEventPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/organizer/staff', StaffManagementPage, requireAuth('organizer')),
  page('/admin', AdminDashboardPage, requireAuth('admin')),
  page('/scan', ScannerPage, requireAuth('staff')),
  notFoundRoute,
]);

export const router = createRouter({ routeTree });

declare module '@tanstack/react-router' {
  interface Register { router: typeof router; }
}

================================================================================
FILE: src/types/event.ts
================================================================================
export interface TicketType {
  id: string
  name: string
  price: number
  capacity: number
  sold: number
}

export interface Event {
  id: string
  title: string
  category: string
  city: string
  venue: string
  date: string // ISO date, e.g. 2026-11-14
  time: string // 24h, e.g. 18:00
  description: string
  organizer: string
  ownerId?: string // id of the organizer account that owns the event
  image: string
  ticketTypes: TicketType[]
}

// cheapest ticket type price, used for cards, filters and sorting
export function fromPrice(event: Event): number {
  if (event.ticketTypes.length === 0) return 0
  return Math.min(...event.ticketTypes.map((type) => type.price))
}

export function remainingSeats(type: TicketType): number {
  return Math.max(0, type.capacity - type.sold)
}

export function isSoldOut(event: Event): boolean {
  return (
    event.ticketTypes.length > 0 &&
    event.ticketTypes.every((type) => remainingSeats(type) === 0)
  )
}

// true when the event date is today or later
export function isUpcomingEvent(event: Event): boolean {
  const today = new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-CA') // YYYY-MM-DD
  return event.date >= today
}

export type EventStatus = 'Draft' | 'Pending' | 'Published' | 'Cancelled'

export interface StoredEvent extends Event {
  status: EventStatus
}

import { defineConfig } from 'vitest/config'
import { loadEnv } from 'vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'

// https://vite.dev/config/
export default defineConfig(({ mode }) => ({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  server: {
    allowedHosts: ['.ngrok-free.app', '.ngrok-free.dev', '.ngrok.app', '.ngrok.io', '.app.github.dev'],
    // Calls to /be/* go to the backend. Change the address with BACKEND_URL in .env.
    proxy: {
      '/be': {
        target: loadEnv(mode, process.cwd(), '').BACKEND_URL || 'http://localhost:3000',
        changeOrigin: true,
        rewrite: (path) => path.replace(/^\/be/, ''),
      },
    },
  },
  test: {
    environment: 'jsdom',
    include: ['src/**/*.test.{ts,tsx}'],
  },
}))
